# CareerOS App Shell and Workspace Screens (Sub-project 3a) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the CareerOS desktop app: core changes for privacy, approvals and incremental indexing; a centralized brand system; a shell-neutral React renderer with every 3a screen; a Node service layer that implements the renderer's bridge over `@careeros/core`; and, last, a hardened Electron shell with end-to-end tests.

**Architecture:** Five layers with one-way dependencies. `@careeros/core` (files are the truth) <- `@careeros/services` (Node, validates every call, owns grants, approvals, watcher) <- shell (`@careeros/app-electron`, the only package that imports `electron`). The renderer (`@careeros/app-ui`, React) talks only to a typed `CareerosBridge` from `@careeros/contract`, built over one `call(method, args)` transport, so a future shell swap touches only the bridge transport. `@careeros/brand` is the single source of truth for tokens (W3C DTCG JSON, built with Style Dictionary), fonts, icons, components and brand assets.

**Tech Stack:** Node.js 22.13+, TypeScript 5.9, React 19.3, Vite 7.3, Vitest 3.2 (projects: node + jsdom), React Testing Library 16, Style Dictionary 4.4, @fontsource (OFL fonts), lucide-react, react-markdown, Electron 44 (Node 24.21 inside), esbuild, Playwright 1.64 (`_electron` and visual snapshots).

**Spec:** `docs/specs/2026-10-08-app-shell-design.md` (approved). Owner decisions after the spec (2026-10-08), which this plan follows: (1) the design system is a centralized brand package `packages/brand` with DTCG tokens built by Style Dictionary v4; (2) the shell is Electron only (the Native SDK alternative is dropped); the renderer keeps a typed bridge seam with no Electron imports.

## Global Constraints

- Node `>=22.13` (root `engines`). No native npm modules in our packages. Electron 44.7.0 bundles Node 24.21.0.
- Electron hardening, all required: `contextIsolation: true`, `sandbox: true`, `nodeIntegration: false`, `webSecurity: true`, strict Content-Security-Policy (no remote scripts, styles, fonts or images), single-instance lock, no `remote` module, navigation and new windows blocked inside the app.
- External links open in the system browser only for `https:` URLs, after showing the real address.
- `careeros-file:` serves only files inside the open workspace (real-path checked) and never from locked folders unless an allow-once grant is active. No telemetry. No local web server at runtime.
- The renderer never touches the file system and never imports `electron`, `node:*` or `@careeros/core` at runtime. It never receives a secret.
- One workspace open at a time. Its path is remembered in the shell's user-data folder, never inside the workspace.
- Locked folders: `me/identity`, `me/employment` (locked by default). Allow-once lasts 15 minutes by default (configurable 1-240), is logged, and re-locks automatically.
- Only the app executes approved actions. The app never submits a job application form.
- Secrets: OS keychain via Electron `safeStorage`, stored in user data, never in the workspace. Linux without a secret service: warn and refuse to save.
- Themes: **Graphite** (dark, default) and **Paper** (light); first run follows the OS setting. Fonts: Instrument Serif (display), Geist (interface), Geist Mono (labels and figures), all SIL OFL, bundled from `@fontsource` packages, never from a CDN.
- Brand: every colour, font, size, radius, shadow and motion value comes from `packages/brand` tokens. No hex, `rgb(`/`rgba(`/`hsl(`, `font-family`, or px font size literal in renderer code (`packages/app-ui`). Enforced by `tests/brand-lint.test.ts`.
- Quality bar: keyboard navigable, labelled controls, WCAG AA contrast (4.5:1 for text) in both themes, 4/8 px spacing grid, `prefers-reduced-motion` respected.
- Fictional data only: Alex Example, Acme Corp, Example Labs, Acme Recruiting, `alex@example.com`, `0491 570 006`, 1 Example Street, Sampletown NSW 2000. Build emails and phones in tests by concatenation (`"alex" + "@" + "example.com"`). No real paths of any real user.
- Exact dependency pins for everything this plan adds (no `^`/`~`). Every new dependency gets a row in `docs/dependencies.md` with its licence; fonts also go in `NOTICE.md` and `licenses/`.
- All record writes go through `packages/core/src/store.ts`.
- Run `npm run typecheck && npm test` before every commit. Commits use the repo's configured git identity; never `--no-verify`.
- Out of scope (3a): mail (3b), in-app Claude chat and voice picker (3c), generating videos (5), installers, signing, auto-update, Windows ACLs and Windows CI (4).

## Review Focus

1. **Path tricks on file access and drops.** `careeros-file:` URLs and `files.readText` with `..`, `%2e%2e`, a symlink in `learning/` that points into `me/identity`, or `ME/Identity/...` on a case-insensitive disk; dropped folders, dotfiles, files inside hidden folders (`~/.ssh/...`) or over 25 MB. Expected: refused with a plain message, nothing copied. Pinned in Task 31.
2. **Grant expiry while the app is closed or the laptop sleeps.** Expected: `.claude/settings.json` never stays unlocked past the grant's `until`; opening the workspace re-locks everything and logs it. Pinned in Task 2 (core ignores expired grants) and Task 32 (open re-locks, timer re-locks).
3. **Double-clicking Approve, or two windows approving the same card.** Expected: the action runs once; the second call fails with "is approved; it can't become approved". Pinned in Task 8 (state machine), Task 28 (button disabled in flight) and Task 32.
4. **A job file broken or deleted while the app is open.** Expected: the watcher refresh does not crash, the list drops or skips the job, and the Job screen shows a readable error instead of a blank page. Pinned in Task 4, Task 20 and Task 30.
5. **Work-rights status changed in Settings while Jobs is open.** Expected: eligibility is recomputed at once (full index rebuild) and the "hidden" count and rows update on the next list. Pinned in Task 30.

describe("3a record kinds", () => {
  it("fills privacy defaults on a workspace record", () => {
    const r = check("workspace", { schema: 1 });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(r.value.privacy).toEqual({ locked: ["me/identity", "me/employment"], grants: [] });
  });
  it("rejects a lock on a folder that is not lockable", () => {
    expect(check("workspace", { schema: 1, privacy: { locked: ["me/resumes"] } }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("fills approval defaults and rejects unknown actions", () => {
    const ok = check("approval", { schema: 1, id: "20260113t090000-abc123", action: "status_change", title: "Move to Interview" });
    expect(ok.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(ok.value).toMatchObject({ status: "pending", steps: [], payload: {}, created_by: "cli", result_note: "" });
    expect(check("approval", { schema: 1, id: "x", action: "submit_form", title: "T" }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("requires folder and reason on an access request and bounds the minutes", () => {
    const ok = check("access_request", { schema: 1, id: "x", folder: "me/identity", why: "visa expiry" });
    expect(ok.value).toMatchObject({ status: "pending", minutes: 15, who: "claude", field: "" });
    expect(check("access_request", { schema: 1, id: "x", folder: "me/history", why: "w" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(check("access_request", { schema: 1, id: "x", folder: "me/identity", why: "w", minutes: 0 }).ok).toBe(false);

Create `packages/core/test/migration-0001.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { migrate, pendingMigrations } from "../src/migrate.js";
import { LATEST_DATA_VERSION } from "../src/migrations/index.js";
import { readRecord, writeFileAtomic } from "../src/store.js";
import { layout } from "../src/paths.js";
import { FIXED, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

function oldWorkspace(): string {
  const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
  initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
  // Rewind to the sub-project 1 shape: no privacy block, data_version 0, old settings file.
  writeFileAtomic(layout(root).workspaceFile, JSON.stringify({ schema: 1, data_version: 0, product_version: "0.1.0", created_at: FIXED.toISOString(), custom: {} }, null, 2) + "\n");
  writeFileAtomic(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), JSON.stringify({ permissions: { deny: ["Read(./me/identity/**)"] } }, null, 2) + "\n");
  return root;
}

describe("migration 1 (privacy)", () => {
  it("is the latest data version and new workspaces start there", () => {
    expect(LATEST_DATA_VERSION).toBe(1);
    const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
    initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
    expect(readRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile)).toMatchObject({ data_version: 1, privacy: { locked: ["me/identity", "me/employment"], grants: [] } });
  });
  it("adds the lock list to an old workspace, regenerates Claude settings and backs up both files", () => {
    const root = oldWorkspace();
    expect(pendingMigrations(root).map((m) => m.id)).toEqual([1]);
    const r = migrate(root, undefined, { now: FIXED });
    expect(r.applied).toEqual([1]);
    const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(layout(root).workspaceFile, "utf8"));
    expect(raw.privacy).toEqual({ locked: ["me/identity", "me/employment"], grants: [] });
    expect(raw.data_version).toBe(1);
    const s = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), "utf8"));
    expect(s.permissions.deny).toContain("Read(./me/employment/**)");
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(r.backup!, "workspace.json"))).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(r.backup!, ".claude", "settings.json"))).toBe(true);
    expect(migrate(root).applied).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

}

- [ ] **Step 5: Move Claude settings into `privacy.ts` and add the migration**

Create `packages/core/src/privacy.ts`:

```ts
import path from "node:path";
import { LOCKED_DIRS } from "./paths.js";
import { writeFileAtomic } from "./store.js";

export function claudeSettings(): { permissions: { deny: string[] } } {
  // Edit rules cover every file-writing tool; Write(...) is not a file-permission rule.
  const deny = LOCKED_DIRS.flatMap((d) => [`Read(./${d}/**)`, `Edit(./${d}/**)`])
    .concat(["Read(./settings/.env)", "Edit(./settings/.env)"]);
  return { permissions: { deny } };
}

export function writeClaudeSettings(root: string): void {
  writeFileAtomic(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), JSON.stringify(claudeSettings(), null, 2) + "\n");
}
```

```ts
import path from "node:path";
import { layout } from "../paths.js";
import { writeClaudeSettings } from "../privacy.js";
import { updateRecord } from "../store.js";
import type { Migration } from "./index.js";

// Adds workspace.privacy (lock list + grants, filled from schema defaults) and
// regenerates .claude/settings.json from it.
export const privacyMigration: Migration = {
  id: 1,
  name: "privacy lock list and Claude settings",
  files: (root) => [layout(root).workspaceFile, path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json")],
  run: (root) => {
    updateRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile, () => undefined);
    writeClaudeSettings(root);
  },
};
```

Replace `packages/core/src/migrations/index.ts`:

```ts
import { privacyMigration } from "./0001-privacy.js";

export interface Migration {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  /** Absolute paths of files this migration may change; they are backed up first. */
  files(root: string): string[];
  run(root: string): void;
}

// Append new migrations here with increasing ids. Never edit or remove a shipped one.
export const MIGRATIONS: Migration[] = [privacyMigration];

export const LATEST_DATA_VERSION = MIGRATIONS.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, x.id), 0);
```

In `packages/core/src/index.ts` add `export * from "./privacy.js";` after the `workspace.js` line.

import { addGrant, claudeSettings, effectiveLocked, expireGrants, readMeta, revokeAllGrants, revokeGrant, setLocked } from "../src/privacy.js";

    expect(d).toContain("Read(./settings/.env)");
    expect(d.some((r) => r.startsWith("Write("))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("still protects settings/.env when nothing is locked", () => {
    expect(claudeSettings([]).permissions.deny).toEqual(["Read(./settings/.env)", "Edit(./settings/.env)", "Bash(*settings/.env*)"]);
  });
});

describe("lock switches and allow-once grants", () => {
  it("unlocking a folder removes its rules from the workspace settings", () => {
    const root = ws();
    setLocked(root, "me/employment", false, { now: FIXED });
    expect(readMeta(root).privacy.locked).toEqual(["me/identity"]);
    expect(deny(root).some((r) => r.includes("me/employment"))).toBe(false);
    setLocked(root, "me/employment", true, { now: FIXED });

  });
  it("refuses folders that are not lockable", () => {
    expect(() => setLocked(ws(), "me/resumes", false)).toThrow(/not a lockable folder/);

    expect(deny(root)).toContain("Read(./me/identity/**)");

    expect(revokeGrant(root, "me/identity", at(1))).toBe(false);
    expect(revokeAllGrants(root, at(1)).map((g) => g.folder)).toEqual(["me/employment"]);

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `packages/core/src/privacy.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { type Clock, iso } from "./clock.js";
import { layout, LOCKED_DIRS } from "./paths.js";
import { readRecord, updateRecord, writeFileAtomic } from "./store.js";
import type { LockableDir, PrivacyGrant, WorkspaceMeta } from "./types.js";

/** Shell commands that read or copy files. Claude Code deny rules match the whole command text. */
export const BASH_READERS = ["cat", "head", "tail", "less", "more", "cp", "mv", "open", "xdg-open", "strings", "xxd", "od", "base64", "grep", "rg", "awk", "sed", "tar", "zip"] as const;

export function claudeSettings(locked: readonly string[] = LOCKED_DIRS): { permissions: { deny: string[] } } {
  const deny: string[] = [];
  for (const d of locked) {
    // Edit rules cover every file-writing tool; Write(...) is not a file-permission rule.
    deny.push(`Read(./${d}/**)`, `Edit(./${d}/**)`);
    for (const cmd of BASH_READERS) deny.push(`Bash(${cmd} *${d}*)`);
    // The catch-all also covers the CLI's --jd-file and Windows-style separators.
    deny.push(`Bash(*--jd-file*${d}*)`, `Bash(*${d}*)`, `Bash(*${d.replace(/\//g, "\\")}*)`);
  }
  deny.push("Read(./settings/.env)", "Edit(./settings/.env)", "Bash(*settings/.env*)");
  return { permissions: { deny } };
}

export function readMeta(root: string): WorkspaceMeta {
  return readRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile);
}

function assertLockable(folder: string): asserts folder is LockableDir {
  if (!(LOCKED_DIRS as readonly string[]).includes(folder)) throw new Error(`"${folder}" is not a lockable folder`);
}

/** Locked folders minus those with an unexpired allow-once grant. Expired grants never count. */
export function effectiveLocked(meta: WorkspaceMeta, c?: Clock): LockableDir[] {
  const now = iso(c);
  const open = new Set(meta.privacy.grants.filter((g) => g.until > now).map((g) => g.folder));
  return meta.privacy.locked.filter((d) => !open.has(d));
}

export function writeClaudeSettings(root: string, c?: Clock): void {
  const locked = effectiveLocked(readMeta(root), c);
  writeFileAtomic(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), JSON.stringify(claudeSettings(locked), null, 2) + "\n");
}

/** Regenerates Claude settings from the lock state and keeps every lockable folder owner-only. */
export function applyLocks(root: string, c?: Clock): void {
  writeClaudeSettings(root, c);
  for (const d of LOCKED_DIRS) {
    const dir = path.join(root, d);
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    fs.chmodSync(dir, 0o700);
  }
}

export function setLocked(root: string, folder: string, locked: boolean, c?: Clock): WorkspaceMeta {
  assertLockable(folder);
  const meta = updateRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile, (w) => {
    const set = new Set(w.privacy.locked);
    if (locked) set.add(folder); else set.delete(folder);
    w.privacy.locked = LOCKED_DIRS.filter((d) => set.has(d));
    // Locking (or unlocking) a folder ends any allow-once grant on it.
    w.privacy.grants = w.privacy.grants.filter((g) => g.folder !== folder);
  });
  applyLocks(root, c);
  return meta;
}

export function addGrant(root: string, folder: string, minutes: number, requestId: string, c?: Clock): PrivacyGrant {
  assertLockable(folder);
  if (!Number.isInteger(minutes) || minutes < 1 || minutes > 240) throw new Error("allow-once lasts 1 to 240 minutes");
  const now = c?.now ?? new Date();
  const grant: PrivacyGrant = { folder, until: new Date(now.getTime() + minutes * 60_000).toISOString(), request_id: requestId, granted_at: now.toISOString() };
  updateRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile, (w) => {
    if (!w.privacy.locked.includes(folder)) throw new Error(`"${folder}" is not locked, so it needs no grant`);
    w.privacy.grants = [...w.privacy.grants.filter((g) => g.folder !== folder), grant];
  });
  applyLocks(root, c);
  return grant;
}

function dropGrants(root: string, keep: (g: PrivacyGrant) => boolean, c?: Clock): PrivacyGrant[] {
  let removed: PrivacyGrant[] = [];
  updateRecord("workspace", layout(root).workspaceFile, (w) => {
    removed = w.privacy.grants.filter((g) => !keep(g));
    w.privacy.grants = w.privacy.grants.filter(keep);
  });
  applyLocks(root, c);
  return removed;
}

export function revokeGrant(root: string, folder: string, c?: Clock): boolean {
  assertLockable(folder);
  return dropGrants(root, (g) => g.folder !== folder, c).length > 0;
}

export function expireGrants(root: string, c?: Clock): PrivacyGrant[] {
  const now = iso(c);
  return dropGrants(root, (g) => g.until > now, c);
}

export function revokeAllGrants(root: string, c?: Clock): PrivacyGrant[] {
  return dropGrants(root, () => false, c);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Append `lockedFolderFor` to `packages/core/src/privacy.ts`**

```ts
function realOrResolved(p: string): string {
  try { return fs.realpathSync.native(p); } catch { return path.resolve(p); }
}

/**
 * Which locked area (if any) a file really lives in. Follows symlinks and, on
 * case-insensitive disks, normalises case, so "ME/Identity/x" or a link that
 * points into me/identity is still caught.
 */
export function lockedFolderFor(root: string, file: string, c?: Clock): string | null {
  const real = realOrResolved(file);
  const rootReal = realOrResolved(root);
  for (const d of effectiveLocked(readMeta(root), c)) {
    const dir = realOrResolved(path.join(rootReal, d));
    if (real === dir || real.startsWith(dir + path.sep)) return d;
  }
  const env = realOrResolved(path.join(rootReal, "settings", ".env"));
  return real === env ? "settings/.env" : null;
}
```

### Task 4: Incremental index refresh `refreshIndex(root, changedPaths)`

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/core/src/index-db.ts` (full rewrite below; `buildIndex` keeps its behaviour)
- Test: `packages/core/test/index-refresh.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `listJobs`, `readSearches`, `insideRoot`, `layout`, `readRecord`, `RecordError`, `isEligible`, `pinRank`.
- Produces:
  - `interface RefreshReport extends IndexReport { mode: "full" | "incremental" }`
  - `interface RefreshPlan { full: boolean; companies: Set<string>; companyJobs: Set<string>; jobs: Set<string>; searches: boolean }`
  - `planRefresh(relPaths: string[]): RefreshPlan` (paths relative to the root, `/`-separated)
  - `refreshIndex(root: string, changedPaths: string[]): RefreshReport` (absolute or relative paths)

Rules: no index file, `workspace.json`, anything under `settings/` (eligibility depends on the profile), the root, or the `companies` folder itself -> full rebuild. `companies/<slug>` -> that company and all its jobs. `companies/<slug>/company.json` -> company row and `company_name` on its jobs. `companies/<slug>/jobs` -> all jobs of that company. `companies/<slug>/jobs/<id>/...` -> that job. `knowledge` or `knowledge/search-log.jsonl` -> searches. Anything else (inbox, me, learning, .index, .careeros) -> nothing.

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { newJob, setStatus } from "../src/jobs.js";
import { buildIndex, openIndex, planRefresh, refreshIndex } from "../src/index-db.js";
import { updateRecord } from "../src/store.js";
import { layout } from "../src/paths.js";
import { appendSearch } from "../src/knowledge.js";
import { FIXED, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

function seeded() {
  const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
  initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
  updateRecord("profile", layout(root).profile, (p) => { p.work_rights.status = "needs_sponsorship"; });
  const a = newJob(root, { company: "Acme Corp", title: "AI Engineer", url: "https://jobs.example.com/1" }, { now: FIXED });
  const b = newJob(root, { company: "Example Labs", title: "Backend Engineer", url: "https://jobs.example.com/2" }, { now: FIXED });
  buildIndex(root);
  return { root, a, b, L: layout(root) };
}
const rows = (root: string, sql: string) => { const db = openIndex(root); const r = db.prepare(sql).all(); db.close(); return r; };

describe("planRefresh", () => {
  it("maps changed paths to the smallest refresh", () => {
    const p = planRefresh(["companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-15-ai-engineer/job.json", "companies/example-labs/company.json", "knowledge/search-log.jsonl", "inbox/cv.pdf"]);
    expect(p.full).toBe(false);
    expect([...p.jobs]).toEqual(["acme-corp/2026-01-15-ai-engineer"]);
    expect([...p.companies]).toEqual(["example-labs"]);
    expect(p.searches).toBe(true);
    expect(planRefresh(["settings/profile.json"]).full).toBe(true);
    expect(planRefresh(["workspace.json"]).full).toBe(true);
    expect([...planRefresh(["companies/new-co"]).companyJobs]).toEqual(["new-co"]);
  });
});

describe("refreshIndex", () => {

    fs.rmSync(L.indexDb);
    expect(refreshIndex(root, [L.profile]).mode).toBe("full");

  });

    expect(rows(root, "SELECT company_slug FROM jobs")).toEqual([{ company_slug: "acme-corp" }]);
    expect(rows(root, "SELECT count(*) AS n FROM events WHERE company_slug = 'example-labs'")).toEqual([{ n: 0 }]);
  });

    const { root, a, L } = seeded();
    updateRecord("company", L.companyFile(a.company_slug), (c) => { c.name = "Acme Corporation"; });
    refreshIndex(root, ["companies/acme-corp/company.json"]);
    expect(rows(root, "SELECT company_name FROM jobs WHERE company_slug = 'acme-corp'")).toEqual([{ company_name: "Acme Corporation" }]);
  });

    const outside = tmpDir("careeros-outside-");
    fs.cpSync(jobDir, outside, { recursive: true });
    fs.rmSync(jobDir, { recursive: true });
    fs.symlinkSync(outside, jobDir, "dir");
    const r = refreshIndex(root, [jobDir]);
    expect(r.skipped).toEqual([{ file: expect.stringContaining("example-labs"), problems: ["outside the workspace"] }]);
    expect(rows(root, "SELECT company_slug FROM jobs")).toEqual([{ company_slug: "acme-corp" }]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `packages/core/src/index-db.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { isEligible, pinRank } from "./eligibility.js";
import { listJobs, type ListJobsSkip } from "./jobs.js";
import { readSearches } from "./knowledge.js";
import { insideRoot, layout } from "./paths.js";
import { readRecord, RecordError } from "./store.js";
import type { Company, Job, WorkRightsStatus } from "./types.js";

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
// Loaded through require so test runners that don't know node:sqlite as a builtin still work.
const { DatabaseSync } = require("node:sqlite") as typeof import("node:sqlite");
type Db = InstanceType<typeof DatabaseSync>;
type JobRef = { company_slug: string; id: string; file: string };

export interface IndexReport { jobs: number; companies: number; events: number; searches: number; skipped: { file: string; problems: string[] }[] }
export interface RefreshReport extends IndexReport { mode: "full" | "incremental" }
export interface RefreshPlan { full: boolean; companies: Set<string>; companyJobs: Set<string>; jobs: Set<string>; searches: boolean }

const DDL = `
CREATE TABLE jobs (company_slug TEXT, id TEXT, company_name TEXT, client TEXT, title TEXT, source TEXT, source_site TEXT,
  url TEXT, posted TEXT, found_at TEXT, location TEXT, work_mode TEXT, job_type TEXT, salary_text TEXT, wr_label TEXT,
  fit REAL, sponsor REAL, career_match REAL, career_odds REAL, career_tier TEXT, status TEXT, eligible INTEGER,
  pinned INTEGER, last_activity TEXT, json TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (company_slug, id));
CREATE TABLE companies (slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, type TEXT, big_tech INTEGER, sponsor_status TEXT);
CREATE TABLE events (company_slug TEXT, job_id TEXT, at TEXT, event TEXT, note TEXT);
CREATE TABLE searches (at TEXT, site TEXT, query TEXT, location TEXT, results TEXT, kept TEXT, notes TEXT);
CREATE INDEX jobs_status ON jobs(status);
CREATE INDEX events_at ON events(at);
`;

export function openIndex(root: string): Db {
  return new DatabaseSync(layout(root).indexDb);
}

interface Ctx { root: string; report: IndexReport; status: WorkRightsStatus; db: Db }

function emptyReport(): IndexReport { return { jobs: 0, companies: 0, events: 0, searches: 0, skipped: [] }; }
const rel = (root: string, f: string) => path.relative(root, f);

function skipError(ctx: Ctx, e: unknown): void {
  if (e instanceof RecordError) ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, e.file), problems: e.problems });
  else throw e;
}

function profileStatus(root: string, report: IndexReport): WorkRightsStatus {
  try { return readRecord("profile", layout(root).profile).work_rights.status; }
  catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof RecordError) { report.skipped.push({ file: rel(root, e.file), problems: e.problems }); return "unknown"; }
    throw e;
  }
}

/** Inserts one company row. Returns its display name, or null if there is no usable record. */
function indexCompany(ctx: Ctx, slug: string): string | null {
  const L = layout(ctx.root);
  const companyDir = L.companyDir(slug);
  if (!fs.existsSync(companyDir)) return null;
  // Security: never read through a folder that resolves outside the workspace.
  if (!insideRoot(ctx.root, companyDir)) {
    ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, companyDir), problems: ["outside the workspace"] });
    return null;
  }
  const f = L.companyFile(slug);
  if (!fs.existsSync(f)) return null;
  let c: Company;
  try { c = readRecord("company", f); } catch (e) { skipError(ctx, e); return null; }
  ctx.db.prepare("INSERT INTO companies VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)").run(slug, c.name, c.type, c.big_tech ? 1 : 0, c.sponsor.status);
  ctx.report.companies++;
  return c.name;
}

function indexJob(ctx: Ctx, ref: JobRef, companyName: string): void {
  // Security: a job or company folder may be a symlink redirecting outside the workspace.
  if (!insideRoot(ctx.root, ref.file)) {
    ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, ref.file), problems: ["outside the workspace"] });
    return;
  }
  let j: Job;
  try { j = readRecord("job", ref.file); } catch (e) { skipError(ctx, e); return; }
  const last = j.timeline.reduce((m, ev) => (ev.at > m ? ev.at : m), j.found_at);
  ctx.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO jobs VALUES (${Array(25).fill("?").join(", ")})`).run(
    ref.company_slug, ref.id, companyName, j.client, j.title, j.source, j.source_site,
    j.url, j.posted, j.found_at, j.location, j.work_mode, j.job_type, j.salary.text, j.work_rights.label,
    j.scores.fit.value, j.scores.sponsor.value, j.scores.career.match, j.scores.career.odds, j.scores.career.tier,
    j.status, isEligible(ctx.status, j.work_rights.label) ? 1 : 0, pinRank(j.work_rights.label), last, JSON.stringify(j),
  );
  ctx.report.jobs++;
  const insEvent = ctx.db.prepare("INSERT INTO events VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
  for (const ev of j.timeline) { insEvent.run(ref.company_slug, ref.id, ev.at, ev.event, ev.note ?? ""); ctx.report.events++; }
}

function indexSearches(ctx: Ctx): void {
  const insSearch = ctx.db.prepare("INSERT INTO searches VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
  const { entries, badLines } = readSearches(ctx.root);
  for (const s of entries) { insSearch.run(s.at, s.site, s.query, s.location, s.results, s.kept, s.notes); ctx.report.searches++; }
  if (badLines.length) ctx.report.skipped.push({ file: rel(ctx.root, layout(ctx.root).searchLog), problems: badLines.map((n) => `line ${n} is not valid JSON`) });
}

export function buildIndex(root: string): IndexReport {
  const L = layout(root);
  const report = emptyReport();
  const status = profileStatus(root, report);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(L.indexDb), { recursive: true });
  const tmp = `${L.indexDb}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
  fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true });
  const db = new DatabaseSync(tmp);
  const ctx: Ctx = { root, report, status, db };
  try {
    db.exec(DDL);
    db.exec("BEGIN");
    const names = new Map<string, string>();
    if (fs.existsSync(L.companiesDir)) {
      for (const slug of fs.readdirSync(L.companiesDir).sort()) {
        const name = indexCompany(ctx, slug);
        if (name !== null) names.set(slug, name);
      }
    }
    const jobSkips: ListJobsSkip[] = [];
    for (const ref of listJobs(root, jobSkips)) indexJob(ctx, ref, names.get(ref.company_slug) ?? ref.company_slug);
    for (const s of jobSkips) report.skipped.push({ file: rel(root, s.file), problems: [s.problem] });
    indexSearches(ctx);
    db.exec("COMMIT");
  } catch (e) {
    db.close();
    fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true });
    throw e;
  }
  db.close();
  fs.renameSync(tmp, L.indexDb);
  return report;
}

export function planRefresh(relPaths: string[]): RefreshPlan {
  const plan: RefreshPlan = { full: false, companies: new Set(), companyJobs: new Set(), jobs: new Set(), searches: false };
  for (const raw of relPaths) {
    const r = raw.replace(/^\.\//, "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
    if (r.startsWith("..")) continue;
    const parts = r.split("/");
    if (r === "" || r === "." || r === "workspace.json" || parts[0] === "settings" || r === "companies") { plan.full = true; continue; }
    if (parts[0] === "knowledge" && (parts.length === 1 || parts[1] === "search-log.jsonl")) { plan.searches = true; continue; }
    if (parts[0] !== "companies" || !parts[1]) continue;
    const slug = parts[1];
    if (parts.length === 2) { plan.companies.add(slug); plan.companyJobs.add(slug); }
    else if (parts[2] === "company.json") plan.companies.add(slug);
    else if (parts[2] === "jobs" && parts.length === 3) plan.companyJobs.add(slug);
    else if (parts[2] === "jobs" && parts[3]) plan.jobs.add(`${slug}/${parts[3]}`);
  }
  return plan;
}

export function refreshIndex(root: string, changedPaths: string[]): RefreshReport {
  const L = layout(root);
  const full = (): RefreshReport => ({ ...buildIndex(root), mode: "full" });
  if (!fs.existsSync(L.indexDb)) return full();
  const rels = changedPaths.map((p) => (path.isAbsolute(p) ? path.relative(root, p) : p).split(path.sep).join("/"));
  const plan = planRefresh(rels);
  if (plan.full) return full();
  const report = emptyReport();
  if (!plan.companies.size && !plan.companyJobs.size && !plan.jobs.size && !plan.searches) return { ...report, mode: "incremental" };

  const db = new DatabaseSync(L.indexDb);
  try {
    const t = db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name IN ('jobs', 'companies', 'events', 'searches')").get() as { n: number };
    if (t.n !== 4) { db.close(); return full(); }
    const ctx: Ctx = { root, report, status: profileStatus(root, report), db };
    db.exec("BEGIN");
    for (const slug of plan.companies) {
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM companies WHERE slug = ?").run(slug);
      const name = indexCompany(ctx, slug);
      db.prepare("UPDATE jobs SET company_name = ? WHERE company_slug = ?").run(name ?? slug, slug);
    }
    const names = new Map((db.prepare("SELECT slug, name FROM companies").all() as { slug: string; name: string }[]).map((r) => [r.slug, r.name]));
    const jobSkips: ListJobsSkip[] = [];
    const refs = listJobs(root, jobSkips);
    const touched = new Set([...plan.companyJobs, ...[...plan.jobs].map((k) => k.split("/")[0]!)]);
    for (const slug of plan.companyJobs) {
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE company_slug = ?").run(slug);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM events WHERE company_slug = ?").run(slug);
      for (const ref of refs.filter((x) => x.company_slug === slug)) indexJob(ctx, ref, names.get(slug) ?? slug);
    }
    for (const key of plan.jobs) {
      const [slug = "", id = ""] = key.split("/");
      if (plan.companyJobs.has(slug)) continue;
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE company_slug = ? AND id = ?").run(slug, id);
      db.prepare("DELETE FROM events WHERE company_slug = ? AND job_id = ?").run(slug, id);
      const ref = refs.find((x) => x.company_slug === slug && x.id === id);
      if (ref) indexJob(ctx, ref, names.get(slug) ?? slug);
    }
    for (const s of jobSkips) {
      const r = rel(root, s.file).split(path.sep);
      if (r[0] === "companies" && touched.has(r[1] ?? "")) report.skipped.push({ file: rel(root, s.file), problems: [s.problem] });
    }
    if (plan.searches) { db.exec("DELETE FROM searches"); indexSearches(ctx); }
    db.exec("COMMIT");
  } catch (e) {
    try { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); } catch { /* no open transaction */ }
    db.close();
    throw e;
  }
  db.close();
  return { ...report, mode: "incremental" };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rewrite `packages/core/src/fields.ts`**

```ts
import { loadSchema } from "./schema.js";
import type { Kind } from "./types.js";

export interface FieldDef {
  path: string; label: string; type: string; options: string[]; optionLabels: Record<string, string>;
  help: string; pattern: string; fields: FieldDef[];
}

type Node = {
  type?: string | string[]; enum?: string[]; pattern?: string; properties?: Record<string, Node>; items?: Node;
  "x-ui"?: { label?: string; help?: string; options?: Record<string, string> };
};

function typeOf(n: Node): string {
  if (n.enum) return "enum";
  const t = Array.isArray(n.type) ? n.type.join("|") : n.type;
  if (t === "array") {
    if (n.items?.enum) return "enum[]";
    if (n.items?.properties) return "object[]";
    if (typeof n.items?.type === "string") return `${n.items.type}[]`;
    return "array";
  }
  return t ?? "unknown";
}

function walk(node: Node, prefix: string, out: FieldDef[]): FieldDef[] {
  for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(node.properties ?? {})) {
    const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key;
    const ui = child["x-ui"];
    if (ui?.label) {
      const type = typeOf(child);
      out.push({
        path, label: ui.label, type,
        options: child.enum ?? child.items?.enum ?? [],
        optionLabels: ui.options ?? {},
        help: ui.help ?? "",
        pattern: child.pattern ?? "",
        fields: type === "object[]" && child.items ? walk(child.items, "", []) : [],
      });
    }
    if (child.properties) walk(child, path, out);
  }
  return out;
}

export function fieldsFor(kind: Kind): FieldDef[] {
  return walk(loadSchema(kind) as Node, "", []);
}
```

### Task 6: Windows-safe permission calls

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/perm.ts`, `packages/core/test/perm.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/store.ts` (`writeFileAtomic`), `packages/core/src/privacy.ts` (`applyLocks`), `packages/core/src/migrate.ts` (backup copy), `packages/core/src/legacy.ts` (`copyPreserveMode`), `packages/core/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `safeChmod(p: string, mode: number, platform?: NodeJS.Platform): boolean` returns `false` and does nothing on `win32` (ACLs come in sub-project 4), otherwise chmods and returns `true`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

import fs from "node:fs";

import { safeChmod } from "../src/perm.js";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { writeFileAtomic } from "../src/store.js";

describe("safeChmod", () => {
  it("is a no-op on Windows", () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(fs, "chmodSync");
    expect(safeChmod(path.join(tmpDir(), "x"), 0o600, "win32")).toBe(false);
    expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

  it("chmods elsewhere", () => {

    const f = path.join(tmpDir(), "x");

    const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
    initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
    writeFileAtomic(path.join(root, "settings", ".env"), "# fictional\n", 0o600);
    expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `packages/core/src/perm.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";

/** POSIX permission bits only. Windows ignores them; folder ACLs arrive in sub-project 4. */
export function safeChmod(p: string, mode: number, platform: NodeJS.Platform = process.platform): boolean {
  if (platform === "win32") return false;
  fs.chmodSync(p, mode);
  return true;
}
```

Add `export * from "./perm.js";` to `packages/core/src/index.ts`.

      const existingMode = fs.statSync(file).mode & 0o777;
      safeChmod(tmp, existingMode);
    } else if (mode !== undefined) {

      safeChmod(tmp, mode);
    }

### Task 7: Inbox suggestion rules `suggestInboxTarget(root, name)`

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/core/src/inbox.ts` (append)
- Test: `packages/core/test/inbox-suggest.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `listJobs`, `readRecord`, `layout`, `assertSegment`, `InboxTarget`, `JobSubdir`.
- Produces:
  - `interface InboxSuggestion { target: InboxTarget | null; reason: string; confidence: "high" | "medium" | "low" }`
  - `suggestInboxTarget(root: string, name: string): InboxSuggestion`

Rule order (first match wins): (1) identity, employment and education words -> `me/...` (privacy first: a passport never goes into a job folder by default); (2) a company name or slug in the file name -> that company's best job (open jobs before Rejected/Skipped, then most title words in common, then newest) with a sub-folder from the name (`emails`, `interview`, `application`, or the job root); (3) resume or CV -> `me/resumes`; (4) portfolio or project -> `me/projects`; (5) for `.md`, `.txt` and `.eml` files up to 256 KB, a company name inside the text -> that job; (6) nothing.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `packages/core/test/inbox-suggest.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { initWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";
import { suggestInboxTarget } from "../src/inbox.js";
import { newJob, setStatus } from "../src/jobs.js";
import { layout } from "../src/paths.js";
import { FIXED, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

function ws() {
  const root = path.join(tmpDir(), "ws");
  initWorkspace(root, { now: FIXED });
  const acme = newJob(root, { company: "Acme Corp", title: "AI Engineer" }, { now: FIXED });
  const old = newJob(root, { company: "Acme Corp", title: "Data Analyst" }, { now: new Date("2025-06-01T00:00:00.000Z") });
  setStatus(root, old.company_slug, old.id, "Rejected");
  const labs = newJob(root, { company: "Example Labs", title: "Backend Engineer (LLM)" }, { now: FIXED });
  return { root, acme, labs };
}
const drop = (root: string, name: string, body = "x") => fs.writeFileSync(path.join(layout(root).inbox, name), body);

describe("suggestInboxTarget", () => {
  it("sends identity, employment and education papers to me/ first", () => {
    const { root } = ws();
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf")).toMatchObject({ target: { kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, confidence: "high" });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "passport-scan.jpg").target).toEqual({ kind: "me", dir: "identity" });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "acme-corp-payslip-march.pdf").target).toEqual({ kind: "me", dir: "employment" });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "Academic Transcript.pdf").target).toEqual({ kind: "me", dir: "education" });
  });
  it("matches a company in the name to its open job and picks a sub-folder", () => {
    const { root, acme, labs } = ws();
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "acme-corp-cover-letter.docx")).toMatchObject({
      target: { kind: "job", company_slug: "acme-corp", id: acme.id, subdir: "application" }, confidence: "high",
    });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "Example Labs interview questions.md").target).toEqual({ kind: "job", company_slug: "example-labs", id: labs.id, subdir: "interview" });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "example_labs_offer.eml").target).toMatchObject({ subdir: "emails" });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "acme corp notes.pdf")).toMatchObject({ target: { kind: "job", subdir: "" }, confidence: "medium" });
  });
  it("files a plain resume into me/resumes and a portfolio into me/projects", () => {
    const { root } = ws();
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "Alex_Example_CV_2026.pdf")).toMatchObject({ target: { kind: "me", dir: "resumes" }, confidence: "medium" });
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "portfolio.pdf")).toMatchObject({ target: { kind: "me", dir: "projects" }, confidence: "low" });
  });
  it("reads small text files for a company name", () => {
    const { root, labs } = ws();
    drop(root, "notes.md", "Call with the Example Labs hiring manager on Thursday.");
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "notes.md")).toMatchObject({ target: { kind: "job", company_slug: "example-labs", id: labs.id }, confidence: "medium" });
  });
  it("says so when nothing matches, and rejects unsafe names", () => {
    const { root } = ws();
    drop(root, "IMG_0001.heic");
    expect(suggestInboxTarget(root, "IMG_0001.heic")).toEqual({ target: null, reason: "No rule matched. Choose a folder.", confidence: "low" });
    expect(() => suggestInboxTarget(root, "../secret.txt")).toThrow(/not an inbox file name/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Append to `packages/core/src/inbox.ts`**

Add `listJobs` to the `./jobs.js` import, `readRecord` to the `./store.js` import, and `import type { Job } from "./types.js";`. Then append:

```ts
export interface InboxSuggestion { target: InboxTarget | null; reason: string; confidence: "high" | "medium" | "low" }

const ME_RULES: { re: RegExp; dir: MeDir; sub?: string; why: string }[] = [
  { re: /\b(visa|vevo|bridging|grant notice)\b/, dir: "identity", sub: "visa", why: "The name mentions a visa." },
  { re: /\b(passport|birth certificate|drivers? licen[cs]e|licen[cs]e|medicare|id card|identity)\b/, dir: "identity", why: "The name looks like an identity document." },
  { re: /\b(payslip|pay slip|payg|separation|employment|employer reference|reference letter|service letter)\b/, dir: "employment", why: "The name looks like an employment document." },
  { re: /\b(transcript|degree|diploma|certificate|academic|graduation|qualification)\b/, dir: "education", why: "The name looks like an education document." },
];

interface Candidate { company_slug: string; id: string; company: string; title: string; status: string; found_at: string }

const words = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ").trim();

function normaliseName(name: string): string {
  return words(name.replace(/\.[A-Za-z0-9]{1,5}$/, ""));
}

function candidates(root: string): Candidate[] {
  const L = layout(root);
  const companyNames = new Map<string, string>();
  const out: Candidate[] = [];
  for (const ref of listJobs(root)) {
    let j: Job;
    try { j = readRecord("job", ref.file); } catch { continue; }
    if (!companyNames.has(ref.company_slug)) {
      let n = ref.company_slug;
      try { n = readRecord("company", L.companyFile(ref.company_slug)).name; } catch { /* use the slug */ }
      companyNames.set(ref.company_slug, n);
    }
    out.push({ company_slug: ref.company_slug, id: ref.id, company: companyNames.get(ref.company_slug)!, title: j.title, status: j.status, found_at: j.found_at });
  }
  return out;
}

function matchCompany(text: string, all: Candidate[]): Candidate[] {
  const t = ` ${text} `;
  return all.filter((c) => {
    const phrase = words(c.company);
    const slug = c.company_slug.replace(/-/g, " ");
    return (phrase.length >= 3 && t.includes(` ${phrase} `)) || (slug.length >= 3 && t.includes(` ${slug} `));
  });
}

function bestJob(found: Candidate[], text: string): Candidate {
  const tw = new Set(text.split(" "));
  const score = (c: Candidate) => words(c.title).split(" ").filter((w) => w.length > 2 && tw.has(w)).length;
  const closed = (c: Candidate) => (c.status === "Rejected" || c.status === "Skipped" ? 1 : 0);
  return [...found].sort((a, b) => closed(a) - closed(b) || score(b) - score(a) || b.found_at.localeCompare(a.found_at))[0]!;
}

function jobSubdir(text: string, ext: string): JobSubdir {
  if (ext === ".eml" || /\b(email|e mail|message|offer)\b/.test(text)) return "emails";
  if (/\b(interview|prep|questions)\b/.test(text)) return "interview";
  if (/\b(cover|letter|answers?|application|resume|cv|tailored)\b/.test(text)) return "application";
  return "";
}

export function suggestInboxTarget(root: string, name: string): InboxSuggestion {
  try { assertSegment(name, "inbox file name"); } catch { throw new Error(`"${name}" is not an inbox file name`); }
  const text = normaliseName(name);
  const ext = path.extname(name).toLowerCase();

  for (const r of ME_RULES) {
    if (r.re.test(text)) return { target: r.sub ? { kind: "me", dir: r.dir, sub: r.sub } : { kind: "me", dir: r.dir }, reason: r.why, confidence: "high" };
  }
  const all = candidates(root);
  const byName = matchCompany(text, all);
  if (byName.length) {
    const job = bestJob(byName, text);
    const subdir = jobSubdir(text, ext);
    const sure = subdir !== "" || /\b(jd|job description)\b/.test(text);
    return { target: { kind: "job", company_slug: job.company_slug, id: job.id, subdir }, reason: `The name mentions ${job.company}.`, confidence: sure ? "high" : "medium" };
  }
  if (/\b(resume|cv|curriculum vitae)\b/.test(text)) return { target: { kind: "me", dir: "resumes" }, reason: "The name looks like a resume.", confidence: "medium" };
  if (/\b(portfolio|project|case study)\b/.test(text)) return { target: { kind: "me", dir: "projects" }, reason: "The name looks like a project.", confidence: "low" };

  if ([".md", ".txt", ".eml"].includes(ext)) {
    const file = path.join(layout(root).inbox, name);
    try {
      const st = fs.lstatSync(file);
      if (st.isFile() && st.size <= 256 * 1024) {
        const found = matchCompany(words(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")), all);
        if (found.length) {
          const job = bestJob(found, text);
          return { target: { kind: "job", company_slug: job.company_slug, id: job.id, subdir: jobSubdir(text, ext) }, reason: `The file mentions ${job.company}.`, confidence: "medium" };
        }
      }
    } catch { /* not in the inbox: name rules only */ }
  }
  return { target: null, reason: "No rule matched. Choose a folder.", confidence: "low" };
}
```

- Produces:

  - `listAccessRequests(root, opts?: { status?: RequestStatus; skipped?: QueueSkip[] }): AccessRequest[]`
  - `readAccessRequest(root, id): AccessRequest`
  - `decideAccessRequest(root, id, status: RequestStatus, opts?: { minutes?: number }, c?): AccessRequest` (pending -> allowed|denied|expired; allowed -> expired)
  - `interface ActivityEntry { at: string; event: string; detail: string }`; `logActivity(root, event, detail?, c?): ActivityEntry`; `readActivity(root, limit?: number): ActivityEntry[]` (newest first)
  - CLI: `approval propose --action status_change --job SLUG/ID --status S [--note N] [--title T]`, `approval list [--status S]`, `request access --folder DIR --why W [--field F] [--minutes N]`, `request list`.

  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `packages/core/src/queue.ts`**

```ts
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { type Clock, iso } from "./clock.js";
import { jobFilePath } from "./jobs.js";
import { assertSegment, insideRoot, LOCKED_DIRS } from "./paths.js";
import { defaults } from "./schema.js";
import { appendLineSafe, readRecord, RecordError, updateRecord, writeRecord } from "./store.js";
import {
  type AccessRequest, type Approval, type ApprovalAction, APPROVAL_ACTIONS, type ApprovalStatus,
  JOB_STATUSES, type RequestStatus,
} from "./types.js";

export type QueueKind = "approvals" | "requests";
export interface QueueSkip { file: string; problems: string[] }
export interface ProposeInput { action: ApprovalAction; title: string; summary?: string; steps?: string[]; payload?: Record<string, unknown>; created_by?: "app" | "claude" | "cli" }
export interface ActivityEntry { at: string; event: string; detail: string }

const ID = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;

function realFolder(root: string, dir: string): void {
  let st: fs.Stats | undefined;
  try { st = fs.lstatSync(dir); } catch { st = undefined; }
  if (st && (st.isSymbolicLink() || !st.isDirectory())) throw new Error(`refusing to use ${path.relative(root, dir)}: not a real folder`);
}

export function careerosDir(root: string): string {
  const dir = path.join(root, ".careeros");
  realFolder(root, dir);
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  return dir;
}

export function queueDir(root: string, kind: QueueKind): string {
  const dir = path.join(careerosDir(root), kind);
  realFolder(root, dir);
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  if (!insideRoot(root, dir)) throw new Error("refusing to use a queue outside the workspace");
  return dir;
}

function queueFile(root: string, kind: QueueKind, id: string): string {
  assertSegment(id, "queue id");
  if (!ID.test(id)) throw new Error(`bad queue id "${id}"`);
  return path.join(queueDir(root, kind), `${id}.json`);
}

export function newQueueId(seed: string, c?: Clock): string {
  const stamp = iso(c).replace(/[-:]/g, "").replace(/\.\d+Z$/, "").toLowerCase();
  return `${stamp}-${crypto.createHash("sha256").update(seed).digest("hex").slice(0, 6)}`;
}

function freshId(root: string, kind: QueueKind, seed: string, c?: Clock): string {
  const base = newQueueId(seed, c);
  const dir = queueDir(root, kind);
  let id = base;
  for (let n = 2; fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, `${id}.json`)); n++) id = `${base}-${n}`;
  return id;
}

function listKind<K extends "approval" | "access_request">(root: string, kind: QueueKind, rk: K, skipped?: QueueSkip[]) {
  const dir = queueDir(root, kind);
  const out = [];
  for (const n of fs.readdirSync(dir).sort()) {
    if (!n.endsWith(".json")) continue;
    const f = path.join(dir, n);
    if (!fs.lstatSync(f).isFile()) { skipped?.push({ file: path.relative(root, f), problems: ["not a regular file"] }); continue; }
    try { out.push(readRecord(rk, f)); }
    catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof RecordError) skipped?.push({ file: path.relative(root, e.file), problems: e.problems });
      else throw e;
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
}

function existing(root: string, kind: QueueKind, id: string): string {
  const f = queueFile(root, kind, id);
  if (!fs.existsSync(f)) throw new Error(`no ${kind === "approvals" ? "approval" : "request"} ${id}`);
  return f;
}

export function proposeApproval(root: string, input: ProposeInput, c?: Clock): Approval {
  if (!(APPROVAL_ACTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(input.action)) throw new Error(`unknown action "${input.action}"`);
  const p = input.payload ?? {};
  if (input.action === "status_change" || input.action === "apply_handoff") jobFilePath(root, String(p.company_slug ?? ""), String(p.id ?? ""));
  if (input.action === "status_change" && !(JOB_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(String(p.status))) throw new Error(`unknown status "${String(p.status)}"`);
  if (input.action === "apply_handoff" && p.url && !String(p.url).startsWith("https://")) throw new Error("hand-off links must start with https://");
  const id = freshId(root, "approvals", JSON.stringify(input), c);
  return writeRecord("approval", queueFile(root, "approvals", id), defaults("approval", {
    id, action: input.action, title: input.title, summary: input.summary ?? "", steps: input.steps ?? [], payload: p,
    created_at: iso(c), created_by: input.created_by ?? "cli",
  }));
}

export function listApprovals(root: string, opts: { status?: ApprovalStatus; skipped?: QueueSkip[] } = {}): Approval[] {
  return listKind(root, "approvals", "approval", opts.skipped).filter((a) => !opts.status || a.status === opts.status);
}

export function readApproval(root: string, id: string): Approval {
  return readRecord("approval", existing(root, "approvals", id));
}

const NEXT: Record<ApprovalStatus, ApprovalStatus[]> = { pending: ["approved", "declined"], approved: ["done", "failed"], declined: [], done: [], failed: [] };

export function setApprovalStatus(root: string, id: string, status: ApprovalStatus, note = "", c?: Clock): Approval {
  return updateRecord("approval", existing(root, "approvals", id), (a) => {
    if (!NEXT[a.status].includes(status)) throw new Error(`approval ${id} is ${a.status}; it can't become ${status}`);
    a.status = status;
    if (status === "approved" || status === "declined") a.decided_at = iso(c);
    if (note) a.result_note = note;
  });
}

export function requestAccess(root: string, input: { folder: string; why: string; field?: string; who?: string; minutes?: number }, c?: Clock): AccessRequest {
  if (!(LOCKED_DIRS as readonly string[]).includes(input.folder)) throw new Error(`"${input.folder}" is not a lockable folder`);
  const id = freshId(root, "requests", JSON.stringify(input), c);
  return writeRecord("access_request", queueFile(root, "requests", id), defaults("access_request", {
    id, folder: input.folder, why: input.why, field: input.field ?? "", who: input.who ?? "claude", minutes: input.minutes ?? 15, created_at: iso(c),
  }));
}

export function listAccessRequests(root: string, opts: { status?: RequestStatus; skipped?: QueueSkip[] } = {}): AccessRequest[] {
  return listKind(root, "requests", "access_request", opts.skipped).filter((r) => !opts.status || r.status === opts.status);
}

export function readAccessRequest(root: string, id: string): AccessRequest {
  return readRecord("access_request", existing(root, "requests", id));
}

const RNEXT: Record<RequestStatus, RequestStatus[]> = { pending: ["allowed", "denied", "expired"], allowed: ["expired"], denied: [], expired: [] };

export function decideAccessRequest(root: string, id: string, status: RequestStatus, opts: { minutes?: number } = {}, c?: Clock): AccessRequest {
  return updateRecord("access_request", existing(root, "requests", id), (r) => {
    if (!RNEXT[r.status].includes(status)) throw new Error(`request ${id} is ${r.status}; it can't become ${status}`);
    r.status = status;
    if (status === "allowed") {
      const minutes = opts.minutes ?? r.minutes;
      r.minutes = minutes;
      r.expires_at = new Date((c?.now ?? new Date()).getTime() + minutes * 60_000).toISOString();
    }
    if (status === "allowed" || status === "denied") r.decided_at = iso(c);
  });
}

const flat = (s: string) => s.replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ");

export function logActivity(root: string, event: string, detail = "", c?: Clock): ActivityEntry {
  const e: ActivityEntry = { at: iso(c), event: flat(event), detail: flat(detail) };
  appendLineSafe(root, path.join(careerosDir(root), "activity.jsonl"), JSON.stringify(e) + "\n");
  return e;
}

export function readActivity(root: string, limit = 50): ActivityEntry[] {
  const f = path.join(root, ".careeros", "activity.jsonl");
  if (!fs.existsSync(f) || fs.lstatSync(f).isSymbolicLink()) return [];
  const out: ActivityEntry[] = [];
  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8").split("\n")) {
    if (!line.trim()) continue;
    try { const v = JSON.parse(line) as ActivityEntry; if (typeof v.event === "string") out.push(v); } catch { /* skip bad lines */ }
  }
  return out.reverse().slice(0, limit);
}
```

## Group B: brand system (`@careeros/brand`, spec §7 + owner brief)

Generated token output (`packages/brand/dist/tokens.css`, `packages/brand/dist/tokens.ts`) is **committed**. A test rebuilds it from the DTCG sources and fails on any drift, so CI and fresh clones need no build step and a hand edit to `dist/` is caught.

### Task 9: Brand package, DTCG tokens, Style Dictionary build, contrast tests, test toolchain

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/brand/package.json`, `packages/brand/tsconfig.json`, `packages/brand/tokens/core.json`, `packages/brand/tokens/semantic.graphite.json`, `packages/brand/tokens/semantic.paper.json`, `packages/brand/tokens/component.json`, `packages/brand/scripts/build-tokens.mjs`, `packages/brand/scripts/build-tokens.d.mts`, `packages/brand/dist/tokens.css` (generated), `packages/brand/dist/tokens.ts` (generated), `packages/brand/test/tokens.test.ts`, `packages/brand/test/contrast.test.ts`, `scripts/typecheck.mjs`, `tests/dom-setup.ts`
- Modify: `package.json` (root), `vitest.config.ts`, `.gitignore`, `docs/dependencies.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - CSS custom properties named from the token path in kebab case, for example `--color-bg`, `--color-text-muted`, `--font-family-display`, `--font-size-14`, `--space-4`, `--radius-lg`, `--size-icon-md`, `--motion-duration-fast`, `--button-primary-bg`, `--nav-item-icon-box`, `--kpi-spark-width`. Primitives in `:root`; Graphite semantic + component tokens in `:root, [data-theme="graphite"]`; Paper in `[data-theme="paper"]`.
  - `dist/tokens.ts`: `export const core`, `graphite`, `paper` (records of token name to value, `as const`), `themes`, `type ThemeName = "graphite" | "paper"`, `type ThemeTokenName`, `type CoreTokenName`.
  - `buildTokens(): Promise<{ css: string; ts: string }>` from `scripts/build-tokens.mjs`; `npm run tokens` writes `dist/`.
  - Root Vitest projects: `*.test.ts` -> node, `*.test.tsx` -> jsdom with `tests/dom-setup.ts`. Aliases `@careeros/core`, `@careeros/contract`, `@careeros/services` point at each package's `src/index.ts`.
  - `npm run typecheck` runs `tsc --noEmit` for every `packages/*/tsconfig.json`.

`scripts/typecheck.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Type-checks every package that has a tsconfig.json. Exit 1 if any fails.
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const root = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const tsc = path.join(root, "node_modules", "typescript", "bin", "tsc");
const pkgs = fs.readdirSync(path.join(root, "packages")).filter((p) => fs.existsSync(path.join(root, "packages", p, "tsconfig.json"))).sort();
let failed = 0;
for (const p of pkgs) {
  try {
    execFileSync(process.execPath, [tsc, "-p", path.join("packages", p, "tsconfig.json"), "--noEmit"], { cwd: root, stdio: "inherit" });
  } catch {
    failed++;
    console.error(`typecheck failed: packages/${p}`);
  }
}
process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);
```

});

`packages/brand/test/contrast.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { themes } from "../dist/tokens.ts";

function lum(hex: string): number {
  const h = hex.replace("#", "");
  const [r, g, b] = [0, 2, 4].map((i) => parseInt(h.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255).map((c) => (c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4));
  return 0.2126 * r! + 0.7152 * g! + 0.0722 * b!;
}
export function contrast(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [x, y] = [lum(a), lum(b)];
  return (Math.max(x, y) + 0.05) / (Math.min(x, y) + 0.05);
}

describe("WCAG AA contrast", () => {
  for (const [name, t] of Object.entries(themes)) {
    it(`${name}: text roles reach 4.5:1 on bg and bg-raised`, () => {
      for (const fg of ["color-text", "color-text-muted", "color-accent", "color-success", "color-warning", "color-danger", "color-info"] as const) {
        for (const bg of ["color-bg", "color-bg-raised"] as const) {
          expect(contrast(t[fg], t[bg]), `${fg} on ${bg}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
        }
      }
    });
    it(`${name}: primary button text reaches 4.5:1`, () => {
      expect(contrast(t["button-primary-fg"], t["button-primary-bg"])).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
      expect(contrast(t["button-primary-fg"], t["button-primary-bg-hover"])).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
    });
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the Style Dictionary build**

`packages/brand/scripts/build-tokens.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Builds dist/tokens.css and dist/tokens.ts from the DTCG sources in tokens/.
// Style Dictionary parses DTCG, resolves references and names tokens (kebab case);
// this script writes the two files deterministically (sorted, no timestamps).
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import StyleDictionary from "style-dictionary";

const pkg = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
const src = (f) => path.join(pkg, "tokens", f);
const THEMES = [
  { name: "graphite", file: "semantic.graphite.json", selector: ':root, [data-theme="graphite"]' },
  { name: "paper", file: "semantic.paper.json", selector: '[data-theme="paper"]' },
];
const HEADER = "Generated by packages/brand/scripts/build-tokens.mjs from packages/brand/tokens/*.json. Do not edit.";

async function tokensFor(files) {
  const sd = new StyleDictionary({
    source: files.map(src),
    log: { verbosity: "silent", warnings: "disabled" },
    platforms: { css: { transforms: ["name/kebab"] } },
  });
  const { allTokens } = await sd.getPlatformTokens("css");
  return allTokens
    .map((t) => ({ name: t.name, value: String(t.$value ?? t.value), core: t.filePath.endsWith("core.json") }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

const block = (selector, list) => `${selector} {\n${list.map((t) => `  --${t.name}: ${t.value};`).join("\n")}\n}\n`;
const record = (list) => JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(list.map((t) => [t.name, t.value])), null, 2);

export async function buildTokens() {
  const core = await tokensFor(["core.json"]);
  const themed = {};
  for (const th of THEMES) themed[th.name] = (await tokensFor(["core.json", th.file, "component.json"])).filter((t) => !t.core);
  const css = [`/* ${HEADER} */\n`, block(":root", core), ...THEMES.map((th) => block(th.selector, themed[th.name]))].join("\n");
  const ts = [
    `// ${HEADER}`,
    `export const core = ${record(core)} as const;`,
    ...THEMES.map((th) => `export const ${th.name} = ${record(themed[th.name])} as const;`),
    "export const themes = { graphite, paper } as const;",
    "export type ThemeName = keyof typeof themes;",
    "export type ThemeTokenName = keyof typeof graphite;",
    "export type CoreTokenName = keyof typeof core;",
  ].join("\n\n") + "\n";
  return { css, ts };
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const { css, ts } = await buildTokens();
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(pkg, "dist"), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(pkg, "dist", "tokens.css"), css);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(pkg, "dist", "tokens.ts"), ts);
  console.log("wrote packages/brand/dist/tokens.css and tokens.ts");
}
```

`packages/brand/scripts/build-tokens.d.mts`:

```ts

```

`tests/brand-lint.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
type Rule = [RegExp, string];
const HEX: Rule = [/#(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{8}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3,4})\b/, "hex colour"];
const COLOUR_FN: Rule = [/\b(?:rgba?|hsla?)\(/, "raw colour function"];
const RENDERER_RULES: Rule[] = [
  HEX, COLOUR_FN,
  [/font-family\s*:/, "font-family literal"], [/fontFamily/, "fontFamily literal"],
  [/font-size\s*:\s*[\d.]+px/, "px font size"], [/fontSize\s*:\s*["']?\d/, "numeric fontSize"],
];
// Brand component sources may set font-family, but only through token variables.
const BRAND_RULES: Rule[] = [HEX, COLOUR_FN, [/font-family\s*:\s*(?!var\()/, "font-family without a token"]];

export function findViolations(text: string, rules: Rule[]): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  text.split("\n").forEach((line, i) => {
    for (const [re, what] of rules) if (re.test(line)) out.push(`line ${i + 1}: ${what}: ${line.trim()}`);
  });
  return out;
}

function files(dir: string): string[] {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((e) => {
    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) return files(p);
    return /\.(tsx?|css|html)$/.test(e.name) && !/\.test\.tsx?$/.test(e.name) ? [p] : [];
  });
}

describe("brand lint", () => {
  it("catches the patterns it is meant to catch", () => {
    expect(findViolations("color: #fff;", RENDERER_RULES)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(findViolations("background: rgba(0,0,0,.3)", RENDERER_RULES)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(findViolations("font-family: Geist", RENDERER_RULES)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(findViolations("style={{ fontSize: 14 }}", RENDERER_RULES)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(findViolations('href="#/jobs" color: var(--color-text)', RENDERER_RULES)).toEqual([]);
    expect(findViolations("font-family: var(--font-family-sans);", BRAND_RULES)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("renderer code uses brand tokens only", () => {
    const bad = [...files(path.join(ROOT, "packages/app-ui/src")), path.join(ROOT, "packages/app-ui/index.html")]
      .filter((f) => fs.existsSync(f))
      .flatMap((f) => findViolations(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8"), RENDERER_RULES).map((v) => `${path.relative(ROOT, f)} ${v}`));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("brand components consume tokens, not raw colours", () => {
    const bad = [...files(path.join(ROOT, "packages/brand/src")), ...files(path.join(ROOT, "packages/brand/guide"))]
      .flatMap((f) => findViolations(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8"), BRAND_RULES).map((v) => `${path.relative(ROOT, f)} ${v}`));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`packages/brand/src/components/Text.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ElementType, HTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from "react";
import { cx } from "../cx";

export type TextRole = "display" | "hero" | "heading-1" | "heading-2" | "body" | "body-sm" | "label" | "numeric";
const TAG: Record<TextRole, ElementType> = {
  display: "h1", hero: "span", "heading-1": "h1", "heading-2": "h2", body: "p", "body-sm": "p", label: "span", numeric: "span",
};

export interface TextProps extends HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> {
  role?: TextRole; as?: ElementType; truncate?: boolean; muted?: boolean; children?: ReactNode;
}

/** Typography by role, never by raw font. Truncated plain text gets a title so the full value stays readable. */
export function Text({ role = "body", as, truncate, muted, className, title, children, ...rest }: TextProps) {
  const Tag = as ?? TAG[role];
  const autoTitle = title ?? (truncate && (typeof children === "string" || typeof children === "number") ? String(children) : undefined);
  return (
    <Tag className={cx(`t-${role}`, truncate && "truncate", muted && "t-muted", className)} title={autoTitle} {...rest}>
      {children}
    </Tag>
  );
}
```

Note: `role` here is the typography role prop, not the ARIA attribute; pass ARIA roles through `{...rest}` is not possible under the same name, so components that need an ARIA role render their own element.

`packages/brand/src/index.ts`:

```ts
export { cx } from "./cx";
export { Text, type TextProps, type TextRole } from "./components/Text";
```

### Task 11: Brand primitives: Icon, Button, Pill, Badge, Kbd, Card, KpiCard, Sparkline, NavItem, form fields

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/brand/src/components/Icon.tsx`, `Button.tsx`, `Pill.tsx`, `Badge.tsx`, `Kbd.tsx`, `Card.tsx`, `KpiCard.tsx`, `NavItem.tsx`, `Field.tsx` (all under `packages/brand/src/components/`), `packages/brand/src/styles/components.css`, `packages/brand/test/primitives.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/brand/src/index.ts`, `packages/brand/src/styles/index.css`, `packages/brand/package.json`, `docs/dependencies.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `cx`, `Text` (Task 10); tokens (Task 9).
- Produces:
  - `Icon({ icon: LucideIcon; size?: "sm" | "md"; label?: string })` (decorative unless `label`; stroke 1.6)
  - `Button({ variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger"; size?: "sm" | "md"; loading?: boolean; icon?: LucideIcon } & ButtonHTMLAttributes)` (default `type="button"`, `secondary`)
  - `Pill({ tone?: "success" | "accent" | "neutral" | "warning" | "danger"; children; title? })`
  - `Badge({ children })`, `Kbd({ children })`
  - `Card({ highlight?: boolean; as?: "section" | "div" | "article"; className?; children } & HTMLAttributes)`
  - `sparkPoints(values: number[], width?: number, height?: number, pad?: number): string`
  - `Sparkline({ values: number[]; label: string })` (renders nothing for fewer than 2 values)
  - `KpiCard({ label: string; value: number | string; delta?: string; trend?: number[]; tone?: "default" | "accent" })`
  - `NavItem({ icon: LucideIcon; label: string; count?: number; current?: boolean; trailing?: ReactNode; onClick?: () => void })`
  - `Input`, `Textarea`, `Select`, `Checkbox`: labelled controls; props are the native element props plus `label: string; help?: string; error?: string; hideLabel?: boolean` (Select also `options: { value: string; label: string }[]`). `error` sets `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`.

New dependency: `lucide-react` 1.53.0 (ISC), brand dependency. One line-icon set for the whole app.

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { Briefcase } from "lucide-react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { Badge, Button, Card, Checkbox, Icon, Input, KpiCard, Kbd, NavItem, Pill, Select, sparkPoints } from "../src";

describe("brand primitives", () => {
  it("Button: variants, sizes, loading and disabled", async () => {
    const onClick = vi.fn();
    const { rerender } = render(<Button variant="primary" onClick={onClick}>Prepare</Button>);
    const b = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Prepare" });
    expect(b).toHaveClass("btn", "btn--primary");
    expect(b).toHaveAttribute("type", "button");
    await userEvent.click(b);
    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    rerender(<Button variant="danger" size="sm" loading onClick={onClick}>Decline</Button>);
    const d = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Decline" });
    expect(d).toHaveClass("btn--danger", "btn--sm");
    expect(d).toBeDisabled();
    expect(d).toHaveAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
    await userEvent.click(d);
    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("Icon: decorative by default, labelled when asked, stroke 1.6", () => {
    const { container } = render(<><Icon icon={Briefcase} /><Icon icon={Briefcase} label="Jobs" /></>);
    const [deco, named] = container.querySelectorAll("svg");
    expect(deco).toHaveAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    expect(deco).toHaveAttribute("stroke-width", "1.6");
    expect(named).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", "Jobs");
    expect(named).toHaveClass("icon", "icon--md");
  });
  it("Pill and Badge carry tone and text", () => {
    render(<><Pill tone="success">Sponsorship offered</Pill><Badge>51</Badge><Kbd>⌘K</Kbd></>);
    expect(screen.getByText("Sponsorship offered")).toHaveClass("pill", "pill--success");
    expect(screen.getByText("Sponsorship offered")).toHaveAttribute("title", "Sponsorship offered");
    expect(screen.getByText("51")).toHaveClass("badge");
    expect(screen.getByText("⌘K").tagName).toBe("KBD");
  });
  it("KpiCard: tabular numbers with an aligned sparkline", () => {
    render(<KpiCard label="Applied" value={4} delta="+2 this week" trend={[0, 0, 1, 2, 4]} tone="accent" />);
    expect(screen.getByText("4")).toHaveClass("kpi__value", "kpi__value--accent");
    expect(screen.getByText("4").parentElement).toHaveClass("kpi__row");
    expect(screen.getByRole("img", { name: "Applied trend: 0, 0, 1, 2, 4" })).toHaveClass("kpi__spark");
    expect(screen.getByText("+2 this week")).toHaveClass("kpi__delta");
  });
  it("sparkPoints spreads values across the box", () => {
    expect(sparkPoints([0, 10], 64, 24, 2)).toBe("0,22 64,2");
    expect(sparkPoints([5, 5, 5], 64, 24, 2)).toBe("0,22 32,22 64,22");
    expect(sparkPoints([])).toBe("");
  });
  it("NavItem: current page, count badge and truncated label", async () => {
    const onClick = vi.fn();
    render(<NavItem icon={Briefcase} label="Jobs" count={51} current onClick={onClick} />);
    const item = screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Jobs/ });
    expect(item).toHaveAttribute("aria-current", "page");
    expect(item.querySelector(".nav-item__icon")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText("51")).toHaveClass("badge");
    expect(screen.getByText("Jobs")).toHaveAttribute("title", "Jobs");
    await userEvent.click(item);
    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("form fields are labelled and announce errors", async () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    render(<>
      <Input label="Full name" defaultValue="Alex Example" />
      <Input label="Visa expiry" error="Use YYYY-MM-DD" defaultValue="soon" />
      <Select label="Work rights" options={[{ value: "citizen", label: "Citizen" }, { value: "needs_sponsorship", label: "Need visa sponsorship" }]} value="citizen" onChange={onChange} />
      <Checkbox label="Willing to relocate" />
    </>);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Full name")).toHaveValue("Alex Example");
    const bad = screen.getByLabelText("Visa expiry");
    expect(bad).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(bad).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Use YYYY-MM-DD");
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("Work rights"), "needs_sponsorship");
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalled();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText("Willing to relocate"));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Willing to relocate")).toBeChecked();
  });
  it("Card renders a section, highlighted when asked", () => {
    render(<Card highlight aria-label="Waiting for you">x</Card>);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Waiting for you")).toHaveClass("card", "card--highlight");
  });
});

```tsx
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { cx } from "../cx";

export interface IconProps { icon: LucideIcon; size?: "sm" | "md"; label?: string; className?: string }

/** Size comes from tokens via CSS (.icon--sm/.icon--md); stroke matches the brand (1.6). */
export function Icon({ icon: Glyph, size = "md", label, className }: IconProps) {
  return (
    <Glyph
      className={cx("icon", `icon--${size}`, className)}
      strokeWidth={1.6}
      aria-hidden={label ? undefined : true}
      aria-label={label}
      role={label ? "img" : undefined}
      focusable="false"
    />
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/Button.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from "react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { cx } from "../cx";
import { Icon } from "./Icon";

export type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
export interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant; size?: "sm" | "md"; loading?: boolean; icon?: LucideIcon; children?: ReactNode;
}

export function Button({ variant = "secondary", size = "md", loading = false, icon, disabled, className, children, type = "button", ...rest }: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      className={cx("btn", `btn--${variant}`, size === "sm" && "btn--sm", className)}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      aria-busy={loading || undefined}
      {...rest}
    >
      {loading ? <span className="btn__spinner" aria-hidden="true" /> : icon ? <Icon icon={icon} size="sm" /> : null}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}
```

export function Card({ highlight, as: Tag = "section", className, children, ...rest }: CardProps) {

`packages/brand/src/components/KpiCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cx } from "../cx";
import { Card } from "./Card";
import { Text } from "./Text";

const round = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 10) / 10;

export function sparkPoints(values: number[], width = 64, height = 24, pad = 2): string {
  if (!values.length) return "";
  const max = Math.max(...values);
  const min = Math.min(...values);
  const span = max - min || 1;
  const step = values.length > 1 ? width / (values.length - 1) : 0;
  return values.map((v, i) => `${round(i * step)},${round(height - pad - ((v - min) / span) * (height - 2 * pad))}`).join(" ");
}

export function Sparkline({ values, label }: { values: number[]; label: string }) {
  if (values.length < 2) return null;
  return (
    <svg className="kpi__spark" viewBox="0 0 64 24" preserveAspectRatio="none" role="img" aria-label={label}>
      <polyline fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} points={sparkPoints(values)} />
    </svg>
  );
}

export interface KpiCardProps { label: string; value: number | string; delta?: string; trend?: number[]; tone?: "default" | "accent" }

/** Number and sparkline share the row's last baseline (see .kpi__row). Numbers use lining tabular figures. */
export function KpiCard({ label, value, delta, trend, tone = "default" }: KpiCardProps) {
  return (
    <Card className="kpi" aria-label={label}>
      <Text role="label" as="div">{label}</Text>
      <div className="kpi__row">
        <span className={cx("kpi__value", tone === "accent" && "kpi__value--accent")}>{value}</span>
        {trend ? <Sparkline values={trend} label={`${label} trend: ${trend.join(", ")}`} /> : null}
      </div>
      {delta ? <div className="kpi__delta">{delta}</div> : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```
