```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "./Badge";
import { Icon } from "./Icon";

export interface NavItemProps { icon: LucideIcon; label: string; count?: number; current?: boolean; trailing?: ReactNode; onClick?: () => void }

export function NavItem({ icon, label, count, current, trailing, onClick }: NavItemProps) {
  return (
    <button type="button" className="nav-item" aria-current={current ? "page" : undefined} onClick={onClick}>
      <span className="nav-item__icon"><Icon icon={icon} /></span>
      <span className="nav-item__label truncate" title={label}>{label}</span>
      {count !== undefined && count > 0 ? <Badge>{count}</Badge> : null}
      {trailing ? <span className="nav-item__trailing">{trailing}</span> : null}
    </button>
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/Field.tsx`:

```tsx
import { forwardRef, useId, type InputHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode, type SelectHTMLAttributes, type TextareaHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cx } from "../cx";

interface FieldBits { label: string; help?: string; error?: string; hideLabel?: boolean }

function useFieldIds(help?: string, error?: string) {
  const id = useId();
  const helpId = help ? `${id}-help` : undefined;
  const errorId = error ? `${id}-error` : undefined;
  return { id, describedBy: [errorId, helpId].filter(Boolean).join(" ") || undefined, helpId, errorId };
}

function Shell({ id, label, hideLabel, help, helpId, error, errorId, children }: FieldBits & { id: string; helpId?: string; errorId?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <label htmlFor={id} className={cx("field__label", hideLabel && "sr-only")}>{label}</label>
      {children}
      {error ? <span id={errorId} className="field__error">{error}</span> : null}
      {help ? <span id={helpId} className="field__help">{help}</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export type InputProps = InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & FieldBits;
export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(function Input({ label, help, error, hideLabel, className, ...rest }, ref) {
  const ids = useFieldIds(help, error);
  return (
    <Shell {...ids} label={label} help={help} error={error} hideLabel={hideLabel}>
      <input ref={ref} id={ids.id} className={cx("input", className)} aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined} aria-describedby={ids.describedBy} {...rest} />
    </Shell>
  );
});

export type TextareaProps = TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement> & FieldBits;
export const Textarea = forwardRef<HTMLTextAreaElement, TextareaProps>(function Textarea({ label, help, error, hideLabel, className, ...rest }, ref) {
  const ids = useFieldIds(help, error);
  return (
    <Shell {...ids} label={label} help={help} error={error} hideLabel={hideLabel}>
      <textarea ref={ref} id={ids.id} className={cx("input", "textarea", className)} aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined} aria-describedby={ids.describedBy} {...rest} />
    </Shell>
  );
});

export type SelectProps = SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> & FieldBits & { options: { value: string; label: string }[] };
export const Select = forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(function Select({ label, help, error, hideLabel, options, className, ...rest }, ref) {
  const ids = useFieldIds(help, error);
  return (
    <Shell {...ids} label={label} help={help} error={error} hideLabel={hideLabel}>
      <select ref={ref} id={ids.id} className={cx("input", "select", className)} aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined} aria-describedby={ids.describedBy} {...rest}>
        {options.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </select>
    </Shell>
  );
});

export type CheckboxProps = Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, "type"> & { label: string; help?: string };
export const Checkbox = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, CheckboxProps>(function Checkbox({ label, help, className, ...rest }, ref) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className={cx("checkbox", className)}>
      <input ref={ref} id={id} type="checkbox" aria-describedby={help ? `${id}-help` : undefined} {...rest} />
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      {help ? <span id={`${id}-help`} className="field__help">{help}</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
});
```

```css
/* Icons */
.icon { display: block; flex: 0 0 auto; stroke-width: var(--size-icon-stroke); }
.icon--sm { width: var(--size-icon-sm); height: var(--size-icon-sm); }
.icon--md { width: var(--size-icon-md); height: var(--size-icon-md); }

/* Buttons */
.btn {
  display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: var(--button-gap);
  height: var(--button-height-md); padding: 0 var(--button-padding-md); border-radius: var(--button-radius);
  border: var(--size-border) solid transparent; font-family: var(--font-family-sans); font-size: var(--button-font-size-md);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium); line-height: var(--font-line-height-tight); cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap;
  transition: background var(--motion-duration-fast) var(--motion-easing-out), border-color var(--motion-duration-fast) var(--motion-easing-out), transform var(--motion-duration-fast) var(--motion-easing-out);
}
.btn:active:not(:disabled) { transform: translateY(var(--size-border)); }
.btn--primary { background: var(--button-primary-bg); color: var(--button-primary-fg); }
.btn--primary:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--button-primary-bg-hover); }
.btn--secondary { background: var(--button-secondary-bg); color: var(--button-secondary-fg); border-color: var(--button-secondary-border); }
.btn--secondary:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--button-secondary-bg-hover); }
.btn--ghost { background: transparent; color: var(--button-ghost-fg); }
.btn--ghost:hover:not(:disabled) { color: var(--button-ghost-fg-hover); background: var(--button-ghost-bg-hover); }
.btn--danger { background: var(--button-danger-bg); color: var(--button-danger-fg); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--button-danger-fg) 35%, transparent); }
.btn--sm { height: var(--button-height-sm); padding: 0 var(--button-padding-sm); font-size: var(--button-font-size-sm); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); }
.btn:disabled { opacity: 0.45; cursor: not-allowed; }
.btn__spinner {
  width: var(--size-icon-sm); height: var(--size-icon-sm); border-radius: var(--radius-full);
  border: var(--size-focus-ring) solid currentColor; border-right-color: transparent; animation: brand-spin var(--motion-duration-slow) linear infinite;
}
@keyframes brand-spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }

/* Pills, badges, keys */
.pill {
  display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-1); height: var(--pill-height); padding: 0 var(--pill-padding-x);
  border-radius: var(--pill-radius); border: var(--size-border) solid; font-family: var(--font-family-sans); font-size: var(--pill-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium); white-space: nowrap; max-width: 100%; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;
}
.pill--success { color: var(--color-success); background: var(--color-success-soft); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-success) 35%, transparent); }
.pill--accent { color: var(--color-accent); background: var(--color-accent-soft); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-accent) 35%, transparent); }
.pill--warning { color: var(--color-warning); background: var(--color-warning-soft); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-warning) 35%, transparent); }
.pill--danger { color: var(--color-danger); background: var(--color-danger-soft); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-danger) 35%, transparent); }
.pill--neutral { color: var(--color-text-muted); border-color: var(--color-border-strong); }
.badge {
  display: inline-grid; place-items: center; min-width: var(--nav-item-badge-min-width); height: var(--nav-item-badge-height);
  padding: 0 var(--space-1); border-radius: var(--radius-full); background: var(--color-accent-soft); color: var(--color-accent);
  font-family: var(--font-family-mono); font-size: var(--font-size-11); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
.kbd {
  font-family: var(--font-family-mono); font-size: var(--font-size-11); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm); padding: 0 var(--space-1); color: var(--color-text-muted);
}

/* Cards and KPIs */
.card {
  background: var(--card-bg); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--card-border); border-radius: var(--card-radius);
  padding: var(--card-padding); box-shadow: var(--card-shadow); min-width: 0;
}
.card--highlight {
  border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-accent) 35%, transparent);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--color-glow), transparent 70%), var(--card-bg);
}
.kpi__row { display: flex; align-items: last baseline; justify-content: space-between; gap: var(--space-2); margin-top: var(--space-2); }
.kpi__value { font-family: var(--font-family-display); font-size: var(--kpi-value-size); line-height: var(--font-line-height-tight); font-variant-numeric: lining-nums tabular-nums; }
.kpi__value--accent { color: var(--color-accent); }
.kpi__spark { display: block; width: var(--kpi-spark-width); height: var(--kpi-spark-height); color: var(--color-text-muted); flex: 0 0 auto; }
.kpi__delta { margin-top: var(--space-1); font-family: var(--font-family-mono); font-size: var(--font-size-11); color: var(--color-success); }

/* Navigation */
.nav-item {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--nav-item-gap); width: 100%; height: var(--nav-item-height); padding: 0 var(--space-3);
  border: 0; border-radius: var(--nav-item-radius); background: transparent; color: var(--color-text-muted); cursor: pointer; text-align: left;
  font-family: var(--font-family-sans); font-size: var(--font-size-14); font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium);
}
.nav-item:hover { color: var(--color-text); background: var(--color-surface); }
.nav-item[aria-current="page"] { color: var(--color-text); background: var(--color-surface); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 var(--size-border) var(--color-border); }
.nav-item__icon { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: var(--nav-item-icon-box); height: var(--nav-item-icon-box); flex: 0 0 var(--nav-item-icon-box); }
.nav-item__label { flex: 1; }
.nav-item__trailing { font-size: var(--font-size-12); color: var(--color-text-muted); }

/* Form fields */
.field { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1); min-width: 0; }
.field__label { font-size: var(--font-size-13); color: var(--color-text-muted); }
.field__help { font-size: var(--font-size-12); color: var(--color-text-muted); }
.field__error { font-size: var(--font-size-12); color: var(--color-danger); }
.input {
  height: var(--input-height); padding: 0 var(--input-padding-x); border-radius: var(--input-radius); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--input-border);
  background: var(--input-bg); color: var(--color-text); font-family: var(--font-family-sans); font-size: var(--font-size-14); min-width: 0;
}
.input:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--input-border-focus); box-shadow: 0 0 0 var(--space-1) var(--input-ring); }
.input[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: var(--color-danger); }
.textarea { height: auto; min-height: calc(var(--input-height) * 3); padding: var(--space-2) var(--input-padding-x); line-height: var(--font-line-height-body); resize: vertical; }
.checkbox { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); }
.checkbox input { width: var(--size-icon-sm); height: var(--size-icon-sm); accent-color: var(--color-accent); }
```

```tsx
describe("brand composites", () => {

      { id: "home", label: "Home", icon: House }, { id: "jobs", label: "Jobs", icon: Briefcase, count: 3 },
      { id: "privacy", label: "Privacy", icon: Lock, section: "secondary", trailing: "Locked" },

    const { rerender } = render(<Table caption="Jobs" rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.id} onRowActivate={onRow} columns={[{ key: "t", header: "Role", render: (r) => r.t }]} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Jobs" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByText("AI Engineer"));
    screen.getByText("AI Developer").closest("tr")!.focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Enter}");
    expect(onRow.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0].id)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    rerender(<Table caption="Jobs" rows={[] as typeof rows} rowKey={(r) => r.id} columns={[]} emptyText="No jobs match." />);
    expect(screen.getByText("No jobs match.")).toBeInTheDocument();

  it("Tabs switch with arrow keys", async () => {

    expect(screen.getByRole("tab", { name: "Score" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
    expect(screen.getByRole("tab", { name: "Score" })).toHaveFocus();
    expect(screen.getByRole("tabpanel")).toHaveTextContent("panel:score");
  });

  it("Dialog traps focus, closes on Escape and restores focus", async () => {

    await userEvent.keyboard("{Escape}");

});
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { cx } from "../cx";

const FOCUSABLE = "input, select, textarea, button, [href], [tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])";

export interface DialogProps { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; title: string; hideTitle?: boolean; children: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode }

export function Dialog({ open, onClose, title, hideTitle, children, actions }: DialogProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const closeRef = useRef(onClose);
  closeRef.current = onClose;
  const titleId = useId();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const el = ref.current;
    const focusables = () => [...(el?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? [])].filter((x) => !x.hasAttribute("disabled"));
    focusables()[0]?.focus();
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") { e.stopPropagation(); closeRef.current(); return; }
      if (e.key !== "Tab") return;
      const f = focusables();
      if (!f.length) return;
      const first = f[0]!;
      const last = f[f.length - 1]!;
      if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) { e.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
      else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) { e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => { document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey); previous?.focus?.(); };
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div className="dialog-scrim" onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) closeRef.current(); }}>
      <div ref={ref} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby={titleId} className="dialog">
        <h2 id={titleId} className={cx("t-heading-2", hideTitle && "sr-only")}>{title}</h2>
        <div className="dialog__body">{children}</div>
        {actions ? <div className="dialog__actions">{actions}</div> : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/CommandBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import { cx } from "../cx";
import { Dialog } from "./Dialog";

export interface CommandItem { id: string; label: string; group: string; hint?: string; run: () => void }
export interface CommandBarProps { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; items: CommandItem[]; query: string; onQueryChange: (q: string) => void; placeholder?: string }

export function useCommandShortcut(onOpen: () => void): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: globalThis.KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") { e.preventDefault(); onOpen(); }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onOpen]);
}

export function CommandBar({ open, onClose, items, query, onQueryChange, placeholder = "Search jobs or run an action" }: CommandBarProps) {
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const listId = useId();
  useEffect(() => setActive(0), [query, items.length]);
  const choose = (i: number) => {
    const it = items[i];
    if (!it) return;
    onClose();
    it.run();
  };
  const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { e.preventDefault(); setActive((a) => Math.min(a + 1, items.length - 1)); }
    else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { e.preventDefault(); setActive((a) => Math.max(a - 1, 0)); }
    else if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); choose(active); }
  };
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Command" hideTitle>
      <input
        role="combobox" aria-label="Command" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls={listId}
        aria-activedescendant={items[active] ? `${listId}-${active}` : undefined}
        className="input cmd-input" value={query} placeholder={placeholder}
        onChange={(e) => onQueryChange(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={onKey}
      />
      <ul role="listbox" id={listId} className="cmd-list" aria-label="Results">
        {items.map((it, i) => (
          <li
            key={it.id} id={`${listId}-${i}`} role="option" aria-selected={i === active}
            className={cx("cmd-item", i === active && "is-active")}
            onMouseEnter={() => setActive(i)} onClick={() => choose(i)}
          >
            <span className="t-label cmd-item__group">{it.group}</span>
            <span className="truncate" title={it.label}>{it.label}</span>
            {it.hint ? <span className="t-body-sm t-muted">{it.hint}</span> : null}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {!items.length ? <p className="t-body-sm t-muted">No matches.</p> : null}
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/NavRail.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { Kbd } from "./Kbd";
import { NavItem } from "./NavItem";

export interface NavRailItem { id: string; label: string; icon: LucideIcon; count?: number; trailing?: ReactNode; section?: "main" | "secondary" }
export interface NavRailProps { items: NavRailItem[]; current: string; onNavigate: (id: string) => void; onOpenCommand: () => void; shortcut?: string; footer?: ReactNode }

export function NavRail({ items, current, onNavigate, onOpenCommand, shortcut = "Ctrl K", footer }: NavRailProps) {
  const list = (section: "main" | "secondary") => (
    <ul className="rail__list">
      {items.filter((i) => (i.section ?? "main") === section).map((i) => (
        <li key={i.id}><NavItem icon={i.icon} label={i.label} count={i.count} trailing={i.trailing} current={i.id === current} onClick={() => onNavigate(i.id)} /></li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
  return (
    <nav className="rail" aria-label="Main">
      <div className="rail__brand"><span className="rail__mark" aria-hidden="true" /><span className="rail__wordmark">CareerOS</span></div>
      <button type="button" className="cmd-trigger" onClick={onOpenCommand}><span>Search or ask…</span><Kbd>{shortcut}</Kbd></button>
      {list("main")}
      <div className="rail__sep" role="separator" />
      {list("secondary")}
      {footer ? <div className="rail__footer">{footer}</div> : null}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/Table.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { cx } from "../cx";

export interface Column<T> { key: string; header: string; render: (row: T) => ReactNode; width?: string; align?: "start" | "end" }
export interface TableProps<T> {
  caption: string; columns: Column<T>[]; rows: T[]; rowKey: (r: T) => string;
  onRowActivate?: (r: T) => void; rowClassName?: (r: T) => string | undefined; emptyText?: string;
}

export function Table<T>({ caption, columns, rows, rowKey, onRowActivate, rowClassName, emptyText = "Nothing here yet." }: TableProps<T>) {
  if (!rows.length) return <p className="table-empty t-body-sm t-muted">{emptyText}</p>;
  return (
    <table className="table">
      <caption className="sr-only">{caption}</caption>
      <thead>
        <tr>{columns.map((c) => <th key={c.key} scope="col" className={cx("t-label", c.align === "end" && "is-end")} style={c.width ? { width: c.width } : undefined}>{c.header}</th>)}</tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {rows.map((r) => (
          <tr
            key={rowKey(r)}
            className={cx("table__row", onRowActivate && "table__row--clickable", rowClassName?.(r))}
            tabIndex={onRowActivate ? 0 : undefined}
            onClick={onRowActivate ? () => onRowActivate(r) : undefined}
            onKeyDown={onRowActivate ? (e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") onRowActivate(r); } : undefined}
          >
            {columns.map((c) => <td key={c.key} className={cx(c.align === "end" && "is-end")}>{c.render(r)}</td>)}
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/Tabs.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useId, useRef, type KeyboardEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Badge } from "./Badge";

export interface TabDef { id: string; label: string; count?: number }
export interface TabsProps { tabs: TabDef[]; value: string; onChange: (id: string) => void; label: string; children: ReactNode }

export function Tabs({ tabs, value, onChange, label, children }: TabsProps) {
  const base = useId();
  const refs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([]);
  const move = (i: number) => {
    const n = (i + tabs.length) % tabs.length;
    onChange(tabs[n]!.id);
    refs.current[n]?.focus();
  };
  const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent, i: number) => {
    if (e.key === "ArrowRight") { e.preventDefault(); move(i + 1); }
    else if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") { e.preventDefault(); move(i - 1); }
    else if (e.key === "Home") { e.preventDefault(); move(0); }
    else if (e.key === "End") { e.preventDefault(); move(tabs.length - 1); }
  };
  return (
    <div className="tabs">
      <div role="tablist" aria-label={label} className="tabs__list">
        {tabs.map((t, i) => (
          <button
            key={t.id} ref={(el) => { refs.current[i] = el; }} type="button" role="tab" id={`${base}-tab-${t.id}`}
            aria-selected={t.id === value} aria-controls={`${base}-panel`} tabIndex={t.id === value ? 0 : -1}
            className="tabs__tab" onClick={() => onChange(t.id)} onKeyDown={(e) => onKey(e, i)}
          >
            {t.label}{t.count ? <Badge>{t.count}</Badge> : null}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div role="tabpanel" id={`${base}-panel`} aria-labelledby={`${base}-tab-${value}`} className="tabs__panel" tabIndex={0}>{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/brand/src/components/Toast.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { cx } from "../cx";

type Tone = "success" | "danger" | "neutral";
interface Msg { id: number; tone: Tone; text: string }
type Push = (t: { text: string; tone?: Tone }) => void;

const ToastContext = createContext<Push>(() => undefined);
let seq = 0;

export function ToastProvider({ children, timeoutMs = 5000 }: { children: ReactNode; timeoutMs?: number }) {
  const [list, setList] = useState<Msg[]>([]);
  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => setList((l) => l.filter((m) => m.id !== id)), []);
  const push = useCallback<Push>((t) => {
    const id = ++seq;
    setList((l) => [...l, { id, tone: t.tone ?? "neutral", text: t.text }]);
    setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), timeoutMs);
  }, [dismiss, timeoutMs]);
  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={push}>
      {children}
      <div className="toasts" role="status" aria-live="polite">
        {list.map((m) => (
          <div key={m.id} className={cx("toast", `toast--${m.tone}`)}>
            <span>{m.text}</span>
            <button type="button" className="toast__close" aria-label="Dismiss" onClick={() => dismiss(m.id)}>×</button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

export const useToast = (): Push => useContext(ToastContext);
```

```css
/* Nav rail */
.rail {
  width: var(--size-rail); flex: 0 0 var(--size-rail); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1);
  padding: var(--space-4) var(--space-3); border-right: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--color-bg-raised), transparent);
}
.rail__brand { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-2) var(--space-4); }
.rail__mark {
  width: var(--space-6); height: var(--space-6); border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: conic-gradient(from 200deg, var(--color-accent), var(--color-mark-mid), var(--color-success), var(--color-accent));
}
.rail__wordmark { font-family: var(--font-family-display); font-size: var(--font-size-24); line-height: var(--font-line-height-tight); }
.rail__list { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1); }
.rail__sep { height: var(--size-border); background: var(--color-border); margin: var(--space-3) var(--space-2); }
.rail__footer {
  margin-top: auto; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-lg); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border);
  background: var(--color-surface); font-size: var(--font-size-12); color: var(--color-text-muted);
}
.status-dot { display: inline-block; width: var(--space-2); height: var(--space-2); border-radius: var(--radius-full); background: var(--color-success); margin-right: var(--space-1); }
.status-dot--off { background: var(--color-text-muted); }

/* Command bar */
.cmd-trigger {
  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 var(--space-1) var(--space-3); padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); background: var(--color-surface); color: var(--color-text-muted); cursor: pointer;
}
.cmd-input { width: 100%; }
.cmd-list { list-style: none; margin: var(--space-3) 0 0; padding: 0; max-height: calc(var(--table-row-height) * 8); overflow-y: auto; }
.cmd-item { display: grid; grid-template-columns: var(--space-12) minmax(0, 1fr) auto; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-3); padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-md); cursor: pointer; }
.cmd-item.is-active { background: var(--color-surface-hover); }

/* Table */
.table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
.table th { text-align: left; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-bottom: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); font-weight: var(--font-weight-regular); }
.table td { height: var(--table-row-height); padding: 0 var(--space-3); border-bottom: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); max-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.table .is-end { text-align: right; }
.table__row--clickable { cursor: pointer; }
.table__row--clickable:hover { background: var(--color-surface-hover); }
.table__row:focus-visible { outline: var(--size-focus-ring) solid var(--color-focus); outline-offset: calc(var(--size-focus-ring) * -1); }
.table-empty { padding: var(--space-6); text-align: center; }

/* Tabs */
.tabs__list { display: flex; gap: var(--space-1); border-bottom: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); }
.tabs__tab {
  display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border: 0; background: transparent;
  color: var(--color-text-muted); cursor: pointer; border-bottom: var(--size-focus-ring) solid transparent;
}
.tabs__tab[aria-selected="true"] { color: var(--color-text); border-bottom-color: var(--color-accent); }
.tabs__panel { padding: var(--space-4) 0; }

/* Dialog */
.dialog-scrim { position: fixed; inset: 0; display: grid; place-items: center; background: var(--dialog-scrim); z-index: 10; }
.dialog {
  width: min(var(--dialog-width), calc(100vw - var(--space-8))); max-height: calc(100vh - var(--space-12)); overflow: auto;
  background: var(--color-bg-raised); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border-strong); border-radius: var(--dialog-radius);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-pop); padding: var(--space-6); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-4);
  animation: brand-rise var(--motion-duration-base) var(--motion-easing-out) both;
}
.dialog__actions { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: var(--space-2); }
@keyframes brand-rise { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(var(--space-2)); } to { opacity: 1; transform: none; } }

/* Toasts */
.toasts { position: fixed; right: var(--space-6); bottom: var(--space-6); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-2); z-index: 20; }
.toast {
  width: var(--toast-width); display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: var(--space-3); padding: var(--space-3) var(--space-4);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border-strong); background: var(--color-bg-raised); box-shadow: var(--shadow-pop);
}
.toast--success { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-success) 45%, transparent); }
.toast--danger { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-danger) 45%, transparent); }
.toast__close { border: 0; background: transparent; color: var(--color-text-muted); cursor: pointer; }
```

### Task 13: Brand assets, brand guide page and visual snapshot test

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/brand/assets/mark.svg`, `packages/brand/assets/wordmark.svg`, `packages/brand/assets/app-icon.svg`, `packages/brand/guide/index.html`, `packages/brand/guide/main.tsx`, `packages/brand/guide/vite.config.ts`, `packages/brand/playwright.config.ts`, `packages/brand/test-visual/guide.spec.ts`, `packages/brand/src/vite-env.d.ts`
- Generate and commit: `docs/assets/brand-snapshots/guide-graphite-linux.png`, `docs/assets/brand-snapshots/guide-paper-linux.png`
- Modify: `packages/brand/package.json`, `.github/workflows/checks.yml`, `docs/dependencies.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every brand component and `dist/tokens.ts`.
- Produces: `npm run guide:build -w @careeros/brand` (static guide in `packages/brand/dist-guide/`), `npm run visual -w @careeros/brand`, `npm run visual:docker -w @careeros/brand` and `visual:update` (run in the pinned Playwright Linux image for stable pixels). Snapshots live in `docs/assets/brand-snapshots/` (the privacy scanner allows binaries only under `docs/assets/`).

Ruling: visual snapshots run only in the pinned Linux image `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.64.0-noble` (locally through Docker and in CI), so one baseline per theme is stable across machines. The wordmark SVG uses live `<text>` in Instrument Serif; the app renders the wordmark as HTML text with the display font, so the SVG is for docs and listings only.

New dependencies (exact pins; licences): `vite` 7.3.7 (MIT), `@vitejs/plugin-react` 5.2.0 (MIT), `@playwright/test` 1.64.0 (Apache-2.0), all brand devDependencies. (Vite 7 is pinned, not 8, because Vitest 3.2.7 supports Vite up to 7.)

```tsx
import { StrictMode, useState } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { Briefcase, House, Inbox, Lock, Send } from "lucide-react";
import "../src/styles/index.css";
import { core, themes, type ThemeName } from "../dist/tokens.ts";
import {
  Badge, Button, Card, Checkbox, Input, Kbd, KpiCard, NavRail, Pill, Select, Table, Tabs, Text,
} from "../src";

const SWATCHES = ["color-bg", "color-bg-raised", "color-surface", "color-border-strong", "color-text", "color-text-muted", "color-accent", "color-success", "color-warning", "color-danger", "color-info", "color-focus"] as const;

function Panel({ theme }: { theme: ThemeName }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("one");
  const t = themes[theme];
  return (
    <section data-theme={theme} className="guide-panel" aria-label={`${theme} theme`}>
      <div className="guide-row guide-between">
        <Text role="heading-1" as="h2">CareerOS</Text>
        <Text role="label">{theme === "graphite" ? "Graphite · dark" : "Paper · light"}</Text>
      </div>
      <Text role="label" as="h3" className="guide-section">Colour · semantic tokens</Text>
      <div className="guide-swatches">
        {SWATCHES.map((name) => (
          <div key={name} className="guide-swatch">
            <span className="guide-chip" style={{ background: `var(--${name})` }} />
            <Text role="body-sm">{name.replace("color-", "")}</Text>
            <Text role="label">{t[name]}</Text>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <Text role="label" as="h3" className="guide-section">Type roles</Text>
      <Text role="display">Good morning, <i className="guide-accent">Alex.</i></Text>
      <div className="guide-row"><Text role="hero" className="guide-accent">158</Text><Text role="heading-1" as="span">Heading one</Text><Text role="heading-2" as="span">Heading two</Text></div>
      <Text role="body">Body · Geist 14/1.5. Two applications are ready for your review.</Text>
      <div className="guide-row"><Text role="label">Label · Geist Mono caps</Text><Text role="numeric">0123456789 · $150–170k</Text></div>
      <Text role="label" as="h3" className="guide-section">Buttons</Text>
      <div className="guide-row">
        <Button variant="primary">Prepare</Button><Button variant="primary" icon={Send}>Review &amp; approve</Button>
        <Button>Practise</Button><Button variant="ghost">Later</Button><Button variant="danger">Decline</Button>
        <Button size="sm">Small</Button><Button variant="primary" loading>Saving</Button><Button variant="primary" disabled>Disabled</Button>
      </div>
      <Text role="label" as="h3" className="guide-section">Pills, badges, keys</Text>
      <div className="guide-row">
        <Pill tone="success">Sponsorship offered</Pill><Pill tone="accent">Interview Thu</Pill><Pill>Ad silent</Pill>
        <Pill tone="warning">Visa ends soon</Pill><Pill tone="danger">Citizens/PR only</Pill><Badge>51</Badge><Kbd>Ctrl K</Kbd>
      </div>
      <Text role="label" as="h3" className="guide-section">Navigation and inputs</Text>
      <div className="guide-row guide-top">
        <NavRail current="home" onNavigate={() => undefined} onOpenCommand={() => undefined} items={[
          { id: "home", label: "Home", icon: House }, { id: "jobs", label: "Jobs", icon: Briefcase, count: 51 },
          { id: "inbox", label: "Inbox", icon: Inbox, count: 3 }, { id: "privacy", label: "Privacy", icon: Lock, section: "secondary", trailing: "Locked" },
        ]} footer={<><span className="status-dot" />Claude · desktop app</>} />
        <div className="guide-col">
          <Input label="Search" placeholder="Search role, company…" />
          <Input label="Visa expiry" defaultValue="soon" error="Use YYYY-MM-DD" />
          <Select label="Work rights" options={[{ value: "needs_sponsorship", label: "Need visa sponsorship" }]} defaultValue="needs_sponsorship" />
          <Checkbox label="Willing to relocate" defaultChecked />
        </div>
      </div>
      <Text role="label" as="h3" className="guide-section">Cards</Text>
      <div className="guide-grid">
        <KpiCard label="Applied" value={4} delta="+2 this week" trend={[0, 0, 1, 2, 4]} tone="accent" />
        <Card highlight><Text role="label">Waiting for you</Text><Text role="heading-2">Application ready</Text><Button variant="primary" size="sm">Review</Button></Card>
      </div>
      <Tabs label="Example" value={tab} onChange={setTab} tabs={[{ id: "one", label: "Description" }, { id: "two", label: "Score", count: 2 }]}>
        <Table caption="Example jobs" rowKey={(r) => r.id} rows={[{ id: "a", role: "Backend Engineer (LLM) at Example Labs, a very long company name that truncates", fit: 64 }]}
          columns={[{ key: "role", header: "Role", render: (r) => <Text truncate as="span">{r.role}</Text> }, { key: "fit", header: "Fit", align: "end", render: (r) => <Text role="numeric">{r.fit}</Text> }]} />
      </Tabs>
      <Text role="label">{Object.keys(core).length} core tokens · {Object.keys(t).length} theme tokens</Text>
    </section>
  );
}

function Guide() {
  return (
    <main className="guide">
      <h1 className="sr-only">CareerOS brand</h1>
      <Panel theme="graphite" />
      <Panel theme="paper" />
    </main>
  );
}

const style = document.createElement("style");
style.textContent = `
.guide { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr); }
.guide-panel { background: var(--color-bg); color: var(--color-text); padding: var(--space-8); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-3); }
.guide-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-3); flex-wrap: wrap; }
.guide-between { justify-content: space-between; }
.guide-top { align-items: flex-start; }
.guide-col { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-3); flex: 1; }
.guide-section { margin-top: var(--space-6); padding-top: var(--space-3); border-top: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border); }
.guide-swatches { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: var(--space-3); }
.guide-swatch { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1); }
.guide-chip { display: block; height: var(--space-10); border-radius: var(--radius-md); border: var(--size-border) solid var(--color-border-strong); }
.guide-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr); gap: var(--space-3); }
.guide-accent { color: var(--color-accent); }
`;
document.head.append(style);
createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(<StrictMode><Guide /></StrictMode>);
```

`packages/brand/test-visual/guide.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

test("guide loads only local files and the bundled fonts", async ({ page }) => {
  const external: string[] = [];
  page.on("request", (r) => { if (!r.url().startsWith("http://127.0.0.1:4179/")) external.push(r.url()); });
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready);
  expect(external).toEqual([]);
  const families = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.fonts].filter((f) => f.status === "loaded").map((f) => f.family.replace(/"/g, "")));
  expect(families).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["Instrument Serif", "Geist", "Geist Mono"]));
});

for (const theme of ["graphite", "paper"] as const) {
  test(`brand guide snapshot: ${theme}`, async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/");
    await page.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready);
    await expect(page.locator(`[data-theme="${theme}"]`)).toHaveScreenshot(`guide-${theme}.png`);
  });
}
```

## Group C: renderer (`@careeros/contract`, `@careeros/app-ui`, spec §3-4)

The renderer depends only on `@careeros/contract` (types, validators, pure view logic) and `@careeros/brand`. It never imports `electron`, `node:*` or `@careeros/core`; Task 16 adds a test that enforces this. Every screen reads and writes through `CareerosBridge`. In tests and in `npm run dev -w @careeros/app-ui` the bridge is the in-memory `MockBridge`; in the app it is `createRemoteBridge(window.careerosTransport)` from the Electron preload (Task 34). A future shell only has to provide another `Transport`.

### Task 14: `@careeros/contract`: the bridge, its argument validators and shared view logic

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/contract/package.json`, `packages/contract/tsconfig.json`, `packages/contract/src/constants.ts`, `packages/contract/src/types.ts`, `packages/contract/src/validate.ts`, `packages/contract/src/methods.ts`, `packages/contract/src/bridge.ts`, `packages/contract/src/logic.ts`, `packages/contract/src/index.ts`, `packages/contract/test/validate.test.ts`, `packages/contract/test/bridge.test.ts`, `packages/contract/test/logic.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (all exported from `@careeros/contract`):
  - Constants mirroring core: `JOB_STATUSES`, `WR_LABELS`, `WR_STATUSES`, `JOB_SOURCES`, `LOCKABLE_DIRS`, `ME_DIRS`, `JOB_SUBDIRS`, `APPROVAL_ACTIONS`, `APPROVAL_STATUSES`, `REQUEST_STATUSES`, plus `SETTINGS_KINDS = ["profile", "search", "integrations"]`, `SECRET_NAMES = ["gmail_app_password", "anthropic_api_key"]`, `CHANGE_AREAS`, `WR_LABEL_TEXT`, `topicSlug(s)`.
  - View types: `FieldDef`, `JobRecord`, `JobRow`, `JobQuery`, `JobList`, `JobFile`, `JobDetail`, `SavedView`, `WeekPoint`, `NextUpItem`, `HomeSummary`, `WorkspaceSummary`, `LegacyPreview`, `InboxTarget`, `InboxSuggestion`, `InboxItem`, `LearnItem`, `LearnTopic`, `InterviewPrep`, `LearnData`, `SponsorRow`, `SearchRow`, `SearchList`, `SettingsKind`, `SettingsChange`, `SettingsData`, `Grant`, `AccessRequestView`, `ActivityEntry`, `PrivacyState`, `ApprovalView`, `ApplyState`, `ClaimLink`, `SecretStatus`, `AppInfo`, `ChangeArea`, `ChangeEvent`, `DroppedFile`.
  - `interface CareerosBridge` (namespaces `app`, `workspace`, `home`, `jobs`, `apply`, `inbox`, `learn`, `files`, `sponsors`, `searches`, `settings`, `privacy`, `approvals`, `secrets`, `links`, `claude`, plus local `onChange`, `pathForFile`, `fileUrl`).
  - `interface Transport { call(method: MethodName, args: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>; onChange(cb: (e: ChangeEvent) => void): () => void; pathForFile(file: DroppedFile): string | null; fileUrl(rel: string): string }`
  - `createRemoteBridge(t: Transport): CareerosBridge`
  - `METHODS` (method name -> argument validators), `type MethodName`, `validateArgs(method: string, args: unknown): unknown[]`, `class ArgError`.
  - Pure logic used by both MockBridge and services: `isEligible(status, label): boolean`, `jobRowFromRecord(job, companyName, eligible): JobRow`, `applyJobQuery(rows, q): JobList`, `visaDaysLeft(expiry, now): number | null`, `isReply(event): boolean`, `weeklyActivity(events, now, weeks?): WeekPoint[]`, `buildNextUp({ rows, approvals, requests, inbox }): NextUpItem[]`, `homeSummary({ name, rows, events, visaExpiry, approvals, requests, inbox, now }): HomeSummary`, `fileKind(path): FileKind`, `gapsFrom(job): string[]`, `applyStep(status, files, approval): ApplyState["step"]`, `buildApplyPrompt(title, company, slug, id): string`.

Ruling: the bridge types live in their own package (not inside `app-ui`) so the Node services can validate against the same definitions without depending on React. Relative imports inside `contract` use `.js` extensions so both Vite (Bundler resolution) and the services' NodeNext typecheck accept them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create the package**

`packages/contract/package.json`:

```json
{
  "name": "@careeros/contract",
  "version": "0.1.0",
  "private": true,
  "type": "module",
  "exports": { ".": "./src/index.ts" }
}
```

`packages/contract/tsconfig.json`:

```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "target": "ES2022", "module": "NodeNext", "moduleResolution": "NodeNext", "strict": true, "noEmit": true,
    "skipLibCheck": true, "lib": ["ES2023"], "types": ["node"], "allowImportingTsExtensions": true,
    "baseUrl": ".", "paths": { "@careeros/core": ["../core/src/index.ts"] }
  },
  "include": ["src", "test"]
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ArgError, METHODS, validateArgs } from "../src/index.js";

const bad = (m: string, args: unknown) => () => validateArgs(m, args);

describe("validateArgs", () => {
  it("rejects unknown methods and wrong argument counts", () => {
    expect(bad("fs.readFile", [])).toThrow(ArgError);
    expect(bad("constructor", [])).toThrow(/unknown method/);
    expect(bad("jobs.get", ["acme-corp"])).toThrow(/expected 2 argument/);
    expect(bad("jobs.get", "acme-corp/x")).toThrow(/must be a list/);
  });
  it("rejects unsafe slugs, ids and paths", () => {
    expect(bad("jobs.get", ["../etc", "x"])).toThrow(/invalid format/);
    expect(bad("approvals.approve", ["A B"])).toThrow(/invalid format/);
    for (const rel of ["../x", "me/../identity/x", "/etc/passwd", "a\\b", "C:/x", "a//b", "a/./b", "x\u0000y"]) {
      expect(bad("files.readText", [rel]), rel).toThrow(ArgError);
    }
    expect(bad("inbox.add", [["relative/file.pdf"]])).toThrow(/absolute path/);
    expect(bad("inbox.add", [[]])).toThrow(/at least 1/);
  });
  it("rejects extra keys, bad enums and prototype keys", () => {
    expect(bad("jobs.list", [{ text: "ai", hack: true }])).toThrow(/hack is not allowed/);
    expect(bad("jobs.list", [{ limit: 0 }])).toThrow(/whole number from 1 to 500/);
    expect(bad("jobs.setStatus", ["acme-corp", "x", "Ghosted", ""])).toThrow(/must be one of/);
    expect(bad("inbox.file", ["cv.pdf", { kind: "job", company_slug: "acme-corp" }, ""])).toThrow(/id/);
    expect(bad("inbox.file", ["cv.pdf", { kind: "disk", dir: "/" }, ""])).toThrow(/"me" or "job"/);
    expect(bad("settings.save", ["profile", [{ path: "__proto__.polluted", value: 1 }]])).toThrow(/not allowed/);
    expect(bad("settings.save", ["profile", JSON.parse('[{"path":"contact.name","value":"x","__proto__":{"a":1}}]')])).toThrow(/not allowed/);
    expect(bad("privacy.allow", ["abc", 241])).toThrow(/1 to 240/);
  });
  it("accepts good input and returns it cleaned", () => {
    expect(validateArgs("jobs.list", [{ text: "ai", statuses: ["Found"], showIneligible: false, limit: 50 }])).toEqual([{ text: "ai", statuses: ["Found"], showIneligible: false, limit: 50 }]);
    expect(validateArgs("inbox.file", ["cv.pdf", { kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, "visa grant"])).toEqual(["cv.pdf", { kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, "visa grant"]);
    expect(validateArgs("files.readText", ["companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer/jd.md"])).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(validateArgs("inbox.add", [["/fictional/alex-example/Downloads/cv.pdf"]])).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(validateArgs("inbox.add", [["C:\\fictional\\cv.pdf"]])).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("has a validator list for every method", () => {
    for (const [name, spec] of Object.entries(METHODS)) expect(Array.isArray(spec), name).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as core from "@careeros/core";
import {
  APPROVAL_ACTIONS, createRemoteBridge, JOB_SOURCES, JOB_STATUSES, JOB_SUBDIRS, LOCKABLE_DIRS, ME_DIRS, METHODS,
  topicSlug, WR_LABELS, WR_STATUSES, type MethodName,
} from "../src/index.js";

describe("createRemoteBridge", () => {
  it("routes every bridge call to exactly the METHODS list", async () => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const bridge = createRemoteBridge({
      call: async (m: MethodName) => { seen.add(m); return null; },
      onChange: () => () => undefined, pathForFile: () => null, fileUrl: (r) => `careeros-file://ws/${r}`,
    });
    for (const [ns, group] of Object.entries(bridge)) {
      if (typeof group !== "object" || group === null) continue;
      for (const fn of Object.values(group as Record<string, unknown>)) {
        if (typeof fn === "function") await (fn as (...a: unknown[]) => Promise<unknown>)();
      }
      expect(ns).toMatch(/^[a-z]+$/);
    }
    expect([...seen].sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(METHODS).sort());
    expect(bridge.fileUrl("learning/a b.md")).toBe("careeros-file://ws/learning/a b.md");
  });
});

describe("constants mirror core", () => {
  it("matches core's enums", () => {
    expect(JOB_STATUSES).toEqual(core.JOB_STATUSES);
    expect(WR_LABELS).toEqual(core.WR_LABELS);
    expect(WR_STATUSES).toEqual(core.WR_STATUSES);
    expect(JOB_SOURCES).toEqual(core.JOB_SOURCES);
    expect(LOCKABLE_DIRS).toEqual(core.LOCKABLE_DIRS);
    expect(ME_DIRS).toEqual(core.ME_DIRS);
    expect(JOB_SUBDIRS).toEqual(core.JOB_SUBDIRS);
    expect(APPROVAL_ACTIONS).toEqual(core.APPROVAL_ACTIONS);
  });
  it("makes the same topic slugs as core", () => {
    for (const s of ["RAG pipelines", "Kubernetes (basics)", "C++ & Rust", "Café design"]) expect(topicSlug(s)).toBe(core.slugify(s, "topic"));
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  applyJobQuery, applyStep, buildApplyPrompt, buildNextUp, gapsFrom, isEligible, isReply, jobRowFromRecord, visaDaysLeft, weeklyActivity,
  type JobRecord, type JobRow,
} from "../src/index.js";

function job(over: Partial<JobRecord> & { id: string; company_slug: string }): JobRecord {
  return {
    client: null, title: "AI Engineer", source: "ats", source_site: "Greenhouse", url: "", apply_via: "", posted: "",
    found_at: "2026-01-12T09:00:00.000Z", location: "Sampletown NSW", work_mode: "Hybrid", job_type: "",
    salary: { min: null, max: null, currency: "", period: "", text: "" },
    work_rights: { label: "silent", quotes: [], decided_by: "none" },
    scores: { fit: { value: null, must_haves: [], nice_to_haves: [] }, sponsor: { value: null, tier: "", reasons: [] }, career: { match: null, odds: null, tier: "", scored_at: "" } },
    status: "Found", timeline: [], files: [], ...over,
  };
}
const row = (j: JobRecord, eligible = true): JobRow => jobRowFromRecord(j, j.company_slug, eligible);

describe("job list logic", () => {
  const rows = [
    row(job({ id: "a", company_slug: "example-labs", scores: { ...job({ id: "x", company_slug: "x" }).scores, fit: { value: 90, must_haves: [], nice_to_haves: [] } } })),
    row(job({ id: "b", company_slug: "acme-corp", work_rights: { label: "sponsorship_offered", quotes: [], decided_by: "scan" } })),
    row(job({ id: "c", company_slug: "acme-recruiting", work_rights: { label: "citizen_pr_only", quotes: [], decided_by: "scan" } }), false),
  ];
  it("pins sponsorship-offered rows first under every sort", () => {
    for (const sort of ["default", "fit", "sponsor", "newest"] as const) expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { sort }).rows[0]?.id).toBe("b");
  });
  it("hides ineligible ads by default and counts them", () => {
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, {})).toMatchObject({ total: 2, hiddenIneligible: 1 });
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { showIneligible: true })).toMatchObject({ total: 3, hiddenIneligible: 0 });
  });
  it("filters by text, status, label, fit, and keeps unknown salaries", () => {
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { text: "acme" }).rows.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["b"]);
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { fitMin: 50 }).rows.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a"]);
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { salaryMin: 150000 }).total).toBe(2);
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { labels: ["silent"] }).rows.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a"]);
    expect(applyJobQuery(rows, { limit: 1, offset: 1 }).rows.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a"]);
  });
  it("knows who can apply", () => {
    expect(isEligible("needs_sponsorship", "citizen_pr_only")).toBe(false);
    expect(isEligible("citizen", "clearance")).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("home logic", () => {
  const now = new Date("2026-01-15T10:00:00.000Z"); // a Thursday
  it("counts days left on a visa, or null when not set", () => {
    expect(visaDaysLeft("2026-01-25", now)).toBe(10);
    expect(visaDaysLeft("", now)).toBeNull();
    expect(visaDaysLeft("soon", now)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("buckets activity into Monday-start weeks", () => {
    const w = weeklyActivity([
      { at: "2026-01-12T09:00:00.000Z", event: "Found" }, { at: "2026-01-13T09:00:00.000Z", event: "Status: Applied" },
      { at: "2026-01-14T09:00:00.000Z", event: "Status: Interview" }, { at: "2026-01-02T09:00:00.000Z", event: "Found" },
      { at: "2025-01-01T09:00:00.000Z", event: "Found" },
    ], now, 3);
    expect(w).toEqual([
      { week: "2025-12-29", found: 1, applied: 0, replies: 0 },
      { week: "2026-01-05", found: 0, applied: 0, replies: 0 },
      { week: "2026-01-12", found: 1, applied: 1, replies: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(isReply("Email received")).toBe(true);
    expect(isReply("Note")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("orders next-up: approvals, requests, interviews, best jobs, inbox", () => {
    const rows = [
      row(job({ id: "i", company_slug: "example-labs", status: "Interview" })),
      row(job({ id: "p", company_slug: "acme-corp", work_rights: { label: "sponsorship_offered", quotes: [], decided_by: "scan" } })),
    ];
    const items = buildNextUp({
      rows,
      approvals: [{ id: "a1", action: "status_change", title: "Move to Interview", summary: "", steps: [], payload: {}, created_at: "", created_by: "claude", status: "pending", decided_at: "", result_note: "" }],
      requests: [{ id: "r1", folder: "me/identity", field: "", why: "visa expiry", who: "claude", minutes: 15, created_at: "", status: "pending", decided_at: "", expires_at: "" }],
      inbox: [{ name: "cv.pdf", size: 1, suggestion: { target: null, reason: "No rule matched. Choose a folder.", confidence: "low" } }],
    });
    expect(items.map((i) => i.kind)).toEqual(["approval", "request", "interview", "prepare", "inbox"]);
  });
});

describe("job detail and apply logic", () => {
  it("reads gaps only from must-haves marked unmet", () => {
    const j = job({ id: "g", company_slug: "acme-corp" });
    j.scores.fit.must_haves = ["Python", { skill: "Kubernetes", met: false }, { item: "RAG", status: "missing" }, { skill: "TypeScript", met: true }, { text: "Kubernetes", met: false }];
    expect(gapsFrom(j)).toEqual(["Kubernetes", "RAG"]);
  });
  it("moves through the apply steps", () => {
    const f = (path: string) => ({ path, rel: path, label: path, at: "", exists: true, size: 1, kind: "markdown" as const });
    const ap = (status: "pending" | "done" | "declined") => ({ id: "a", action: "apply_handoff" as const, title: "", summary: "", steps: [], payload: {}, created_at: "", created_by: "app" as const, status, decided_at: "", result_note: "" });
    expect(applyStep("Found", [], null)).toBe("prepare");
    expect(applyStep("Applying", [f("application/PREPARE.md")], null)).toBe("prepare");
    expect(applyStep("Applying", [f("application/resume.md")], null)).toBe("review");
    expect(applyStep("Applying", [f("application/resume.md")], ap("pending"))).toBe("handoff");
    expect(applyStep("Applying", [f("application/resume.md")], ap("declined"))).toBe("review");
    expect(applyStep("Applying", [f("application/resume.md")], ap("done"))).toBe("confirm");
    expect(applyStep("Applied", [], null)).toBe("done");
  });
  it("writes a prompt that names the job folder and forbids submitting", () => {
    const p = buildApplyPrompt("AI Engineer", "Acme Corp", "acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer");
    expect(p).toContain("companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer");
    expect(p).toContain("Do not submit anything");
  });
});
```

```ts
export const JOB_STATUSES = ["Found", "Saved", "Applying", "Applied", "Interview", "Offer", "Rejected", "Skipped"] as const;
export type JobStatus = (typeof JOB_STATUSES)[number];
export const WR_LABELS = ["sponsorship_offered", "visa_ok", "silent", "citizen_pr_only", "clearance"] as const;
export type WorkRightsLabel = (typeof WR_LABELS)[number];
export const WR_STATUSES = ["citizen", "pr", "full_visa", "limited_visa", "needs_sponsorship", "unknown"] as const;
export type WorkRightsStatus = (typeof WR_STATUSES)[number];
export const JOB_SOURCES = ["ats", "adzuna", "browser", "pasted"] as const;
export type JobSource = (typeof JOB_SOURCES)[number];
export const LOCKABLE_DIRS = ["me/identity", "me/employment"] as const;
export type LockableDir = (typeof LOCKABLE_DIRS)[number];
export const ME_DIRS = ["resumes", "history", "identity", "employment", "education", "projects"] as const;
export type MeDir = (typeof ME_DIRS)[number];
export const JOB_SUBDIRS = ["", "application", "emails", "interview"] as const;
export type JobSubdir = (typeof JOB_SUBDIRS)[number];
export const APPROVAL_ACTIONS = ["apply_handoff", "status_change", "email_send"] as const;
export type ApprovalAction = (typeof APPROVAL_ACTIONS)[number];
export const APPROVAL_STATUSES = ["pending", "approved", "declined", "done", "failed"] as const;
export type ApprovalStatus = (typeof APPROVAL_STATUSES)[number];
export const REQUEST_STATUSES = ["pending", "allowed", "denied", "expired"] as const;
export type RequestStatus = (typeof REQUEST_STATUSES)[number];
export const SETTINGS_KINDS = ["profile", "search", "integrations"] as const;
export type SettingsKind = (typeof SETTINGS_KINDS)[number];
export const SECRET_NAMES = ["gmail_app_password", "anthropic_api_key"] as const;
export type SecretName = (typeof SECRET_NAMES)[number];
export const CHANGE_AREAS = ["workspace", "home", "jobs", "inbox", "learn", "sponsors", "searches", "settings", "privacy", "approvals"] as const;
export type ChangeArea = (typeof CHANGE_AREAS)[number];

export const WR_LABEL_TEXT: Record<WorkRightsLabel, string> = {
  sponsorship_offered: "Sponsorship offered", visa_ok: "Open to visa holders", silent: "Ad silent",
  citizen_pr_only: "Citizens/PR only", clearance: "Security clearance",
};

/** Same algorithm as core's slugify(input, "topic"); used to link job gaps to Learn topics. */
export function topicSlug(input: string): string {
  const s = input.normalize("NFKD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").toLowerCase().replace(/&/g, " and ")
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "").slice(0, 60).replace(/-+$/, "");
  return s || "topic";
}
```

```ts
import type {
  ApprovalAction, ApprovalStatus, ChangeArea, JobSource, JobStatus, JobSubdir, LockableDir, MeDir,
  RequestStatus, SecretName, SettingsKind, WorkRightsLabel,
} from "./constants.js";

export interface FieldDef { path: string; label: string; type: string; options: string[]; optionLabels: Record<string, string>; help: string; pattern: string; fields: FieldDef[] }
export interface TimelineEvent { at: string; event: string; note: string }
export interface FileRef { path: string; label: string; at: string }
export interface JobRecord {
  id: string; company_slug: string; client: string | null; title: string; source: JobSource; source_site: string; url: string;
  apply_via: string; posted: string; found_at: string; location: string; work_mode: string; job_type: string;
  salary: { min: number | null; max: number | null; currency: string; period: string; text: string };
  work_rights: { label: WorkRightsLabel; quotes: string[]; decided_by: string };
  scores: {
    fit: { value: number | null; must_haves: unknown[]; nice_to_haves: unknown[] };
    sponsor: { value: number | null; tier: string; reasons: string[] };
    career: { match: number | null; odds: number | null; tier: string; scored_at: string };
  };
  status: JobStatus; timeline: TimelineEvent[]; files: FileRef[];
}
export interface JobRow {
  company_slug: string; id: string; company_name: string; client: string | null; title: string; location: string; work_mode: string;
  salary_text: string; salary_min: number | null; salary_max: number | null; wr_label: WorkRightsLabel; fit: number | null; sponsor: number | null;
  status: JobStatus; source: JobSource; source_site: string; posted: string; found_at: string; eligible: boolean; pinned: boolean; last_activity: string;
}
export interface JobQuery {
  text?: string; statuses?: JobStatus[]; showIneligible?: boolean; labels?: WorkRightsLabel[]; location?: string; modes?: string[];
  salaryMin?: number; sources?: JobSource[]; fitMin?: number; sponsorMin?: number; since?: string;
  sort?: "default" | "fit" | "sponsor" | "newest"; limit?: number; offset?: number;
}
export interface JobList { rows: JobRow[]; total: number; hiddenIneligible: number }
export type FileKind = "markdown" | "text" | "pdf" | "image" | "video" | "other";
export interface JobFile { path: string; rel: string; label: string; at: string; exists: boolean; size: number | null; kind: FileKind }
export interface JobDetail {
  job: JobRecord; company: { name: string; slug: string; website: string; sponsor_status: string } | null;
  description: string; scoreReport: string; files: JobFile[]; gaps: string[]; eligible: boolean;
}
export interface SavedView { name: string; query: JobQuery }
export interface WeekPoint { week: string; found: number; applied: number; replies: number }
export interface NextUpItem {
  kind: "approval" | "request" | "interview" | "prepare" | "inbox"; title: string; subtitle: string;
  company_slug?: string; id?: string; approvalId?: string; requestId?: string; inboxName?: string; wr_label?: WorkRightsLabel; fit?: number | null;
}
export interface HomeSummary {
  name: string; counts: Record<JobStatus, number>; total: number; weekly: WeekPoint[]; sponsorshipMix: Record<WorkRightsLabel, number>;
  visaDaysLeft: number | null; nextUp: NextUpItem[]; pendingApprovals: number; pendingRequests: number; inboxCount: number;
}
export interface WorkspaceSummary { root: string; name: string; dataVersion: number; migrated: number[]; indexSkipped: number }
export interface LegacyPreview { actions: { kind: string; from: string; to: string; note?: string }[]; unmapped: string[]; skipped: string[]; issues: { file: string; problems: string[] }[] }
export type InboxTarget = { kind: "me"; dir: MeDir; sub?: string } | { kind: "job"; company_slug: string; id: string; subdir?: JobSubdir };
export interface InboxSuggestion { target: InboxTarget | null; reason: string; confidence: "high" | "medium" | "low" }
export interface InboxItem { name: string; size: number; suggestion: InboxSuggestion }
export interface LearnItem { path: string; title: string; kind: "guide" | "video" }
export interface LearnTopic { slug: string; title: string; items: LearnItem[] }
export interface InterviewPrep { company_slug: string; id: string; title: string; company_name: string; items: LearnItem[] }
export interface LearnData { topics: LearnTopic[]; interviewPrep: InterviewPrep[] }
export interface SponsorRow { name: string; slug: string | null; status: string; evidence: { date: string; note: string; url: string }[]; last_checked: string; score: number | null; jobs: number }
export interface SearchRow { at: string; site: string; query: string; location: string; results: string; kept: string; notes: string; recent: boolean }
export interface SearchList { rows: SearchRow[]; sites: string[] }
export interface SettingsChange { path: string; value: unknown }
export interface SettingsData { kind: SettingsKind; fields: FieldDef[]; values: Record<string, unknown> }
export interface Grant { folder: LockableDir; until: string; request_id: string }
export interface AccessRequestView { id: string; folder: LockableDir; field: string; why: string; who: string; minutes: number; created_at: string; status: RequestStatus; decided_at: string; expires_at: string }
export interface ActivityEntry { at: string; event: string; detail: string }
export interface PrivacyState { locked: LockableDir[]; grants: Grant[]; requests: AccessRequestView[]; activity: ActivityEntry[] }
export interface ApprovalView {
  id: string; action: ApprovalAction; title: string; summary: string; steps: string[]; payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  created_at: string; created_by: "app" | "claude" | "cli"; status: ApprovalStatus; decided_at: string; result_note: string;
}
export interface ClaimLink { claim: string; source: string }
export interface ApplyState {
  company_slug: string; id: string; title: string; company_name: string; url: string;
  step: "prepare" | "review" | "handoff" | "confirm" | "done"; files: JobFile[]; claims: ClaimLink[] | null; approval: ApprovalView | null; prompt: string;
}
export interface SecretStatus { name: SecretName; available: boolean; set: boolean }
export interface AppInfo { version: string; shell: "electron" | "mock"; platform: string }
export interface ChangeEvent { areas: ChangeArea[] }
/** What the renderer can hand to the shell for a dropped file (a DOM File satisfies this). */
export interface DroppedFile { readonly name: string }
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the validators and the method table**

`packages/contract/src/validate.ts`:

```ts
export class ArgError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) { super(message); this.name = "ArgError"; }
}
export type V<T> = (v: unknown, at: string) => T;

const fail = (at: string, what: string): never => { throw new ArgError(`${at} ${what}`); };
const CTRL = /[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/;
const BANNED_KEYS = new Set(["__proto__", "constructor", "prototype"]);
const isPlain = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v) && Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === Object.prototype;

export const str = (o: { min?: number; max: number; pattern?: RegExp }): V<string> => (v, at) => {
  if (typeof v !== "string") return fail(at, "must be text");
  if (v.includes("\u0000")) fail(at, "contains a NUL character");
  if (v.length < (o.min ?? 0)) fail(at, o.min === 1 ? "is required" : `must be at least ${o.min} characters`);
  if (v.length > o.max) fail(at, `must be at most ${o.max} characters`);
  if (o.pattern && !o.pattern.test(v)) fail(at, "has an invalid format");
  return v;
};
export const int = (min: number, max: number): V<number> => (v, at) =>
  typeof v === "number" && Number.isInteger(v) && v >= min && v <= max ? v : fail(at, `must be a whole number from ${min} to ${max}`);
export const num = (min: number, max: number): V<number> => (v, at) =>
  typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) && v >= min && v <= max ? v : fail(at, `must be a number from ${min} to ${max}`);
export const bool: V<boolean> = (v, at) => (typeof v === "boolean" ? v : fail(at, "must be true or false"));
export const oneOf = <T extends string>(values: readonly T[]): V<T> => (v, at) =>
  typeof v === "string" && (values as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as T) : fail(at, `must be one of: ${values.map((x) => (x === "" ? '""' : x)).join(", ")}`);
export const arr = <T>(of: V<T>, o: { min?: number; max: number }): V<T[]> => (v, at) => {
  if (!Array.isArray(v)) return fail(at, "must be a list");
  if (v.length < (o.min ?? 0)) fail(at, `must have at least ${o.min} item(s)`);
  if (v.length > o.max) fail(at, `must have at most ${o.max} items`);
  return v.map((x, i) => of(x, `${at}[${i}]`));
};
export const opt = <T>(inner: V<T>): V<T | undefined> => (v, at) => (v === undefined ? undefined : inner(v, at));
export function obj<S extends Record<string, V<unknown>>>(shape: S): V<{ [K in keyof S]: ReturnType<S[K]> }> {
  return (v, at) => {
    if (!isPlain(v)) return fail(at, "must be an object");
    for (const k of Object.keys(v)) if (!Object.hasOwn(shape, k) || BANNED_KEYS.has(k)) fail(`${at}.${k}`, "is not allowed");
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(shape)) {
      const r = val(v[k], `${at}.${k}`);
      if (r !== undefined) out[k] = r;
    }
    return out as { [K in keyof S]: ReturnType<S[K]> };
  };
}
/** Any JSON value, bounded in size and depth, with no prototype keys. */
export const json: V<unknown> = (v, at) => {
  const walk = (x: unknown, depth: number, where: string): void => {
    if (depth > 6) fail(where, "is nested too deeply");
    if (x === null || typeof x === "boolean") return;
    if (typeof x === "number") { if (!Number.isFinite(x)) fail(where, "must be a finite number"); return; }
    if (typeof x === "string") { if (x.length > 10_000) fail(where, "is too long"); return; }
    if (Array.isArray(x)) { if (x.length > 200) fail(where, "has too many items"); x.forEach((y, i) => walk(y, depth + 1, `${where}[${i}]`)); return; }
    if (isPlain(x)) {
      const keys = Object.keys(x);
      if (keys.length > 200) fail(where, "has too many keys");
      for (const k of keys) { if (BANNED_KEYS.has(k)) fail(`${where}.${k}`, "is not allowed"); walk(x[k], depth + 1, `${where}.${k}`); }
      return;
    }
    fail(where, "must be plain JSON");
  };
  walk(v, 0, at);
  return v;
};

export const slug = str({ min: 1, max: 120, pattern: /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/ });
export const queueId = str({ min: 1, max: 80, pattern: /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/ });
export const fileName: V<string> = (v, at) => {
  const s = str({ min: 1, max: 255 })(v, at);
  if (CTRL.test(s) || s.includes("/") || s.includes("\\") || s === "." || s === "..") fail(at, "must be a plain file name");
  return s;
};
export const absPath: V<string> = (v, at) => {
  const s = str({ min: 1, max: 4096 })(v, at);
  if (CTRL.test(s)) fail(at, "contains control characters");
  if (!(s.startsWith("/") || /^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]/.test(s) || s.startsWith("\\\\"))) fail(at, "must be an absolute path");
  return s;
};
export const relPath: V<string> = (v, at) => {
  const s = str({ min: 1, max: 1024 })(v, at);
  if (CTRL.test(s) || s.startsWith("/") || s.includes("\\") || /^[A-Za-z]:/.test(s)) fail(at, "must be a workspace-relative path");
  if (s.split("/").some((p) => p === "" || p === "." || p === "..")) fail(at, "must not contain empty, . or .. segments");
  return s;
};
export const settingsPath: V<string> = (v, at) => {
  const s = str({ min: 1, max: 200, pattern: /^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*(\.[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*)*$/ })(v, at);
  if (s.split(".").some((p) => BANNED_KEYS.has(p))) fail(at, "is not allowed");
  return s;
};
```

```ts
import {
  JOB_SOURCES, JOB_STATUSES, JOB_SUBDIRS, LOCKABLE_DIRS, ME_DIRS, SECRET_NAMES, SETTINGS_KINDS, WR_LABELS,
} from "./constants.js";
import type { InboxTarget } from "./types.js";
import {
  absPath, ArgError, arr, bool, fileName, int, json, num, obj, oneOf, opt, queueId, relPath, settingsPath, slug, str, type V,
} from "./validate.js";

const text = (max: number) => str({ max });
const required = (max: number) => str({ min: 1, max });

export const jobQuery = obj({
  text: opt(text(200)), statuses: opt(arr(oneOf(JOB_STATUSES), { max: 8 })), showIneligible: opt(bool),
  labels: opt(arr(oneOf(WR_LABELS), { max: 5 })), location: opt(text(120)), modes: opt(arr(required(20), { max: 5 })),
  salaryMin: opt(num(0, 10_000_000)), sources: opt(arr(oneOf(JOB_SOURCES), { max: 4 })), fitMin: opt(num(0, 100)),
  sponsorMin: opt(num(0, 100)), since: opt(str({ max: 30, pattern: /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/ })),
  sort: opt(oneOf(["default", "fit", "sponsor", "newest"] as const)), limit: opt(int(1, 500)), offset: opt(int(0, 1_000_000)),
});

const meTarget = obj({ kind: oneOf(["me"] as const), dir: oneOf(ME_DIRS), sub: opt(str({ min: 1, max: 200, pattern: /^[A-Za-z0-9 _.-]+(\/[A-Za-z0-9 _.-]+)*$/ })) });
const jobTarget = obj({ kind: oneOf(["job"] as const), company_slug: slug, id: slug, subdir: opt(oneOf(JOB_SUBDIRS)) });
export const inboxTarget: V<InboxTarget> = (v, at) => {
  const kind = typeof v === "object" && v !== null ? (v as { kind?: unknown }).kind : undefined;
  if (kind === "me") return meTarget(v, at) as InboxTarget;
  if (kind === "job") return jobTarget(v, at) as InboxTarget;
  throw new ArgError(`${at}.kind must be "me" or "job"`);
};
const settingsChange = obj({ path: settingsPath, value: json });

/** Every bridge call and its argument validators, in order. The services validate with this table. */
export const METHODS = {
  "app.info": [],
  "workspace.current": [],
  "workspace.pickFolder": [oneOf(["create", "open", "legacy"] as const)],
  "workspace.create": [absPath, required(120)],
  "workspace.open": [absPath],
  "workspace.previewLegacy": [absPath, absPath],
  "workspace.importLegacy": [absPath, absPath],
  "workspace.close": [],
  "home.summary": [],
  "jobs.list": [jobQuery],
  "jobs.get": [slug, slug],
  "jobs.setStatus": [slug, slug, oneOf(JOB_STATUSES), text(500)],
  "jobs.addNote": [slug, slug, required(2000)],
  "jobs.attach": [slug, slug, absPath, required(120), oneOf(JOB_SUBDIRS)],
  "jobs.views": [],
  "jobs.saveView": [required(60), jobQuery],
  "jobs.deleteView": [required(60)],
  "apply.state": [slug, slug],
  "apply.prepare": [slug, slug],
  "apply.requestApproval": [slug, slug],
  "apply.markApplied": [slug, slug],
  "inbox.list": [],
  "inbox.add": [arr(absPath, { min: 1, max: 50 })],
  "inbox.file": [fileName, inboxTarget, text(200)],
  "learn.list": [],
  "files.readText": [relPath],
  "files.reveal": [relPath],
  "files.pick": [],
  "sponsors.list": [],
  "searches.list": [],
  "settings.get": [oneOf(SETTINGS_KINDS)],
  "settings.save": [oneOf(SETTINGS_KINDS), arr(settingsChange, { min: 1, max: 100 })],
  "privacy.get": [],
  "privacy.setLocked": [oneOf(LOCKABLE_DIRS), bool],
  "privacy.allow": [queueId, int(1, 240)],
  "privacy.deny": [queueId],
  "privacy.revoke": [oneOf(LOCKABLE_DIRS)],
  "approvals.list": [],
  "approvals.approve": [queueId],
  "approvals.decline": [queueId, text(500)],
  "secrets.status": [oneOf(SECRET_NAMES)],
  "secrets.set": [oneOf(SECRET_NAMES), required(4096)],
  "secrets.remove": [oneOf(SECRET_NAMES)],
  "links.open": [required(2048)],
  "claude.openDesktop": [],
} as const satisfies Record<string, readonly V<unknown>[]>;

export type MethodName = keyof typeof METHODS;

export function validateArgs(method: string, args: unknown): unknown[] {
  if (typeof method !== "string" || !Object.hasOwn(METHODS, method)) throw new ArgError(`unknown method "${String(method).slice(0, 80)}"`);
  const spec = METHODS[method as MethodName] as readonly V<unknown>[];
  if (!Array.isArray(args)) throw new ArgError(`${method}: arguments must be a list`);
  if (args.length !== spec.length) throw new ArgError(`${method}: expected ${spec.length} argument(s), got ${args.length}`);
  return spec.map((v, i) => v(args[i], `${method} argument ${i + 1}`));
}
```

```ts
import type { JobStatus, JobSubdir, LockableDir, SecretName, SettingsKind } from "./constants.js";
import type { MethodName } from "./methods.js";
import type {
  AppInfo, ApplyState, ApprovalView, ChangeEvent, DroppedFile, HomeSummary, InboxItem, InboxTarget, JobDetail, JobList, JobQuery,
  LearnData, LegacyPreview, PrivacyState, SavedView, SearchList, SecretStatus, SettingsChange, SettingsData, SponsorRow, WorkspaceSummary,
} from "./types.js";

export interface CareerosBridge {
  app: { info(): Promise<AppInfo> };
  workspace: {
    current(): Promise<WorkspaceSummary | null>;
    pickFolder(purpose: "create" | "open" | "legacy"): Promise<string | null>;
    create(dir: string, name: string): Promise<WorkspaceSummary>;
    open(dir: string): Promise<WorkspaceSummary>;
    previewLegacy(src: string, dest: string): Promise<LegacyPreview>;
    importLegacy(src: string, dest: string): Promise<LegacyPreview>;
    close(): Promise<void>;
  };
  home: { summary(): Promise<HomeSummary> };
  jobs: {
    list(q: JobQuery): Promise<JobList>;
    get(slug: string, id: string): Promise<JobDetail>;
    setStatus(slug: string, id: string, status: JobStatus, note: string): Promise<JobDetail>;
    addNote(slug: string, id: string, note: string): Promise<JobDetail>;
    attach(slug: string, id: string, src: string, label: string, subdir: JobSubdir): Promise<JobDetail>;
    views(): Promise<SavedView[]>;
    saveView(name: string, query: JobQuery): Promise<SavedView[]>;
    deleteView(name: string): Promise<SavedView[]>;
  };
  apply: {
    state(slug: string, id: string): Promise<ApplyState>;
    prepare(slug: string, id: string): Promise<ApplyState>;
    requestApproval(slug: string, id: string): Promise<ApplyState>;
    markApplied(slug: string, id: string): Promise<ApplyState>;
  };
  inbox: { list(): Promise<InboxItem[]>; add(paths: string[]): Promise<InboxItem[]>; file(name: string, target: InboxTarget, note: string): Promise<InboxItem[]> };
  learn: { list(): Promise<LearnData> };
  files: { readText(rel: string): Promise<string>; reveal(rel: string): Promise<void>; pick(): Promise<string | null> };
  sponsors: { list(): Promise<SponsorRow[]> };
  searches: { list(): Promise<SearchList> };
  settings: { get(kind: SettingsKind): Promise<SettingsData>; save(kind: SettingsKind, changes: SettingsChange[]): Promise<SettingsData> };
  privacy: {
    get(): Promise<PrivacyState>;
    setLocked(folder: LockableDir, locked: boolean): Promise<PrivacyState>;
    allow(requestId: string, minutes: number): Promise<PrivacyState>;
    deny(requestId: string): Promise<PrivacyState>;
    revoke(folder: LockableDir): Promise<PrivacyState>;
  };
  approvals: { list(): Promise<ApprovalView[]>; approve(id: string): Promise<ApprovalView>; decline(id: string, note: string): Promise<ApprovalView> };
  secrets: { status(name: SecretName): Promise<SecretStatus>; set(name: SecretName, value: string): Promise<SecretStatus>; remove(name: SecretName): Promise<SecretStatus> };
  links: { open(url: string): Promise<boolean> };
  claude: { openDesktop(): Promise<boolean> };
  /** Local (not a call): subscribe to "something changed" pushes. Returns an unsubscribe function. */
  onChange(cb: (e: ChangeEvent) => void): () => void;
  /** Local: the real path of a dropped file, or null when the shell can't tell. */
  pathForFile(file: DroppedFile): string | null;
  /** Local: a careeros-file: URL for showing a workspace file (video, image, PDF). */
  fileUrl(rel: string): string;
}

export interface Transport {
  call(method: MethodName, args: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>;
  onChange(cb: (e: ChangeEvent) => void): () => void;
  pathForFile(file: DroppedFile): string | null;
  fileUrl(rel: string): string;
}

export function createRemoteBridge(t: Transport): CareerosBridge {
  // The transport returns unknown; each method's declared return type is the contract.
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
  const c = (m: MethodName, ...args: unknown[]): Promise<any> => t.call(m, args);
  return {
    app: { info: () => c("app.info") },
    workspace: {
      current: () => c("workspace.current"), pickFolder: (p) => c("workspace.pickFolder", p),
      create: (dir, name) => c("workspace.create", dir, name), open: (dir) => c("workspace.open", dir),
      previewLegacy: (src, dest) => c("workspace.previewLegacy", src, dest), importLegacy: (src, dest) => c("workspace.importLegacy", src, dest),
      close: () => c("workspace.close"),
    },
    home: { summary: () => c("home.summary") },
    jobs: {
      list: (q) => c("jobs.list", q), get: (s, i) => c("jobs.get", s, i), setStatus: (s, i, st, n) => c("jobs.setStatus", s, i, st, n),
      addNote: (s, i, n) => c("jobs.addNote", s, i, n), attach: (s, i, src, l, sub) => c("jobs.attach", s, i, src, l, sub),
      views: () => c("jobs.views"), saveView: (n, q) => c("jobs.saveView", n, q), deleteView: (n) => c("jobs.deleteView", n),
    },
    apply: {
      state: (s, i) => c("apply.state", s, i), prepare: (s, i) => c("apply.prepare", s, i),
      requestApproval: (s, i) => c("apply.requestApproval", s, i), markApplied: (s, i) => c("apply.markApplied", s, i),
    },
    inbox: { list: () => c("inbox.list"), add: (p) => c("inbox.add", p), file: (n, tg, note) => c("inbox.file", n, tg, note) },
    learn: { list: () => c("learn.list") },
    files: { readText: (r) => c("files.readText", r), reveal: (r) => c("files.reveal", r), pick: () => c("files.pick") },
    sponsors: { list: () => c("sponsors.list") },
    searches: { list: () => c("searches.list") },
    settings: { get: (k) => c("settings.get", k), save: (k, ch) => c("settings.save", k, ch) },
    privacy: {
      get: () => c("privacy.get"), setLocked: (f, l) => c("privacy.setLocked", f, l), allow: (id, m) => c("privacy.allow", id, m),
      deny: (id) => c("privacy.deny", id), revoke: (f) => c("privacy.revoke", f),
    },
    approvals: { list: () => c("approvals.list"), approve: (id) => c("approvals.approve", id), decline: (id, n) => c("approvals.decline", id, n) },
    secrets: { status: (n) => c("secrets.status", n), set: (n, v) => c("secrets.set", n, v), remove: (n) => c("secrets.remove", n) },
    links: { open: (u) => c("links.open", u) },
    claude: { openDesktop: () => c("claude.openDesktop") },
    onChange: (cb) => t.onChange(cb),
    pathForFile: (f) => t.pathForFile(f),
    fileUrl: (r) => t.fileUrl(r),
  };
}
```
