- [ ] **Step 7: Write the shared view logic**

`packages/contract/src/logic.ts`:

```ts
import { JOB_STATUSES, WR_LABELS, type JobStatus, type WorkRightsLabel, type WorkRightsStatus } from "./constants.js";
import type {
  AccessRequestView, ApplyState, ApprovalView, FileKind, HomeSummary, InboxItem, JobFile, JobList, JobQuery, JobRecord, JobRow, NextUpItem, WeekPoint,
} from "./types.js";

const DAY = 86_400_000;

/** Same rule as core's isEligible. */
export function isEligible(status: WorkRightsStatus, label: WorkRightsLabel): boolean {
  if (status === "citizen" || status === "pr" || status === "unknown") return true;
  return label !== "citizen_pr_only" && label !== "clearance";
}

export function jobRowFromRecord(j: JobRecord, companyName: string, eligible: boolean): JobRow {
  const last = j.timeline.reduce((m, ev) => (ev.at > m ? ev.at : m), j.found_at);
  return {
    company_slug: j.company_slug, id: j.id, company_name: companyName, client: j.client, title: j.title, location: j.location,
    work_mode: j.work_mode, salary_text: j.salary.text, salary_min: j.salary.min, salary_max: j.salary.max, wr_label: j.work_rights.label,
    fit: j.scores.fit.value, sponsor: j.scores.sponsor.value, status: j.status, source: j.source, source_site: j.source_site,
    posted: j.posted, found_at: j.found_at, eligible, pinned: j.work_rights.label === "sponsorship_offered", last_activity: last,
  };
}

const desc = (a: number | null, b: number | null) => (b ?? -Infinity) - (a ?? -Infinity) || 0;
const SORTS: Record<NonNullable<JobQuery["sort"]>, (a: JobRow, b: JobRow) => number> = {
  default: (a, b) => desc(a.fit, b.fit) || desc(a.sponsor, b.sponsor) || b.found_at.localeCompare(a.found_at),
  fit: (a, b) => desc(a.fit, b.fit),
  sponsor: (a, b) => desc(a.sponsor, b.sponsor),
  newest: (a, b) => b.found_at.localeCompare(a.found_at),
};

/** Filters, hides ineligible ads (unless asked), pins sponsorship-offered ads first, sorts and pages. */
export function applyJobQuery(all: JobRow[], q: JobQuery): JobList {
  const text = q.text?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const loc = q.location?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const modes = q.modes?.map((m) => m.toLowerCase());
  const base = all.filter((r) =>
    (!text || [r.title, r.company_name, r.location, r.client ?? ""].some((s) => s.toLowerCase().includes(text))) &&
    (!q.statuses?.length || q.statuses.includes(r.status)) &&
    (!q.labels?.length || q.labels.includes(r.wr_label)) &&
    (!loc || r.location.toLowerCase().includes(loc)) &&
    (!modes?.length || modes.some((m) => r.work_mode.toLowerCase().includes(m))) &&
    // Unknown salaries stay visible: most ads don't state one.
    (q.salaryMin === undefined || (r.salary_min === null && r.salary_max === null) || (r.salary_max ?? r.salary_min ?? 0) >= q.salaryMin) &&
    (!q.sources?.length || q.sources.includes(r.source)) &&
    (q.fitMin === undefined || (r.fit ?? -1) >= q.fitMin) &&
    (q.sponsorMin === undefined || (r.sponsor ?? -1) >= q.sponsorMin) &&
    (!q.since || r.found_at >= q.since));
  const visible = q.showIneligible ? base : base.filter((r) => r.eligible);
  const sort = SORTS[q.sort ?? "default"];
  const sorted = [...visible].sort((a, b) => Number(b.pinned) - Number(a.pinned) || sort(a, b) || a.company_slug.localeCompare(b.company_slug) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  const offset = q.offset ?? 0;
  return { rows: sorted.slice(offset, offset + (q.limit ?? 200)), total: visible.length, hiddenIneligible: base.length - visible.length };
}

export function visaDaysLeft(expiry: string, now: Date): number | null {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(expiry)) return null;
  const t = Date.parse(`${expiry}T00:00:00.000Z`);
  return Number.isNaN(t) ? null : Math.ceil((t - now.getTime()) / DAY);
}

export function isReply(event: string): boolean {
  return /^Status: (Interview|Offer|Rejected)$/.test(event) || /\b(reply|replied|email received)\b/i.test(event);
}

function mondayUtc(d: Date): Date {
  const x = new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth(), d.getUTCDate()));
  x.setUTCDate(x.getUTCDate() - ((x.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7));
  return x;
}

export function weeklyActivity(events: { at: string; event: string }[], now: Date, weeks = 8): WeekPoint[] {
  const first = mondayUtc(now).getTime() - (weeks - 1) * 7 * DAY;
  const pts: WeekPoint[] = Array.from({ length: weeks }, (_, i) => ({ week: new Date(first + i * 7 * DAY).toISOString().slice(0, 10), found: 0, applied: 0, replies: 0 }));
  for (const e of events) {
    const t = Date.parse(e.at);
    if (Number.isNaN(t)) continue;
    const p = pts[Math.floor((t - first) / (7 * DAY))];
    if (!p || t < first) continue;
    if (e.event === "Found") p.found++;
    else if (e.event === "Status: Applied") p.applied++;
    else if (isReply(e.event)) p.replies++;
  }
  return pts;
}

export function buildNextUp(input: { rows: JobRow[]; approvals: ApprovalView[]; requests: AccessRequestView[]; inbox: InboxItem[] }): NextUpItem[] {
  const out: NextUpItem[] = [];
  for (const a of input.approvals.filter((x) => x.status === "pending")) out.push({ kind: "approval", title: a.title, subtitle: a.summary || "Waiting for your OK", approvalId: a.id });
  for (const r of input.requests.filter((x) => x.status === "pending")) out.push({ kind: "request", title: `Claude asks to read ${r.folder}`, subtitle: r.why, requestId: r.id });
  for (const j of input.rows.filter((r) => r.status === "Interview")) {
    out.push({ kind: "interview", title: j.title, subtitle: j.company_name, company_slug: j.company_slug, id: j.id, wr_label: j.wr_label, fit: j.fit });
  }
  const best = applyJobQuery(input.rows.filter((r) => r.status === "Found" || r.status === "Saved"), { limit: 5 }).rows;
  for (const j of best) out.push({ kind: "prepare", title: j.title, subtitle: j.company_name, company_slug: j.company_slug, id: j.id, wr_label: j.wr_label, fit: j.fit });
  for (const i of input.inbox.slice(0, 3)) out.push({ kind: "inbox", title: i.name, subtitle: i.suggestion.reason, inboxName: i.name });
  return out.slice(0, 10);
}

export function homeSummary(input: {
  name: string; rows: JobRow[]; events: { at: string; event: string }[]; visaExpiry: string;
  approvals: ApprovalView[]; requests: AccessRequestView[]; inbox: InboxItem[]; now: Date;
}): HomeSummary {
  const counts = Object.fromEntries(JOB_STATUSES.map((s) => [s, 0])) as Record<JobStatus, number>;
  const mix = Object.fromEntries(WR_LABELS.map((l) => [l, 0])) as Record<WorkRightsLabel, number>;
  for (const r of input.rows) { counts[r.status]++; mix[r.wr_label]++; }
  return {
    name: input.name, counts, total: input.rows.length, weekly: weeklyActivity(input.events, input.now),
    sponsorshipMix: mix, visaDaysLeft: visaDaysLeft(input.visaExpiry, input.now),
    nextUp: buildNextUp(input), pendingApprovals: input.approvals.filter((a) => a.status === "pending").length,
    pendingRequests: input.requests.filter((r) => r.status === "pending").length, inboxCount: input.inbox.length,
  };
}

export function fileKind(path: string): FileKind {
  const ext = path.toLowerCase().split(".").pop() ?? "";
  if (ext === "md" || ext === "markdown") return "markdown";
  if (ext === "txt" || ext === "eml" || ext === "json") return "text";
  if (ext === "pdf") return "pdf";
  if (["png", "jpg", "jpeg", "gif", "webp"].includes(ext)) return "image";
  if (["mp4", "webm", "m4v", "mov"].includes(ext)) return "video";
  return "other";
}

/**
 * Skill gaps from a job's must-haves. An item counts as a gap when it is an object
 * with `met: false` or a status of "missing" / "gap" / "no"; its text comes from
 * `skill`, `item`, `name` or `text`. Plain strings carry no verdict, so they are skipped.
 */
export function gapsFrom(job: JobRecord): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const m of job.scores.fit.must_haves) {
    if (typeof m !== "object" || m === null) continue;
    const o = m as Record<string, unknown>;
    const missing = o.met === false || ["missing", "gap", "no"].includes(String(o.status ?? "").toLowerCase());
    const label = [o.skill, o.item, o.name, o.text].find((x): x is string => typeof x === "string" && x.trim() !== "");
    if (missing && label && !out.includes(label.trim())) out.push(label.trim());
  }
  return out;
}

const CLOSED: JobStatus[] = ["Applied", "Interview", "Offer", "Rejected"];

/** Where an application stands: prepare -> review -> handoff -> confirm -> done. */
export function applyStep(status: JobStatus, files: JobFile[], approval: ApprovalView | null): ApplyState["step"] {
  if (CLOSED.includes(status)) return "done";
  if (approval?.status === "done") return "confirm";
  if (approval?.status === "pending" || approval?.status === "approved") return "handoff";
  if (files.some((f) => f.exists && !f.path.endsWith("PREPARE.md"))) return "review";
  return "prepare";
}

export function buildApplyPrompt(title: string, company: string, slug: string, id: string): string {
  return [
    `Prepare my application for "${title}" at ${company} (CareerOS job companies/${slug}/jobs/${id}).`,
    "Read jd.md in that folder, and my history in me/history and me/resumes.",
    "Write a tailored resume (application/resume.md), a cover letter (application/cover-letter.md) and answers to any application questions (application/answers.md).",
    "Also write application/claims.json: a list of {\"claim\", \"source\"} that links every claim to the file it came from.",
    "Do not submit anything and do not open locked folders.",
  ].join("\n");
}
```

```ts
import type { AccessRequestView, ApprovalView, FieldDef, JobRecord, LearnItem, LearnTopic, LockableDir, SettingsKind } from "@careeros/contract";

}
```

`packages/app-ui/scripts/make-fixture.ts`:

```ts
// Builds src/mock/alex-fixture.json from the fictional sample workspace.
// The renderer never imports core; only this dev script does.
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import {
  fieldsFor, layout, listAccessRequests, listApprovals, listJobs, readRecord, readSearches, type Company,
} from "../../core/src/index.js";
import type { Fixture, FixtureCompany, FixtureJob } from "../src/mock/fixture-types";

export const SAMPLE = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../../../samples/alex-example");
export const FIXTURE_ROOT = "/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS";
const OUT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../src/mock/alex-fixture.json");

const titleOf = (text: string, fallback: string) => text.match(/^#\s+(.+)$/m)?.[1]?.trim() ?? fallback;

export function buildFixture(root = SAMPLE): Fixture {
  const L = layout(root);
  const rel = (p: string) => path.relative(root, p).split(path.sep).join("/");
  const texts: Record<string, string> = {};
  const read = (p: string) => { const t = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8"); texts[rel(p)] = t; return t; };

  const companies = new Map<string, Company>();
  for (const slug of fs.readdirSync(L.companiesDir).sort()) companies.set(slug, readRecord("company", L.companyFile(slug)));

  const jobs: FixtureJob[] = listJobs(root).map((ref) => {
    const job = readRecord("job", ref.file);
    const c = companies.get(ref.company_slug)!;
    const dir = L.jobDir(ref.company_slug, ref.id);
    const jd = path.join(dir, "jd.md");
    const idir = path.join(dir, "interview");
    const interview = fs.existsSync(idir)
      ? fs.readdirSync(idir).filter((n) => n.endsWith(".md")).sort().map((n) => {
          const p = path.join(idir, n);
          return { path: rel(p), title: titleOf(read(p), n), kind: "guide" as const };
        })
      : [];
    return { job, company: { name: c.name, slug: c.slug, website: c.website, sponsor_status: c.sponsor.status }, description: fs.existsSync(jd) ? read(jd) : "", interview };
  });

  const topics = fs.readdirSync(L.learning).sort().filter((n) => fs.lstatSync(path.join(L.learning, n)).isDirectory()).map((slug) => {
    const dir = path.join(L.learning, slug);
    const items = fs.readdirSync(dir).sort().flatMap((n) => {
      const p = path.join(dir, n);
      if (n.endsWith(".md")) return [{ path: rel(p), title: titleOf(read(p), n), kind: "guide" as const }];
      if (/\.(mp4|webm|m4v)$/i.test(n)) return [{ path: rel(p), title: n, kind: "video" as const }];
      return [];
    });
    return { slug, title: items.find((i) => i.kind === "guide")?.title ?? slug, items };
  });

  const meta = readRecord("workspace", L.workspaceFile);
  const profile = readRecord("profile", L.profile);
  const fixtureCompanies: FixtureCompany[] = [...companies.values()].map((c) => ({
    name: c.name, slug: c.slug,
    sponsor: { status: c.sponsor.status, evidence: c.sponsor.evidence.map((e) => ({ date: e.date, note: e.note, url: e.url })), last_checked: c.sponsor.last_checked },
  }));
  return {
    workspace: { root: FIXTURE_ROOT, name: profile.contact.name, dataVersion: meta.data_version, locked: meta.privacy.locked },
    profile, search: readRecord("search", L.search), integrations: readRecord("integrations", L.integrations),
    fields: { profile: fieldsFor("profile"), search: fieldsFor("search"), integrations: fieldsFor("integrations") },
    jobs, companies: fixtureCompanies, employers: readRecord("sponsors", L.sponsors).employers,
    searches: readSearches(root).entries,
    learning: { topics },
    texts,
    approvals: listApprovals(root).map(({ schema: _s, custom: _c, ...a }) => a),
    requests: listAccessRequests(root).map(({ schema: _s, custom: _c, ...r }) => r),
  };
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  fs.writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(buildFixture(), null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`wrote ${path.relative(process.cwd(), OUT)}`);
}
```

Run: `npm run fixture -w @careeros/app-ui`
Expected: `wrote src/mock/alex-fixture.json`; the file contains `"name": "Alex Example"`, three jobs, one approval and one access request.

`packages/app-ui/test/mock-bridge.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";

describe("MockBridge", () => {
  it("lists jobs with sponsorship pinned and ineligible ads hidden", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const list = await b.jobs.list({});
    expect(list.rows[0]).toMatchObject({ company_name: "Acme Corp", pinned: true, wr_label: "sponsorship_offered" });
    expect(list.rows.map((r) => r.title)).not.toContain("AI Developer");
    expect(list.hiddenIneligible).toBe(1);
    expect((await b.jobs.list({ showIneligible: true })).rows.map((r) => r.title)).toContain("AI Developer");
  });
  it("recomputes eligibility when the profile's work rights change", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    await b.settings.save("profile", [{ path: "work_rights.status", value: "citizen" }]);
    expect((await b.jobs.list({})).hiddenIneligible).toBe(0);
  });
  it("executes an approval only on approve, and only once", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const [a] = await b.approvals.list();
    const slug = a!.payload.company_slug as string;
    const id = a!.payload.id as string;
    expect((await b.jobs.get(slug, id)).job.status).toBe("Found");
    const done = await b.approvals.approve(a!.id);
    expect(done.status).toBe("done");
    expect((await b.jobs.get(slug, id)).job.status).toBe("Interview");
    await expect(b.approvals.approve(a!.id)).rejects.toThrow(/is done; it can't become approved/);
  });
  it("declining changes nothing", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const [a] = await b.approvals.list();
    await b.approvals.decline(a!.id, "Not yet");
    expect((await b.jobs.get(a!.payload.company_slug as string, a!.payload.id as string)).job.status).toBe("Found");
  });
  it("files inbox items and tells listeners", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const seen = vi.fn();
    b.onChange(seen);
    const items = await b.inbox.list();
    expect(items.find((i) => i.name === "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf")?.suggestion.target).toEqual({ kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" });
    await b.inbox.file("Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf", { kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, "");
    expect((await b.inbox.list()).map((i) => i.name)).toEqual(["acme-corp-cover-letter.pdf"]);
    expect(seen).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ areas: ["inbox", "home"] });
  });
  it("allows locked folders once, with an expiry, and refuses to read them otherwise", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    await expect(b.files.readText("me/identity/visa/grant.md")).rejects.toThrow(/locked/);
    const p = await b.privacy.get();
    const req = p.requests.find((r) => r.status === "pending")!;
    const after = await b.privacy.allow(req.id, 15);
    expect(after.grants).toEqual([{ folder: "me/identity", until: "2026-01-15T09:15:00.000Z", request_id: req.id }]);
    expect(after.activity[0]?.event).toBe("Allowed once");
  });
  it("opens only https links", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    expect(await b.links.open("http://jobs.example.com")).toBe(false);
    expect(await b.links.open("https://jobs.example.com/acme/1")).toBe(true);
    expect(b.opened).toEqual(["https://jobs.example.com/acme/1"]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/app-ui/src/mock/MockBridge.ts`**

```ts
import {
  applyJobQuery, applyStep, buildApplyPrompt, fileKind, gapsFrom, homeSummary, isEligible, jobRowFromRecord,
  type AccessRequestView, type ActivityEntry, type AppInfo, type ApplyState, type ApprovalView, type CareerosBridge, type ChangeArea,
  type ChangeEvent, type DroppedFile, type Grant, type InboxItem, type InboxSuggestion, type InboxTarget, type JobDetail, type JobFile,
  type JobQuery, type JobStatus, type JobSubdir, type LearnData, type LegacyPreview, type LockableDir, type PrivacyState, type SavedView,
  type SearchList, type SecretName, type SecretStatus, type SettingsChange, type SettingsData, type SettingsKind, type SponsorRow,
  type WorkRightsStatus, type WorkspaceSummary,
} from "@careeros/contract";
import fixtureJson from "./alex-fixture.json";
import type { Fixture, FixtureJob } from "./fixture-types";

export interface MockOptions { now?: Date; fixture?: Fixture; workspaceOpen?: boolean; inbox?: string[]; secretStoreAvailable?: boolean }

const clone = <T>(v: T): T => (v === undefined ? v : structuredClone(v));
const DEFAULT_INBOX = ["acme-corp-cover-letter.pdf", "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf"];
const DOWNLOADS = "/fictional/alex-example/Downloads";
const basename = (p: string) => p.split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? p;

export function loadFixture(): Fixture {
  return clone(fixtureJson as unknown as Fixture);
}

function getPath(obj: Record<string, unknown>, path: string): unknown {
  return path.split(".").reduce<unknown>((o, k) => (o && typeof o === "object" ? (o as Record<string, unknown>)[k] : undefined), obj);
}
function setPath(obj: Record<string, unknown>, path: string, value: unknown): void {
  const keys = path.split(".");
  let o = obj;
  for (const k of keys.slice(0, -1)) {
    if (typeof o[k] !== "object" || o[k] === null) o[k] = {};
    o = o[k] as Record<string, unknown>;
  }
  o[keys.at(-1)!] = value;
}

export class MockBridge implements CareerosBridge {
  readonly calls: { method: string; args: unknown[] }[] = [];
  readonly opened: string[] = [];
  readonly revealed: string[] = [];
  private f: Fixture;
  private now: Date;
  private isOpen: boolean;
  private inboxNames: string[];
  private views: SavedView[] = [];
  private locked: LockableDir[];
  private grants: Grant[] = [];
  private activity: ActivityEntry[] = [];
  private secretSet = new Set<SecretName>();
  private secretsAvailable: boolean;
  private appFiles = new Map<string, JobFile[]>();
  private listeners = new Set<(e: ChangeEvent) => void>();
  private seq = 0;

  constructor(opts: MockOptions = {}) {
    this.f = clone(opts.fixture ?? (fixtureJson as unknown as Fixture));
    this.now = opts.now ?? new Date("2026-01-15T09:00:00.000Z");
    this.isOpen = opts.workspaceOpen ?? true;
    this.inboxNames = [...(opts.inbox ?? DEFAULT_INBOX)];
    this.locked = [...this.f.workspace.locked];
    this.secretsAvailable = opts.secretStoreAvailable ?? true;
  }

  // ---- helpers -------------------------------------------------------------
  private rec<T>(method: string, args: unknown[], fn: () => T): Promise<T> {
    this.calls.push({ method, args: clone(args) });
    try { return Promise.resolve(clone(fn())); } catch (e) { return Promise.reject(e); }
  }
  private emit(...areas: ChangeArea[]): void { for (const l of [...this.listeners]) l({ areas }); }
  private iso(): string { return this.now.toISOString(); }
  private status(): WorkRightsStatus { return (getPath(this.f.profile, "work_rights.status") as WorkRightsStatus) ?? "unknown"; }
  private rows() { return this.f.jobs.map((j) => jobRowFromRecord(j.job, j.company.name, isEligible(this.status(), j.job.work_rights.label))); }
  private find(slug: string, id: string): FixtureJob {
    const j = this.f.jobs.find((x) => x.job.company_slug === slug && x.job.id === id);
    if (!j) throw new Error(`no job ${slug}/${id}`);
    return j;
  }
  private summary(): WorkspaceSummary {
    return { root: this.f.workspace.root, name: String(getPath(this.f.profile, "contact.name") ?? ""), dataVersion: this.f.workspace.dataVersion, migrated: [], indexSkipped: 0 };
  }
  private event(j: FixtureJob, event: string, note = ""): void { j.job.timeline.push({ at: this.iso(), event, note }); }
  private setJobStatus(j: FixtureJob, status: JobStatus, note: string): void { j.job.status = status; this.event(j, `Status: ${status}`, note); }
  private detail(j: FixtureJob): JobDetail {
    const base = `companies/${j.job.company_slug}/jobs/${j.job.id}`;
    const listed: JobFile[] = j.job.files.map((fr) => ({
      path: fr.path, rel: `${base}/${fr.path}`, label: fr.label, at: fr.at, exists: true,
      size: this.f.texts[`${base}/${fr.path}`]?.length ?? null, kind: fileKind(fr.path),
    }));
    return {
      job: j.job, company: j.company, description: j.description, scoreReport: this.f.texts[`${base}/score.md`] ?? "",
      files: [...listed, ...(this.appFiles.get(`${j.job.company_slug}/${j.job.id}`) ?? [])], gaps: gapsFrom(j.job),
      eligible: isEligible(this.status(), j.job.work_rights.label),
    };
  }
  private suggest(name: string): InboxSuggestion {
    const t = name.toLowerCase().replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/, "").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ");
    if (/\b(visa|grant)\b/.test(t)) return { target: { kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, reason: "The name mentions a visa.", confidence: "high" };
    if (/\bpassport\b/.test(t)) return { target: { kind: "me", dir: "identity" }, reason: "The name looks like an identity document.", confidence: "high" };
    const j = this.f.jobs.find((x) => ` ${t} `.includes(` ${x.job.company_slug.replace(/-/g, " ")} `));
    if (j) {
      const subdir: JobSubdir = /\b(cover|letter|resume|cv|answers)\b/.test(t) ? "application" : "";
      return { target: { kind: "job", company_slug: j.job.company_slug, id: j.job.id, subdir }, reason: `The name mentions ${j.company.name}.`, confidence: "high" };
    }
    if (/\b(cv|resume)\b/.test(t)) return { target: { kind: "me", dir: "resumes" }, reason: "The name looks like a resume.", confidence: "medium" };
    return { target: null, reason: "No rule matched. Choose a folder.", confidence: "low" };
  }
  private inboxItems(): InboxItem[] { return this.inboxNames.map((name) => ({ name, size: 2048, suggestion: this.suggest(name) })); }
  private activeGrants(): Grant[] { return this.grants.filter((g) => g.until > this.iso()); }
  private lockedNow(): LockableDir[] { const open = new Set(this.activeGrants().map((g) => g.folder)); return this.locked.filter((d) => !open.has(d)); }
  private privacyState(): PrivacyState { return { locked: [...this.locked], grants: this.activeGrants(), requests: this.f.requests, activity: [...this.activity].reverse() }; }
  private log(event: string, detail: string): void { this.activity.push({ at: this.iso(), event, detail }); }
  private applyState(slug: string, id: string): ApplyState {
    const j = this.find(slug, id);
    const files = this.appFiles.get(`${slug}/${id}`) ?? [];
    const approval = [...this.f.approvals].reverse().find((a) => a.action === "apply_handoff" && a.payload.company_slug === slug && a.payload.id === id) ?? null;
    const claimsText = this.f.texts[`companies/${slug}/jobs/${id}/application/claims.json`];
    const url = [j.job.apply_via, j.job.url].find((u) => u.startsWith("https://")) ?? "";
    return {
      company_slug: slug, id, title: j.job.title, company_name: j.company.name, url, step: applyStep(j.job.status, files, approval),
      files, claims: claimsText ? JSON.parse(claimsText) : null, approval, prompt: buildApplyPrompt(j.job.title, j.company.name, slug, id),
    };
  }
  private addAppFile(slug: string, id: string, name: string, text: string): void {
    const key = `${slug}/${id}`;
    const rel = `companies/${slug}/jobs/${id}/application/${name}`;
    this.f.texts[rel] = text;
    const list = (this.appFiles.get(key) ?? []).filter((x) => x.path !== `application/${name}`);
    list.push({ path: `application/${name}`, rel, label: name, at: this.iso(), exists: true, size: text.length, kind: fileKind(name) });
    this.appFiles.set(key, list);
  }

  // ---- test hooks ----------------------------------------------------------
  /** Pretend Claude wrote the application files into the job folder. */
  simulateClaudeOutput(slug: string, id: string, withClaims = true): void {
    this.addAppFile(slug, id, "resume.md", "# Alex Example\n\nAI Engineer. Built retrieval pipelines (fictional).\n");
    this.addAppFile(slug, id, "cover-letter.md", "Dear hiring team,\n\nI would like to apply for this role (fictional).\n");
    if (withClaims) this.addAppFile(slug, id, "claims.json", JSON.stringify([{ claim: "Built retrieval pipelines", source: "me/history/roles.md" }]));
    this.emit("jobs");
  }
  emitExternalChange(...areas: ChangeArea[]): void { this.emit(...areas); }

  // ---- bridge --------------------------------------------------------------
  app = { info: () => this.rec("app.info", [], (): AppInfo => ({ version: "0.1.0", shell: "mock", platform: "mock" })) };

  workspace = {
    current: () => this.rec("workspace.current", [], () => (this.isOpen ? this.summary() : null)),
    pickFolder: (purpose: "create" | "open" | "legacy") => this.rec("workspace.pickFolder", [purpose], () =>
      purpose === "legacy" ? "/fictional/alex-example/OldCareerFolder" : "/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS"),
    create: (dir: string, name: string) => this.rec("workspace.create", [dir, name], () => {
      this.isOpen = true; this.f.workspace.root = dir; setPath(this.f.profile, "contact.name", name); this.emit("workspace"); return this.summary();
    }),
    open: (dir: string) => this.rec("workspace.open", [dir], () => { this.isOpen = true; this.f.workspace.root = dir; this.emit("workspace"); return this.summary(); }),
    previewLegacy: (src: string, dest: string) => this.rec("workspace.previewLegacy", [src, dest], (): LegacyPreview => ({
      actions: [
        { kind: "job", from: "Company/Acme Corp", to: "companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer" },
        { kind: "copy", from: "Resume/alex-example-resume.pdf", to: "me/resumes/alex-example-resume.pdf" },
      ],
      unmapped: ["Notes"], skipped: [".DS_Store"], issues: [],
    })),
    importLegacy: (src: string, dest: string) => this.rec("workspace.importLegacy", [src, dest], (): LegacyPreview => {
      this.isOpen = true; this.f.workspace.root = dest; this.emit("workspace");
      return { actions: [{ kind: "job", from: "Company/Acme Corp", to: "companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer" }], unmapped: ["Notes"], skipped: [], issues: [] };
    }),
    close: () => this.rec("workspace.close", [], () => { this.isOpen = false; this.emit("workspace"); }),
  };

  home = {
    summary: () => this.rec("home.summary", [], () => homeSummary({
      name: String(getPath(this.f.profile, "contact.name") ?? ""), rows: this.rows(),
      events: this.f.jobs.flatMap((j) => j.job.timeline), visaExpiry: String(getPath(this.f.profile, "work_rights.visa_expiry") ?? ""),
      approvals: this.f.approvals, requests: this.f.requests, inbox: this.inboxItems(), now: this.now,
    })),
  };

  jobs = {
    list: (q: JobQuery) => this.rec("jobs.list", [q], () => applyJobQuery(this.rows(), q)),
    get: (slug: string, id: string) => this.rec("jobs.get", [slug, id], () => this.detail(this.find(slug, id))),
    setStatus: (slug: string, id: string, status: JobStatus, note: string) => this.rec("jobs.setStatus", [slug, id, status, note], () => {
      const j = this.find(slug, id); this.setJobStatus(j, status, note); this.emit("jobs", "home"); return this.detail(j);
    }),
    addNote: (slug: string, id: string, note: string) => this.rec("jobs.addNote", [slug, id, note], () => {
      const j = this.find(slug, id); this.event(j, "Note", note); this.emit("jobs"); return this.detail(j);
    }),
    attach: (slug: string, id: string, src: string, label: string, subdir: JobSubdir) => this.rec("jobs.attach", [slug, id, src, label, subdir], () => {
      const j = this.find(slug, id);
      j.job.files.push({ path: `${subdir ? `${subdir}/` : ""}${basename(src)}`, label, at: this.iso() });
      this.emit("jobs"); return this.detail(j);
    }),
    views: () => this.rec("jobs.views", [], () => this.views),
    saveView: (name: string, query: JobQuery) => this.rec("jobs.saveView", [name, query], () => {
      this.views = [...this.views.filter((v) => v.name !== name), { name, query }]; return this.views;
    }),
    deleteView: (name: string) => this.rec("jobs.deleteView", [name], () => { this.views = this.views.filter((v) => v.name !== name); return this.views; }),
  };

  apply = {
    state: (slug: string, id: string) => this.rec("apply.state", [slug, id], () => this.applyState(slug, id)),
    prepare: (slug: string, id: string) => this.rec("apply.prepare", [slug, id], () => {
      const j = this.find(slug, id);
      if (!(this.appFiles.get(`${slug}/${id}`) ?? []).some((f) => f.path === "application/PREPARE.md")) {
        this.addAppFile(slug, id, "PREPARE.md", buildApplyPrompt(j.job.title, j.company.name, slug, id));
      }
      if (j.job.status === "Found" || j.job.status === "Saved") this.setJobStatus(j, "Applying", "Preparing the application");
      this.emit("jobs", "home");
      return this.applyState(slug, id);
    }),
    requestApproval: (slug: string, id: string) => this.rec("apply.requestApproval", [slug, id], () => {
      const st = this.applyState(slug, id);
      const files = st.files.filter((f) => !f.path.endsWith("PREPARE.md"));
      if (!files.length) throw new Error("Nothing to hand off yet. Prepare the application first.");
      const a: ApprovalView = {
        id: `20260115t090000-mock${++this.seq}`, action: "apply_handoff", title: `Hand off application to ${st.company_name}`,
        summary: `${files.length} file(s) ready for ${st.title}.`,
        steps: [...(st.url ? [`Open ${st.url} in your browser`] : []), `Show the application folder (${files.length} files)`, 'Add a timeline event "Handed off"'],
        payload: { company_slug: slug, id, url: st.url, files: files.map((f) => f.path) }, created_at: this.iso(), created_by: "app",
        status: "pending", decided_at: "", result_note: "",
      };
      this.f.approvals.push(a);
      this.emit("approvals", "home");
      return this.applyState(slug, id);
    }),
    markApplied: (slug: string, id: string) => this.rec("apply.markApplied", [slug, id], () => {
      this.setJobStatus(this.find(slug, id), "Applied", "Marked applied in CareerOS"); this.emit("jobs", "home"); return this.applyState(slug, id);
    }),
  };

  inbox = {
    list: () => this.rec("inbox.list", [], () => this.inboxItems()),
    add: (paths: string[]) => this.rec("inbox.add", [paths], () => {
      for (const p of paths) {
        let name = basename(p);
        for (let n = 2; this.inboxNames.includes(name); n++) name = basename(p).replace(/(\.[^.]*)?$/, `-${n}$1`);
        this.inboxNames.push(name);
      }
      this.emit("inbox", "home"); return this.inboxItems();
    }),
    file: (name: string, target: InboxTarget, note: string) => this.rec("inbox.file", [name, target, note], () => {
      if (!this.inboxNames.includes(name)) throw new Error(`"${name}" is not in the inbox`);
      if (target.kind === "job") {
        const j = this.find(target.company_slug, target.id);
        j.job.files.push({ path: `${target.subdir ? `${target.subdir}/` : ""}${name}`, label: note || name, at: this.iso() });
      }
      this.inboxNames = this.inboxNames.filter((n) => n !== name);
      this.emit(...(target.kind === "job" ? (["inbox", "home", "jobs"] as ChangeArea[]) : (["inbox", "home"] as ChangeArea[])));
      return this.inboxItems();
    }),
  };

  learn = {
    list: () => this.rec("learn.list", [], (): LearnData => ({
      topics: this.f.learning.topics,
      interviewPrep: this.f.jobs.filter((j) => j.interview.length).map((j) => ({ company_slug: j.job.company_slug, id: j.job.id, title: j.job.title, company_name: j.company.name, items: j.interview })),
    })),
  };

  files = {
    readText: (rel: string) => this.rec("files.readText", [rel], () => {
      const lockedIn = this.lockedNow().find((d) => rel === d || rel.startsWith(`${d}/`));
      if (lockedIn) throw new Error(`${lockedIn} is locked. Allow it once in Privacy first.`);
      const t = this.f.texts[rel];
      if (t === undefined) throw new Error(`no file ${rel}`);
      return t;
    }),
    reveal: (rel: string) => this.rec("files.reveal", [rel], () => { this.revealed.push(rel); }),
    pick: () => this.rec("files.pick", [], () => `${DOWNLOADS}/alex-example-cover-letter.pdf`),
  };

  sponsors = {
    list: () => this.rec("sponsors.list", [], (): SponsorRow[] => {
      const rows: SponsorRow[] = this.f.companies.map((c) => {
        const jobs = this.f.jobs.filter((j) => j.job.company_slug === c.slug);
        const scores = jobs.map((j) => j.job.scores.sponsor.value).filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
        return { name: c.name, slug: c.slug, status: c.sponsor.status, evidence: c.sponsor.evidence, last_checked: c.sponsor.last_checked, score: scores.length ? Math.max(...scores) : null, jobs: jobs.length };
      });
      for (const e of this.f.employers) {
        const name = String(e.name ?? "");
        if (name && !rows.some((r) => r.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
          rows.push({ name, slug: null, status: String(e.status ?? "unknown"), evidence: [], last_checked: String(e.last_checked ?? ""), score: null, jobs: 0 });
        }
      }
      return rows.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }),
  };

  searches = {
    list: () => this.rec("searches.list", [], (): SearchList => {
      const cutoff = new Date(this.now.getTime() - 7 * 86_400_000).toISOString();
      const rows = [...this.f.searches].sort((a, b) => b.at.localeCompare(a.at)).map((s) => ({ ...s, recent: s.at >= cutoff }));
      return { rows, sites: [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.site))].sort() };
    }),
  };

  settings = {
    get: (kind: SettingsKind) => this.rec("settings.get", [kind], (): SettingsData => ({ kind, fields: this.f.fields[kind], values: this.f[kind] })),
    save: (kind: SettingsKind, changes: SettingsChange[]) => this.rec("settings.save", [kind, changes], (): SettingsData => {
      const fields = this.f.fields[kind].filter((x) => x.type !== "object");
      const next = clone(this.f[kind]);
      for (const ch of changes) {
        const field = fields.find((x) => x.path === ch.path);
        if (!field) throw new Error(`can't change ${ch.path}`);
        if (field.pattern && typeof ch.value === "string" && !new RegExp(field.pattern).test(ch.value)) throw new Error(`${field.label}: has an invalid format`);
        setPath(next, ch.path, ch.value);
      }
      this.f[kind] = next;
      this.emit(...(kind === "profile" ? (["settings", "jobs", "home"] as ChangeArea[]) : (["settings"] as ChangeArea[])));
      return { kind, fields: this.f.fields[kind], values: next };
    }),
  };

  privacy = {
    get: () => this.rec("privacy.get", [], () => this.privacyState()),
    setLocked: (folder: LockableDir, locked: boolean) => this.rec("privacy.setLocked", [folder, locked], () => {
      this.locked = (["me/identity", "me/employment"] as LockableDir[]).filter((d) => (d === folder ? locked : this.locked.includes(d)));
      this.grants = this.grants.filter((g) => g.folder !== folder);
      this.log(locked ? "Locked" : "Unlocked", folder);
      this.emit("privacy");
      return this.privacyState();
    }),
    allow: (requestId: string, minutes: number) => this.rec("privacy.allow", [requestId, minutes], () => {
      const r = this.f.requests.find((x) => x.id === requestId);
      if (!r) throw new Error(`no request ${requestId}`);
      if (r.status !== "pending") throw new Error(`request ${requestId} is ${r.status}; it can't become allowed`);
      const until = new Date(this.now.getTime() + minutes * 60_000).toISOString();
      Object.assign(r, { status: "allowed", minutes, expires_at: until, decided_at: this.iso() } satisfies Partial<AccessRequestView>);
      this.grants = [...this.grants.filter((g) => g.folder !== r.folder), { folder: r.folder, until, request_id: r.id }];
      this.log("Allowed once", `${r.folder} for ${minutes} minutes`);
      this.emit("privacy", "home");
      return this.privacyState();
    }),
    deny: (requestId: string) => this.rec("privacy.deny", [requestId], () => {
      const r = this.f.requests.find((x) => x.id === requestId);
      if (!r) throw new Error(`no request ${requestId}`);
      if (r.status !== "pending") throw new Error(`request ${requestId} is ${r.status}; it can't become denied`);
      Object.assign(r, { status: "denied", decided_at: this.iso() });
      this.log("Denied", r.folder);
      this.emit("privacy", "home");
      return this.privacyState();
    }),
    revoke: (folder: LockableDir) => this.rec("privacy.revoke", [folder], () => {
      this.grants = this.grants.filter((g) => g.folder !== folder);
      this.log("Re-locked", folder);
      this.emit("privacy");
      return this.privacyState();
    }),
  };

  approvals = {
    list: () => this.rec("approvals.list", [], () => this.f.approvals),
    approve: (id: string) => this.rec("approvals.approve", [id], () => {
      const a = this.f.approvals.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!a) throw new Error(`no approval ${id}`);
      if (a.status !== "pending") throw new Error(`approval ${id} is ${a.status}; it can't become approved`);
      if (a.action === "email_send") throw new Error("Sending email arrives in a later update.");
      a.status = "approved";
      a.decided_at = this.iso();
      const p = a.payload as { company_slug: string; id: string; status?: JobStatus; note?: string; url?: string };
      const j = this.find(p.company_slug, p.id);
      if (a.action === "status_change") { this.setJobStatus(j, p.status!, p.note ?? ""); a.result_note = `Status set to ${p.status}`; }
      if (a.action === "apply_handoff") {
        if (p.url) this.opened.push(p.url);
        this.revealed.push(`companies/${p.company_slug}/jobs/${p.id}/application`);
        this.event(j, "Handed off", p.url ?? "");
        a.result_note = "Opened the apply page and the files";
      }
      a.status = "done";
      this.emit("approvals", "jobs", "home");
      return a;
    }),
    decline: (id: string, note: string) => this.rec("approvals.decline", [id, note], () => {
      const a = this.f.approvals.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!a) throw new Error(`no approval ${id}`);
      if (a.status !== "pending") throw new Error(`approval ${id} is ${a.status}; it can't become declined`);
      Object.assign(a, { status: "declined", decided_at: this.iso(), result_note: note });
      this.emit("approvals", "home");
      return a;
    }),
  };

  secrets = {
    status: (name: SecretName) => this.rec("secrets.status", [name], (): SecretStatus => ({ name, available: this.secretsAvailable, set: this.secretSet.has(name) })),
    set: (name: SecretName, value: string) => this.rec("secrets.set", [name, value.length ? "<redacted>" : ""], (): SecretStatus => {
      if (!this.secretsAvailable) throw new Error("Secure storage isn't available on this computer, so the secret was not saved.");
      this.secretSet.add(name);
      return { name, available: true, set: true };
    }),
    remove: (name: SecretName) => this.rec("secrets.remove", [name], (): SecretStatus => { this.secretSet.delete(name); return { name, available: this.secretsAvailable, set: false }; }),
  };

  links = {
    open: (url: string) => this.rec("links.open", [url], () => {
      if (!/^https:\/\//i.test(url)) return false;
      this.opened.push(url);
      return true;
    }),
  };

  claude = { openDesktop: () => this.rec("claude.openDesktop", [], () => true) };

  onChange = (cb: (e: ChangeEvent) => void): (() => void) => { this.listeners.add(cb); return () => { this.listeners.delete(cb); }; };
  pathForFile = (file: DroppedFile): string | null => `${DOWNLOADS}/${file.name}`;
  fileUrl = (rel: string): string => `careeros-file://ws/${rel.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/")}`;
}

export function createMockBridge(opts: MockOptions = {}): MockBridge {
  return new MockBridge(opts);
}
```

### Task 16: App shell: bridge context, router, theme, nav rail, command bar, renderer boundary

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/index.html`, `packages/app-ui/src/main.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/App.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/Shell.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/router.ts`, `packages/app-ui/src/bridge-context.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/theme.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/format.ts`, `packages/app-ui/src/components/States.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/styles/app.css`, `packages/app-ui/test/render.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/shell.test.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/boundary.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CareerosBridge`, `createRemoteBridge`, `Transport`, `ChangeArea`, `JobStatus`, `WorkRightsLabel`, `WR_LABEL_TEXT`, `InboxTarget` (contract); `NavRail`, `CommandBar`, `useCommandShortcut`, `ToastProvider`, `useToast`, `PillTone` (brand); `MockBridge`.
- Produces:
  - `type ScreenName = "home" | "jobs" | "job" | "apply" | "inbox" | "learn" | "sponsors" | "searches" | "settings" | "privacy" | "approvals"`
  - `type Route = { screen: "job"; slug: string; id: string; tab?: string } | { screen: "apply"; slug: string; id: string } | { screen: "learn"; topic?: string } | { screen: "jobs"; status?: JobStatus } | { screen: "home" | "inbox" | "sponsors" | "searches" | "settings" | "privacy" | "approvals" }`
  - `parseHash(hash: string): Route`, `toHash(r: Route): string`, `navigate(r: Route): void`, `useRoute(): Route`
  - `BridgeProvider({ bridge, children })`, `useBridge(): CareerosBridge`, `useLoad<T>(load: (b) => Promise<T>, deps: unknown[], areas: ChangeArea[]): { data?: T; error: string | null; loading: boolean; reload(): void }`, `useAction(): { busy: string | null; run<T>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>, ok?: string): Promise<T | undefined> }` (ignores a second run with the same key while the first is in flight), `errorText(e: unknown): string`
  - `type ThemePref = "graphite" | "paper" | "system"`, `ThemeProvider`, `useThemePref(): [ThemePref, (p: ThemePref) => void]`, `resolveTheme(p): "graphite" | "paper"` (stored in `localStorage["careeros.theme"]`)
  - `format.ts`: `fmtDate`, `fmtDateTime`, `fmtTime`, `initials`, `greeting`, `firstName`, `plural`, `bytes`, `wrTone`, `targetText`
  - `States.tsx`: `Loading()`, `ErrorState({ title, text, action? })`, `ScreenHead({ title, sub?, right? })`
  - `screens/registry.tsx`: `SCREENS: Record<ScreenName, (p: { route: Route }) => JSX.Element>`, `OnboardingScreen` (each screen task replaces one entry)
  - `App({ bridge })`; test helper `renderApp(ui, { bridge?, hash? })` returning `{ bridge, ...RTL utils }`

`packages/app-ui/test/render.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render } from "@testing-library/react";
import { ToastProvider } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { ReactElement } from "react";
import { BridgeProvider } from "../src/bridge-context";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { ThemeProvider } from "../src/theme";

export function renderApp(ui: ReactElement, opts: { bridge?: MockBridge; hash?: string } = {}) {
  const bridge = opts.bridge ?? new MockBridge();
  if (opts.hash) window.location.hash = opts.hash;
  const utils = render(<BridgeProvider bridge={bridge}><ThemeProvider><ToastProvider>{ui}</ToastProvider></ThemeProvider></BridgeProvider>);
  return { bridge, ...utils };
}
```

`packages/app-ui/test/shell.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { App } from "../src/App";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { parseHash, toHash } from "../src/router";

describe("router", () => {
  it("round-trips routes and falls back to Home on junk", () => {
    for (const r of [{ screen: "job", slug: "acme-corp", id: "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", tab: "files" }, { screen: "apply", slug: "acme-corp", id: "x" }, { screen: "learn", topic: "rag-pipelines" }, { screen: "jobs", status: "Applying" }, { screen: "privacy" }] as const) {
      expect(parseHash(toHash(r))).toEqual(r);
    }
    expect(parseHash("#/job/../../etc/x")).toEqual({ screen: "home" });
    expect(parseHash("#/%E0%A4%A")).toEqual({ screen: "home" });
    expect(parseHash("#/jobs?status=Ghosted")).toEqual({ screen: "jobs" });
  });
});

describe("App shell", () => {
  it("shows onboarding when no workspace is open", async () => {
    render(<App bridge={new MockBridge({ workspaceOpen: false })} />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /Welcome to CareerOS/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("shows the nav rail with live counts and the connection footer", async () => {
    render(<App bridge={new MockBridge()} />);
    const nav = await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(nav).getByRole("button", { name: /Approvals/ })).toHaveTextContent("1"));
    expect(within(nav).getByRole("button", { name: /Inbox/ })).toHaveTextContent("2");
    expect(within(nav).getByRole("button", { name: /Privacy/ })).toHaveTextContent("Locked");
    expect(within(nav).getByText(/Claude · desktop app/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(nav).getByText(/Mail · coming in 3b/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("navigates from the rail and the command bar", async () => {
    render(<App bridge={new MockBridge()} />);
    const nav = await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByRole("button", { name: /Sponsors/ }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe("#/sponsors"));
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Control>}k{/Control}");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Command" }), "inbox");
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Enter}");
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe("#/inbox"));
  });
  it("finds jobs from the command bar", async () => {
    render(<App bridge={new MockBridge()} />);
    await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Control>}k{/Control}");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Command" }), "backend");
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("option", { name: /Backend Engineer \(LLM\)/ }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe("#/job/example-labs/2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm"));
  });
  it("follows the OS theme on first run and remembers a choice", async () => {
    render(<App bridge={new MockBridge()} />);
    await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("graphite");
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Control>}k{/Control}");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Command" }), "paper");
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Enter}");
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("paper");
    expect(localStorage.getItem("careeros.theme")).toBe("paper");
  });
});
```

`packages/app-ui/test/boundary.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const SRC = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../src");
const walk = (d: string): string[] => fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((e) => (e.isDirectory() ? walk(path.join(d, e.name)) : /\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(e.name) ? [path.join(d, e.name)] : []));

describe("renderer boundary", () => {
  it("never imports Node, Electron or the core library", () => {
    const bad: string[] = [];
    for (const f of walk(SRC)) {
      const text = fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8");
      for (const m of text.matchAll(/(?:from\s+|import\s*\(\s*|require\(\s*)["']([^"']+)["']/g)) {
        const spec = m[1]!;
        if (spec.startsWith("node:") || spec === "electron" || spec.startsWith("electron/") || spec === "@careeros/core" || spec === "@careeros/services" || /^(fs|path|os|child_process)$/.test(spec)) {
          bad.push(`${path.relative(SRC, f)}: ${spec}`);
        }
      }
    }
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`packages/app-ui/src/router.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { JOB_STATUSES, type JobStatus } from "@careeros/contract";

export type ScreenName = "home" | "jobs" | "job" | "apply" | "inbox" | "learn" | "sponsors" | "searches" | "settings" | "privacy" | "approvals";
type Simple = "home" | "inbox" | "sponsors" | "searches" | "settings" | "privacy" | "approvals";
export type Route =
  | { screen: "job"; slug: string; id: string; tab?: string }
  | { screen: "apply"; slug: string; id: string }
  | { screen: "learn"; topic?: string }
  | { screen: "jobs"; status?: JobStatus }
  | { screen: Simple };

const SIMPLE: Simple[] = ["home", "inbox", "sponsors", "searches", "settings", "privacy", "approvals"];
const SLUG = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
const TAB = /^[a-z]{1,20}$/;

export function parseHash(hash: string): Route {
  const [pathPart = "", query = ""] = hash.replace(/^#\/?/, "").split("?");
  let parts: string[];
  try { parts = pathPart.split("/").filter(Boolean).map(decodeURIComponent); } catch { return { screen: "home" }; }
  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
  const [screen, a, b] = parts;
  if ((screen === "job" || screen === "apply") && a && b && SLUG.test(a) && SLUG.test(b) && parts.length === 3) {
    if (screen === "apply") return { screen, slug: a, id: b };
    const tab = params.get("tab");
    return tab && TAB.test(tab) ? { screen, slug: a, id: b, tab } : { screen, slug: a, id: b };
  }
  if (screen === "learn" && parts.length === 1) {
    const topic = params.get("topic");
    return topic && SLUG.test(topic) ? { screen: "learn", topic } : { screen: "learn" };
  }
  if (screen === "jobs" && parts.length === 1) {
    const status = params.get("status");
    return status && (JOB_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status) ? { screen: "jobs", status: status as JobStatus } : { screen: "jobs" };
  }
  if (parts.length === 1 && (SIMPLE as string[]).includes(screen!)) return { screen: screen as Simple };
  return { screen: "home" };
}

export function toHash(r: Route): string {
  switch (r.screen) {
    case "job": return `#/job/${r.slug}/${r.id}${r.tab ? `?tab=${r.tab}` : ""}`;
    case "apply": return `#/apply/${r.slug}/${r.id}`;
    case "learn": return r.topic ? `#/learn?topic=${r.topic}` : "#/learn";
    case "jobs": return r.status ? `#/jobs?status=${r.status}` : "#/jobs";
    default: return `#/${r.screen}`;
  }
}

export function navigate(r: Route): void { window.location.hash = toHash(r); }

export function useRoute(): Route {
  const [hash, setHash] = useState(() => window.location.hash);
  useEffect(() => {
    const on = () => setHash(window.location.hash);
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", on);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", on);
  }, []);
  return useMemo(() => parseHash(hash), [hash]);
}
```

`packages/app-ui/src/bridge-context.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useToast } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { CareerosBridge, ChangeArea } from "@careeros/contract";

const Ctx = createContext<CareerosBridge | null>(null);

export function BridgeProvider({ bridge, children }: { bridge: CareerosBridge; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <Ctx.Provider value={bridge}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export function useBridge(): CareerosBridge {
  const b = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!b) throw new Error("useBridge needs a BridgeProvider");
  return b;
}

/** Strips IPC wrapper text so people see only the plain message. */
export function errorText(e: unknown): string {
  const m = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
  return m.replace(/^Error invoking remote method '[^']+': (?:[A-Za-z]*Error: )?/, "").split("\n")[0]!;
}

export function useLoad<T>(load: (b: CareerosBridge) => Promise<T>, deps: unknown[], areas: ChangeArea[]) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ data?: T; error: string | null; loading: boolean }>({ error: null, loading: true });
  const [tick, setTick] = useState(0);
  const reload = useCallback(() => setTick((t) => t + 1), []);
  const loadRef = useRef(load);
  loadRef.current = load;

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    setState((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true }));
    loadRef.current(bridge).then(
      (data) => { if (live) setState({ data, error: null, loading: false }); },
      (e: unknown) => { if (live) setState((s) => ({ data: s.data, error: errorText(e), loading: false })); },
    );
    return () => { live = false; };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [bridge, tick, ...deps]);

  const key = areas.join(",");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!key) return undefined;
    const wanted = key.split(",");
    return bridge.onChange((e) => { if (e.areas.some((a) => wanted.includes(a))) reload(); });
  }, [bridge, key, reload]);

  return { data: state.data, error: state.error, loading: state.loading, reload };
}

/** Runs one mutation per key at a time and reports the result as a toast. */
export function useAction() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const inflight = useRef(new Set<string>());
  const run = useCallback(async <T,>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>, ok?: string): Promise<T | undefined> => {
    if (inflight.current.has(key)) return undefined;
    inflight.current.add(key);
    setBusy(key);
    try {
      const r = await fn();
      if (ok) toast({ text: ok, tone: "success" });
      return r;
    } catch (e) {
      toast({ text: errorText(e), tone: "danger" });
      return undefined;
    } finally {
      inflight.current.delete(key);
      setBusy(null);
    }
  }, [toast]);
  return { busy, run };
}
```

`packages/app-ui/src/theme.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

export type ThemePref = "graphite" | "paper" | "system";
const KEY = "careeros.theme";

export function resolveTheme(pref: ThemePref): "graphite" | "paper" {
  if (pref !== "system") return pref;
  return window.matchMedia?.("(prefers-color-scheme: light)")?.matches ? "paper" : "graphite";
}

const Ctx = createContext<[ThemePref, (p: ThemePref) => void]>(["system", () => undefined]);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [pref, setPref] = useState<ThemePref>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    return saved === "graphite" || saved === "paper" || saved === "system" ? saved : "system";
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    const apply = () => { document.documentElement.dataset.theme = resolveTheme(pref); };
    apply();
    const mq = window.matchMedia?.("(prefers-color-scheme: light)");
    if (pref !== "system" || !mq) return undefined;
    mq.addEventListener("change", apply);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener("change", apply);
  }, [pref]);
  const set = useCallback((p: ThemePref) => { localStorage.setItem(KEY, p); setPref(p); }, []);
  return <Ctx.Provider value={[pref, set]}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export const useThemePref = () => useContext(Ctx);
```

```ts
import type { PillTone } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { InboxTarget, WorkRightsLabel } from "@careeros/contract";

const valid = (iso: string) => !!iso && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(iso));
export const fmtDate = (iso: string) => (valid(iso) ? new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("en-AU", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" }) : "");
export const fmtDateTime = (iso: string) => (valid(iso) ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString("en-AU", { day: "numeric", month: "short", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }) : "");
export const fmtTime = (iso: string) => (valid(iso) ? new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString("en-AU", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }) : "");
export const initials = (name: string) => name.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2).map((w) => w[0]!.toUpperCase()).join("");
export const greeting = (d: Date) => (d.getHours() < 12 ? "Good morning" : d.getHours() < 18 ? "Good afternoon" : "Good evening");
export const firstName = (name: string) => name.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] ?? "";
export const plural = (n: number, one: string, many = `${one}s`) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
export const bytes = (n: number) => (n < 1024 ? `${n} B` : n < 1_048_576 ? `${Math.round(n / 1024)} KB` : `${(n / 1_048_576).toFixed(1)} MB`);
export const wrTone = (l: WorkRightsLabel): PillTone =>
  ({ sponsorship_offered: "success", visa_ok: "accent", silent: "neutral", citizen_pr_only: "danger", clearance: "danger" } as const)[l];
export function targetText(t: InboxTarget | null, jobTitle?: string): string {
  if (!t) return "Choose a folder";
  if (t.kind === "me") return `me/${t.dir}${t.sub ? `/${t.sub}` : ""}`;
  return `${jobTitle ?? `${t.company_slug}/${t.id}`}${t.subdir ? ` · ${t.subdir}` : ""}`;
}
```

```tsx
}
```

```tsx
import type { JSX } from "react";
import { Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { Route, ScreenName } from "../router";

// Each screen task replaces its entry with the real screen.
function Pending({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  return <Text role="heading-1">{route.screen}</Text>;
}

export function OnboardingScreen({ onDone: _onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  return <Text role="display">Welcome to CareerOS</Text>;
}

export const SCREENS: Record<ScreenName, (p: { route: Route }) => JSX.Element> = {
  home: Pending, jobs: Pending, job: Pending, apply: Pending, inbox: Pending, learn: Pending,
  sponsors: Pending, searches: Pending, settings: Pending, privacy: Pending, approvals: Pending,
};
```

`packages/app-ui/src/Shell.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Briefcase, CircleCheck, GraduationCap, House, Inbox, Lock, Search, Send, Settings, Star } from "lucide-react";
import { CommandBar, NavRail, useCommandShortcut, type CommandItem, type NavRailItem } from "@careeros/brand";
import { useLoad } from "./bridge-context";
import { navigate, type Route } from "./router";
import { useThemePref } from "./theme";

const SCREEN_LABELS: [Route, string][] = [
  [{ screen: "home" }, "Home"], [{ screen: "jobs" }, "Jobs"], [{ screen: "inbox" }, "Inbox"], [{ screen: "learn" }, "Learn"],
  [{ screen: "sponsors" }, "Sponsors"], [{ screen: "searches" }, "Searches"], [{ screen: "approvals" }, "Approvals"],
  [{ screen: "privacy" }, "Privacy"], [{ screen: "settings" }, "Settings"],
];

export function Shell({ route, children }: { route: Route; children: ReactNode }) {
  const counts = useLoad(async (b) => {
    const [home, privacy] = await Promise.all([b.home.summary(), b.privacy.get()]);
    return { home, privacy };
  }, [], ["home", "jobs", "inbox", "approvals", "privacy", "settings"]);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const openCommand = useCallback(() => { setQuery(""); setOpen(true); }, []);
  useCommandShortcut(openCommand);
  const [, setTheme] = useThemePref();
  const hits = useLoad((b) => (query.trim().length >= 2 ? b.jobs.list({ text: query.trim(), limit: 6, showIneligible: true }) : Promise.resolve(null)), [query], []);

  const h = counts.data?.home;
  const locked = counts.data?.privacy.locked.length ?? 0;
  const current = route.screen === "job" ? "jobs" : route.screen === "apply" || (route.screen === "jobs" && route.status === "Applying") ? "apply" : route.screen;
  const items: NavRailItem[] = [
    { id: "home", label: "Home", icon: House },
    { id: "jobs", label: "Jobs", icon: Briefcase, count: h?.total },
    { id: "apply", label: "Apply", icon: Send, count: h?.counts.Applying },
    { id: "inbox", label: "Inbox", icon: Inbox, count: h?.inboxCount },
    { id: "learn", label: "Learn", icon: GraduationCap },
    { id: "sponsors", label: "Sponsors", icon: Star },
    { id: "searches", label: "Searches", icon: Search },
    { id: "approvals", label: "Approvals", icon: CircleCheck, count: h?.pendingApprovals, section: "secondary" },
    { id: "privacy", label: "Privacy", icon: Lock, trailing: locked ? "Locked" : "Open", section: "secondary" },
    { id: "settings", label: "Settings", icon: Settings, section: "secondary" },
  ];
  const onNavigate = (id: string) => navigate(id === "apply" ? { screen: "jobs", status: "Applying" } : ({ screen: id } as Route));

  const commands = useMemo<CommandItem[]>(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const screens: CommandItem[] = SCREEN_LABELS.map(([r, label]) => ({ id: `go-${label}`, label: `Go to ${label}`, group: "Go to", run: () => navigate(r) }));
    const actions: CommandItem[] = [
      { id: "theme-graphite", label: "Use the Graphite (dark) theme", group: "Theme", run: () => setTheme("graphite") },
      { id: "theme-paper", label: "Use the Paper (light) theme", group: "Theme", run: () => setTheme("paper") },
      { id: "theme-system", label: "Follow the system theme", group: "Theme", run: () => setTheme("system") },
    ];
    const jobs: CommandItem[] = (hits.data?.rows ?? []).map((r) => ({
      id: `job-${r.company_slug}-${r.id}`, label: `${r.title} · ${r.company_name}`, group: "Job", run: () => navigate({ screen: "job", slug: r.company_slug, id: r.id }),
    }));
    return [...jobs, ...[...screens, ...actions].filter((c) => !q || c.label.toLowerCase().includes(q))];
  }, [query, hits.data, setTheme]);

  const isMac = typeof navigator !== "undefined" && /Mac/i.test(navigator.platform);
  return (
    <div className="app">
      <NavRail
        items={items} current={current} onNavigate={onNavigate} onOpenCommand={openCommand} shortcut={isMac ? "⌘K" : "Ctrl K"}
        footer={<><div><span className="status-dot" />Claude · desktop app</div><div className="t-muted"><span className="status-dot status-dot--off" />Mail · coming in 3b</div></>}
      />
      <main className="app__main" id="main">{children}</main>
      <CommandBar open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} items={commands} query={query} onQueryChange={setQuery} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`packages/app-ui/src/App.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ToastProvider } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { CareerosBridge } from "@careeros/contract";
import { BridgeProvider, useLoad } from "./bridge-context";
import { Loading } from "./components/States";
import { useRoute } from "./router";
import { OnboardingScreen, SCREENS } from "./screens/registry";
import { Shell } from "./Shell";
import { ThemeProvider } from "./theme";

function Gate() {
  const ws = useLoad((b) => b.workspace.current(), [], ["workspace"]);
  const route = useRoute();
  if (ws.loading && ws.data === undefined) return <div className="app-loading"><Loading /></div>;
  if (!ws.data) return <OnboardingScreen onDone={ws.reload} />;
  const Screen = SCREENS[route.screen];
  return <Shell route={route}><Screen route={route} /></Shell>;
}

export function App({ bridge }: { bridge: CareerosBridge }) {
  return (
    <BridgeProvider bridge={bridge}>
      <ThemeProvider>
        <ToastProvider><Gate /></ToastProvider>
      </ThemeProvider>
    </BridgeProvider>
  );
}
```

`packages/app-ui/src/main.tsx`:

```tsx
import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import "@careeros/brand/styles.css";
import "./styles/app.css";
import { createRemoteBridge, type CareerosBridge, type Transport } from "@careeros/contract";
import { App } from "./App";

declare global {
  interface Window { careerosTransport?: Transport }
}

async function pickBridge(): Promise<CareerosBridge> {
  // In the desktop app the preload exposes the transport; in `npm run dev` we use the fictional mock.
  if (window.careerosTransport) return createRemoteBridge(window.careerosTransport);
  const { createMockBridge } = await import("./mock/MockBridge");
  return createMockBridge();
}

void pickBridge().then((bridge) => {
  createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(<StrictMode><App bridge={bridge} /></StrictMode>);
});
```

- Produces: `OnboardingScreen({ onCreated, onDone }: { onCreated: () => void; onDone: () => void })`; `WR_STATUS_TEXT: Record<WorkRightsStatus, string>` in `format.ts`.

Ruling: the legacy import always shows the dry-run preview first; nothing is written until the person presses Import. After Create, the screen stays up for two short steps (work rights, targets); both can be skipped.

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { App } from "../src/App";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";

const fresh = () => new MockBridge({ workspaceOpen: false });
const called = (b: MockBridge, m: string) => b.calls.filter((c) => c.method === m);

describe("Onboarding", () => {
  it("creates a workspace, then asks for work rights and targets", async () => {
    const b = fresh();
    render(<App bridge={b} />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Create a new workspace/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Your name"), "Alex Example");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Create workspace" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Your work rights" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("Work rights"), "needs_sponsorship");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Visa expiry date"), "2028-06-30");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Next" }));
    await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText("Roles you want"), "AI Engineer, Backend Engineer");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Finish" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(called(b, "workspace.create")[0]?.args).toEqual(["/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS", "Alex Example"]);
    expect(called(b, "settings.save").map((c) => c.args)).toEqual([
      ["profile", [{ path: "work_rights.status", value: "needs_sponsorship" }, { path: "work_rights.visa_expiry", value: "2028-06-30" }]],
      ["profile", [{ path: "targets.roles", value: ["AI Engineer", "Backend Engineer"] }]],
    ]);
  });

  it("needs a name before creating", async () => {
    render(<App bridge={fresh()} />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Create a new workspace/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Create workspace" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("opens an existing workspace", async () => {
    const b = fresh();
    render(<App bridge={b} />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Open a workspace/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(called(b, "workspace.open")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("previews an old career folder before importing anything", async () => {
    const b = fresh();
    render(<App bridge={b} />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Import an old career folder/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("companies/acme-corp/jobs/2026-01-12-ai-engineer")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Notes")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(called(b, "workspace.importLegacy")).toHaveLength(0);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Import" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(called(b, "workspace.importLegacy")).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Your work rights" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Onboarding.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { FolderOpen, FolderPlus, History } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, Select, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import { WR_STATUSES, type LegacyPreview, type WorkRightsStatus } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge } from "../bridge-context";
import { WR_STATUS_TEXT } from "../format";

type Step =
  | { name: "start" }
  | { name: "create"; dir: string }
  | { name: "legacy"; src: string; dest: string; preview: LegacyPreview }
  | { name: "rights" }
  | { name: "targets" };

export function OnboardingScreen({ onCreated, onDone }: { onCreated: () => void; onDone: () => void }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>({ name: "start" });
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<WorkRightsStatus>("unknown");
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState("");
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState("");

  const startCreate = () => run("pick", async () => {
    const dir = await bridge.workspace.pickFolder("create");
    if (dir) setStep({ name: "create", dir });
  });
  const create = (dir: string) => run("create", async () => {
    onCreated();
    await bridge.workspace.create(dir, name.trim());
    setStep({ name: "rights" });
  });
  const open = () => run("open", async () => {
    const dir = await bridge.workspace.pickFolder("open");
    if (!dir) return;
    await bridge.workspace.open(dir);
    onDone();
  });
  const startLegacy = () => run("legacy", async () => {
    const src = await bridge.workspace.pickFolder("legacy");
    if (!src) return;
    const dest = await bridge.workspace.pickFolder("create");
    if (!dest) return;
    setStep({ name: "legacy", src, dest, preview: await bridge.workspace.previewLegacy(src, dest) });
  });
  const doImport = (src: string, dest: string) => run("import", async () => {
    onCreated();
    await bridge.workspace.importLegacy(src, dest);
    setStep({ name: "rights" });
  }, "Imported your old folder");
  const saveRights = () => run("rights", async () => {
    await bridge.settings.save("profile", [{ path: "work_rights.status", value: status }, { path: "work_rights.visa_expiry", value: expiry }]);
    setStep({ name: "targets" });
  });
  const finish = () => run("targets", async () => {
    const list = roles.split(",").map((r) => r.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (list.length) await bridge.settings.save("profile", [{ path: "targets.roles", value: list }]);
    onDone();
  });

  return (
    <main className="onboarding app__main">
      <Text role="display">Welcome to CareerOS</Text>
      <Text role="body" muted>Your job search lives in one folder on this computer. Nothing leaves it unless you say so.</Text>

      {step.name === "start" ? (
        <div className="split">
          <Card className="stack">
            <Button variant="primary" icon={FolderPlus} loading={busy === "pick" || busy === "create"} onClick={startCreate}>Create a new workspace</Button>
            <Button icon={FolderOpen} loading={busy === "open"} onClick={open}>Open a workspace</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" icon={History} loading={busy === "legacy"} onClick={startLegacy}>Import an old career folder</Button>
          </Card>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {step.name === "create" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="heading-2">Create your workspace</Text>
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>Folder</Text>
          <Text role="body" truncate>{step.dir}</Text>
          <Input label="Your name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} autoFocus />
          <div className="row">
            <Button variant="primary" disabled={!name.trim()} loading={busy === "create"} onClick={() => void create(step.dir)}>Create workspace</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setStep({ name: "start" })}>Back</Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {step.name === "legacy" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="heading-2">Check the import</Text>
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>Nothing has been copied yet. This is what will happen.</Text>
          <ul className="list-plain">
            {step.preview.actions.map((a) => (
              <li key={`${a.from}->${a.to}`} className="row"><span className="truncate grow" title={a.from}>{a.from}</span><span>→</span><span className="truncate grow">{a.to}</span></li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          {step.preview.unmapped.length ? (
            <>
              <Text role="label">Left where it is</Text>
              <ul className="list-plain">{step.preview.unmapped.map((u) => <li key={u}>{u}</li>)}</ul>
            </>
          ) : null}
          {step.preview.issues.length ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>{step.preview.issues.length} file(s) have problems and will be skipped.</Text> : null}
          <div className="row">
            <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "import"} onClick={() => void doImport(step.src, step.dest)}>Import</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setStep({ name: "start" })}>Cancel</Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {step.name === "rights" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="heading-2">Your work rights</Text>
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>CareerOS hides ads you can't apply for and pins ads that offer sponsorship.</Text>
          <Select label="Work rights" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value as WorkRightsStatus)}
            options={WR_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: WR_STATUS_TEXT[s] }))} />
          <Input label="Visa expiry date" type="date" value={expiry} onChange={(e) => setExpiry(e.target.value)} help="Leave empty if it doesn't apply." />
          <div className="row">
            <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "rights"} onClick={() => void saveRights()}>Next</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setStep({ name: "targets" })}>Skip</Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {step.name === "targets" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="heading-2">What are you looking for?</Text>
          <Input label="Roles you want" value={roles} onChange={(e) => setRoles(e.target.value)} help="Separate roles with commas." />
          <div className="row">
            <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "targets"} onClick={() => void finish()}>Finish</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>Skip</Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}
    </main>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Wire it in**

In `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`, delete the `OnboardingScreen` stub function and add `export { OnboardingScreen } from "./Onboarding";`.

Replace `Gate` in `packages/app-ui/src/App.tsx` (add `useState` to a `react` import):

```tsx
function Gate() {
  const ws = useLoad((b) => b.workspace.current(), [], ["workspace"]);
  const route = useRoute();
  // While setup runs, the workspace already exists but the remaining steps must stay on screen.
  const [setup, setSetup] = useState(false);
  if (ws.loading && ws.data === undefined) return <div className="app-loading"><Loading /></div>;
  if (!ws.data || setup) return <OnboardingScreen onCreated={() => setSetup(true)} onDone={() => { setSetup(false); ws.reload(); }} />;
  const Screen = SCREENS[route.screen];
  return <Shell route={route}><Screen route={route} /></Shell>;
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge, loadFixture } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { HomeScreen } from "../src/screens/Home";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

describe("Home", () => {
  it("greets by first name and counts the visa days", async () => {
    renderApp(<HomeScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /, Alex\.$/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("897")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("days left on visa")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("says so when no visa date is set", async () => {
    const f = loadFixture();
    (f.profile.work_rights as Record<string, unknown>).visa_expiry = "";

});
```
