- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Home.tsx`**

```tsx
import { Button, Card, KpiCard, Pill, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import { WR_LABELS, WR_LABEL_TEXT, type HomeSummary, type NextUpItem } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading } from "../components/States";
import { firstName, greeting, initials, plural, wrTone } from "../format";
import { navigate, type Route } from "../router";

export function nextAction(item: NextUpItem): { label: string; route: Route } {
  switch (item.kind) {
    case "approval": return { label: "Review", route: { screen: "approvals" } };
    case "request": return { label: "Decide", route: { screen: "privacy" } };
    case "interview": return { label: "Practise", route: { screen: "learn" } };
    case "prepare": return { label: "Prepare", route: { screen: "apply", slug: item.company_slug!, id: item.id! } };
    case "inbox": return { label: "File it", route: { screen: "inbox" } };
  }
}

const KIND_TEXT: Record<NextUpItem["kind"], string> = { approval: "Approval", request: "Access request", interview: "Interview", prepare: "Ready to prepare", inbox: "Inbox" };

function waitingText(h: HomeSummary): string {
  const parts = [h.pendingApprovals ? plural(h.pendingApprovals, "approval") : "", h.pendingRequests ? plural(h.pendingRequests, "access request") : ""].filter(Boolean);
  return `${parts.join(" and ")} waiting for your OK.`;
}

export function HomeScreen() {
  const { data: h, error } = useLoad((b) => b.home.summary(), [], ["home", "jobs", "inbox", "approvals", "privacy", "settings"]);
  if (error && !h) return <ErrorState title="Home can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!h) return <Loading />;

  const applied = h.counts.Applied + h.counts.Interview + h.counts.Offer + h.counts.Rejected;
  const interviews = h.counts.Interview + h.counts.Offer;
  const funnel: [string, number][] = [[`${h.total} found`, h.total], [`${applied} applied`, applied], [`${interviews} interview`, interviews], [`${h.counts.Offer} offer`, h.counts.Offer]];
  const pct = (n: number) => `${h.total ? Math.round((n / h.total) * 100) : 0}%`;
  const replies = h.weekly.reduce((s, w) => s + w.replies, 0);

  return (
    <>
      <header className="screen-head">
        <div>
          <Text role="display">{greeting(new Date())}, <em>{firstName(h.name)}.</em></Text>
          <Text role="body" muted className="screen-head__sub">
            {plural(h.sponsorshipMix.sponsorship_offered, "job")} with sponsorship offered · {plural(h.inboxCount, "file")} in your inbox
          </Text>
        </div>
        <div className="visa">
          {h.visaDaysLeft === null ? <Text role="body" muted>No visa date set</Text> : (
            <><Text role="hero" as="div">{h.visaDaysLeft}</Text><Text role="label" as="div">days left on visa</Text></>
          )}
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="grid-kpi">
        <KpiCard label="Jobs" value={h.total} trend={h.weekly.map((w) => w.found)} delta={`+${h.weekly.at(-1)?.found ?? 0} this week`} />
        <KpiCard label="Applying" value={h.counts.Applying} />
        <KpiCard label="Applied" value={applied} tone="accent" trend={h.weekly.map((w) => w.applied)} />
        <KpiCard label="Replies" value={replies} trend={h.weekly.map((w) => w.replies)} />
        <KpiCard label="Offers" value={h.counts.Offer} />
      </div>

      <div className="cols">
        <Card aria-label="Next up section">
          <div className="row row--between"><Text role="heading-2">Next up</Text><Text role="label">Sponsorship first, then fit</Text></div>
          {h.nextUp.length === 0 ? <Text role="body" muted>Nothing waiting. Add jobs from the CLI or ask Claude to run a search.</Text> : (
            <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="Next up">
              {h.nextUp.map((item, i) => {
                const act = nextAction(item);
                return (
                  <li key={`${item.kind}-${i}`} className="next-line">
                    <span className="avatar" aria-hidden="true">{initials(item.subtitle && item.kind !== "request" && item.kind !== "approval" ? item.subtitle : item.title)}</span>
                    <span className="grow">
                      <Text role="body" truncate as="div">{item.title}</Text>
                      <Text role="body-sm" muted truncate as="div">{item.subtitle}</Text>
                    </span>
                    {item.wr_label ? <Pill tone={wrTone(item.wr_label)}>{WR_LABEL_TEXT[item.wr_label]}</Pill> : <Pill>{KIND_TEXT[item.kind]}</Pill>}
                    <Text role="numeric">{item.fit ?? ""}</Text>
                    <Button size="sm" variant={i === 0 ? "primary" : "ghost"} aria-label={`${act.label} ${item.title}`} onClick={() => navigate(act.route)}>{act.label}</Button>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </Card>

        <div className="stack">
          {h.pendingApprovals + h.pendingRequests > 0 ? (
            <Card highlight aria-label="Waiting for you">
              <Text role="label">Waiting for you</Text>
              <Text role="body">{waitingText(h)}</Text>
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => navigate(h.pendingApprovals ? { screen: "approvals" } : { screen: "privacy" })}>Review</Button>
            </Card>
          ) : null}
          <Card aria-label="Pipeline">
            <Text role="heading-2">Pipeline</Text>
            <div className="funnel">
              {funnel.map(([label, n]) => (
                <div key={label} className="funnel__row">
                  <div className="bar-track"><div className="bar-fill" style={{ width: pct(n) }} /></div>
                  <Text role="body-sm" muted>{label}</Text>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Card>
          <Card aria-label="Sponsorship">
            <Text role="heading-2">Sponsorship</Text>
            <ul className="list-plain">
              {WR_LABELS.filter((l) => h.sponsorshipMix[l] > 0).map((l) => (
                <li key={l} className="row row--between"><Pill tone={wrTone(l)}>{WR_LABEL_TEXT[l]}</Pill><Text role="numeric">{h.sponsorshipMix[l]}</Text></li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

The inline `style={{ width }}` carries a percentage, not a colour or font, so the brand lint allows it.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Jobs.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button, Checkbox, Dialog, Input, Pill, Select, Table, Text, type Column } from "@careeros/brand";
import { JOB_STATUSES, WR_LABELS, WR_LABEL_TEXT, type JobQuery, type JobRow, type JobStatus, type WorkRightsLabel } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { wrTone } from "../format";
import { navigate, type Route } from "../router";

export interface JobsForm { text: string; status: "" | JobStatus; label: "" | WorkRightsLabel; fitMin: string; sort: NonNullable<JobQuery["sort"]>; showIneligible: boolean }

export function queryFromForm(f: JobsForm): JobQuery {
  const q: JobQuery = {};
  if (f.text.trim()) q.text = f.text.trim();
  if (f.status) q.statuses = [f.status];
  if (f.label) q.labels = [f.label];
  if (f.fitMin.trim() && Number.isFinite(Number(f.fitMin))) q.fitMin = Number(f.fitMin);
  if (f.showIneligible) q.showIneligible = true;
  q.sort = f.sort;
  q.limit = 200;
  return q;
}

function formFromQuery(q: JobQuery): JobsForm {
  return {
    text: q.text ?? "", status: q.statuses?.[0] ?? "", label: q.labels?.[0] ?? "", fitMin: q.fitMin === undefined ? "" : String(q.fitMin),
    sort: q.sort ?? "default", showIneligible: q.showIneligible ?? false,
  };
}

const COLUMNS: Column<JobRow>[] = [
  { key: "role", header: "Role", render: (r) => (<div><Text role="body" truncate as="div">{r.title}</Text><Text role="body-sm" muted truncate as="div">{r.client ? `${r.company_name} · client ${r.client}` : r.company_name}</Text></div>) },
  { key: "rights", header: "Work rights", width: "11rem", render: (r) => <Pill tone={wrTone(r.wr_label)}>{WR_LABEL_TEXT[r.wr_label]}</Pill> },
  { key: "where", header: "Location", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm" truncate>{[r.location, r.work_mode].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}</Text> },
  { key: "salary", header: "Salary", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm" truncate>{r.salary_text || "Not stated"}</Text> },
  { key: "fit", header: "Fit", align: "end", width: "4rem", render: (r) => <Text role="numeric">{r.fit ?? "–"}</Text> },
  { key: "status", header: "Status", width: "7rem", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm">{r.status}</Text> },
];

export function JobsScreen({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { run } = useAction();
  const routeStatus = route.screen === "jobs" ? route.status : undefined;
  const [form, setForm] = useState<JobsForm>({ ...formFromQuery({}), status: routeStatus ?? "" });
  const [viewName, setViewName] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, status: routeStatus ?? "" })), [routeStatus]);

  const query = queryFromForm(form);
  const list = useLoad((b) => b.jobs.list(query), [JSON.stringify(query)], ["jobs", "settings"]);
  const views = useLoad((b) => b.jobs.views(), [], ["jobs"]);
  const set = <K extends keyof JobsForm>(k: K, v: JobsForm[K]) => { setViewName(""); setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: v })); };

  const applyView = (name: string) => {
    setViewName(name);
    const v = views.data?.find((x) => x.name === name);
    if (v) setForm(formFromQuery(v.query));
  };
  const saveView = () => run("save-view", async () => {
    await bridge.jobs.saveView(newName.trim(), query);
    setSaving(false);
    setViewName(newName.trim());
    setNewName("");
    views.reload();
  }, "View saved");
  const deleteView = () => run("delete-view", async () => { await bridge.jobs.deleteView(viewName); setViewName(""); views.reload(); });

  const hidden = list.data?.hiddenIneligible ?? 0;
  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Jobs" sub={list.data ? `${list.data.total} shown` : undefined} />
      <div className="filters">
        <Input label="Search jobs" value={form.text} onChange={(e) => set("text", e.target.value)} placeholder="Title, company or place" />
        <Select label="Status" value={form.status} onChange={(e) => set("status", e.target.value as JobsForm["status"])}
          options={[{ value: "", label: "Any status" }, ...JOB_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s }))]} />
        <Select label="Work rights" value={form.label} onChange={(e) => set("label", e.target.value as JobsForm["label"])}
          options={[{ value: "", label: "Any" }, ...WR_LABELS.map((l) => ({ value: l, label: WR_LABEL_TEXT[l] }))]} />
        <Input label="Minimum fit" type="number" min={0} max={100} value={form.fitMin} onChange={(e) => set("fitMin", e.target.value)} />
        <Select label="Sort by" value={form.sort} onChange={(e) => set("sort", e.target.value as JobsForm["sort"])}
          options={[{ value: "default", label: "Best match" }, { value: "fit", label: "Fit" }, { value: "sponsor", label: "Sponsor score" }, { value: "newest", label: "Newest" }]} />
        {views.data?.length ? (
          <Select label="Saved view" value={viewName} onChange={(e) => applyView(e.target.value)}
            options={[{ value: "", label: "None" }, ...views.data.map((v) => ({ value: v.name, label: v.name }))]} />
        ) : null}
        <div className="row">
          <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setSaving(true)}>Save view</Button>
          {viewName ? <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => void deleteView()}>Delete view</Button> : null}
        </div>
        <Checkbox label={`Show ads I can't apply for (${hidden} hidden)`} checked={form.showIneligible} onChange={(e) => set("showIneligible", e.target.checked)} />
      </div>

      {list.error ? <ErrorState title="Jobs can't load" text={list.error} /> : null}
      {list.data ? (
        <Table caption="Jobs" columns={COLUMNS} rows={list.data.rows} rowKey={(r) => `${r.company_slug}/${r.id}`}
          rowClassName={(r) => (r.pinned ? "is-pinned" : !r.eligible ? "is-ineligible" : undefined)}
          onRowActivate={(r) => navigate({ screen: "job", slug: r.company_slug, id: r.id })}
          emptyText="No jobs match these filters." />
      ) : null}

      <Dialog open={saving} onClose={() => setSaving(false)} title="Save this view"
        actions={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setSaving(false)}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" disabled={!newName.trim()} onClick={() => void saveView()}>Save</Button></>}>
        <Input label="View name" value={newName} maxLength={60} onChange={(e) => setNewName(e.target.value)} />
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 20: Job screen and safe Markdown rendering

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/components/Markdown.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Job.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/markdown.test.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/job.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`, `docs/dependencies.md` (add `react-markdown` 10.1.0, MIT, app-ui)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `jobs.get/setStatus/addNote/attach`, `files.reveal/pick`, `links.open`, `fileUrl`; `Tabs`, `Pill`, `Select`, `Textarea`, `Button`; `topicSlug`, `WR_LABEL_TEXT`.
- Produces:
  - `safeUrl(url: string, kind: "link" | "image", baseDir: string | undefined, fileUrl: (rel: string) => string): string` ("" means "drop it")
  - `Markdown({ text: string; baseDir?: string })` (raw HTML skipped; links open through `links.open`; remote images replaced by a note; relative images served through `careeros-file:`)
  - `JobScreen({ route })`

Ruling: Markdown never renders raw HTML (`skipHtml`), and only `https:` links survive. Remote images are blocked so opening a job description can't make a network request; images next to the file still show through the `careeros-file:` scheme.

```tsx
import { Markdown, safeUrl } from "../src/components/Markdown";

const fu = (rel: string) => `careeros-file://ws/${rel}`;

describe("safeUrl", () => {
  it("keeps only https links", () => {
    expect(safeUrl("https://jobs.example.com/1", "link", undefined, fu)).toBe("https://jobs.example.com/1");
    for (const u of ["http://jobs.example.com", "javascript:alert(1)", "JaVaScRiPt:alert(1)", "file:///etc/passwd", "careeros-file://ws/x", "data:text/html,x", "/abs", "notes.md"]) {
      expect(safeUrl(u, "link", "a", fu), u).toBe("");
    }
  });
  it("serves relative images from the workspace and blocks the rest", () => {
    expect(safeUrl("diagram.png", "image", "companies/acme-corp/jobs/j1", fu)).toBe("careeros-file://ws/companies/acme-corp/jobs/j1/diagram.png");
    expect(safeUrl("./img/a.png", "image", "learning/rag", fu)).toBe("careeros-file://ws/learning/rag/img/a.png");
    for (const u of ["https://cdn.example.com/x.png", "../../../me/identity/passport.png", "/etc/x.png", "data:image/png;base64,AA", "a\\b.png"]) {
      expect(safeUrl(u, "image", "learning/rag", fu), u).toBe("");
    }
    expect(safeUrl("a.png", "image", undefined, fu)).toBe("");
  });
});

describe("Markdown", () => {
  const md = [
    "# Role", "", "<script>window.bad = 1</script><b>raw bold</b>", "",

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Apply here" }));
    expect(bridge.opened).toEqual(["https://jobs.example.com/acme/1"]);

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/components/Markdown.tsx`**

```tsx
import ReactMarkdown, { type Components } from "react-markdown";
import { useBridge } from "../bridge-context";

/** Returns a URL that is safe to render, or "" to drop it. */
export function safeUrl(url: string, kind: "link" | "image", baseDir: string | undefined, fileUrl: (rel: string) => string): string {
  const u = url.trim();
  if (kind === "link") return /^https:\/\//i.test(u) ? u : "";
  // Images: never fetch from the network, never escape the workspace.
  if (!baseDir || /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(u) || u.startsWith("/") || u.includes("\\") || /[\x00-\x1f]/.test(u)) return "";
  const parts = [...baseDir.split("/"), ...u.split("/")].filter((p) => p !== "" && p !== ".");
  if (parts.includes("..")) return "";
  return fileUrl(parts.join("/"));
}

export function Markdown({ text, baseDir }: { text: string; baseDir?: string }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const components: Components = {
    a: ({ href, children }) => (href
      ? <a href={href} onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void bridge.links.open(href); }}>{children}</a>
      : <span>{children}</span>),
    img: ({ src, alt }) => (typeof src === "string" && src
      ? <img src={src} alt={alt ?? ""} className="media" />
      : <span className="t-body-sm t-muted">Image not shown: {alt || "remote image"}</span>),
  };
  return (
    <div className="markdown">
      <ReactMarkdown skipHtml urlTransform={(url, key) => safeUrl(url, key === "src" ? "image" : "link", baseDir, bridge.fileUrl)} components={components}>
        {text}
      </ReactMarkdown>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Job.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { ExternalLink, FolderOpen, Paperclip, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Pill, Select, Tabs, Text, Textarea } from "@careeros/brand";
import { JOB_STATUSES, WR_LABEL_TEXT, topicSlug, type JobDetail, type JobStatus } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { Markdown } from "../components/Markdown";
import { ErrorState, Loading } from "../components/States";
import { bytes, fmtDate, fmtDateTime, wrTone } from "../format";
import { navigate, type Route } from "../router";

const TABS = ["description", "score", "files", "timeline", "gaps"] as const;
type Tab = (typeof TABS)[number];

export function JobScreen({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  if (route.screen !== "job") return <Loading />;
  return <JobView slug={route.slug} id={route.id} tab={(TABS as readonly string[]).includes(route.tab ?? "") ? (route.tab as Tab) : "description"} />;
}

function JobView({ slug, id, tab }: { slug: string; id: string; tab: Tab }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const { data: d, error } = useLoad((b) => b.jobs.get(slug, id), [slug, id], ["jobs", "settings"]);

  if (error && !d) {
    return (
      <ErrorState title="This job can't be shown"
        text={`${error}. The job file may have been moved, deleted or edited by hand. Run "careeros validate" to see what is wrong.`}
        action={<Button onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "jobs" })}>Back to Jobs</Button>} />
    );
  }
  if (!d) return <Loading />;
  const j = d.job;
  const base = `companies/${slug}/jobs/${id}`;
  const url = [j.apply_via, j.url].find((u) => u.startsWith("https://"));

  const setStatus = (s: JobStatus) => run("status", () => bridge.jobs.setStatus(slug, id, s, ""), `Status set to ${s}`);
  const addNote = () => run("note", async () => { await bridge.jobs.addNote(slug, id, note.trim()); setNote(""); });
  const attach = () => run("attach", async () => {
    const p = await bridge.files.pick();
    if (p) await bridge.jobs.attach(slug, id, p, p.split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? "File", "");
  }, "File attached");

  return (
    <>
      <header className="screen-head">
        <div className="grow">
          <Text role="display" truncate>{j.title}</Text>
          <div className="row">
            <Text role="body" muted>{d.company?.name ?? slug}{j.client ? ` · client ${j.client}` : ""} · {[j.location, j.work_mode].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}</Text>
            <Pill tone={wrTone(j.work_rights.label)}>{WR_LABEL_TEXT[j.work_rights.label]}</Pill>
            {!d.eligible ? <Pill tone="danger">You can't apply</Pill> : null}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="row">
          <Select label="Status" value={j.status} onChange={(e) => void setStatus(e.target.value as JobStatus)} options={JOB_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s }))} />
          {url ? <Button icon={ExternalLink} onClick={() => void bridge.links.open(url)}>Open ad</Button> : null}
          <Button variant="primary" icon={Send} onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "apply", slug, id })}>Prepare</Button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <Tabs label="Job sections" value={tab} onChange={(t) => navigate({ screen: "job", slug, id, tab: t })}
        tabs={[
          { id: "description", label: "Description" }, { id: "score", label: "Score" }, { id: "files", label: "Files", count: d.files.length },
          { id: "timeline", label: "Timeline", count: j.timeline.length }, { id: "gaps", label: "Gaps", count: d.gaps.length },
        ]}>
        {tab === "description" ? (d.description ? <Markdown text={d.description} baseDir={base} /> : <Text role="body" muted>No description saved for this job.</Text>) : null}
        {tab === "score" ? <ScoreTab d={d} base={base} /> : null}
        {tab === "files" ? (
          <div className="stack">
            <ul className="list-plain">
              {d.files.map((f) => (
                <li key={f.rel} className="row">
                  <span className="grow"><Text role="body" truncate as="div">{f.label}</Text><Text role="body-sm" muted as="div">{f.path}{f.size !== null ? ` · ${bytes(f.size)}` : ""}{f.at ? ` · ${fmtDate(f.at)}` : ""}</Text></span>
                  {f.exists ? <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={FolderOpen} aria-label={`Show ${f.label} in folder`} onClick={() => void bridge.files.reveal(f.rel)}>Show</Button> : <Pill tone="warning">Missing</Pill>}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <div><Button icon={Paperclip} loading={busy === "attach"} onClick={() => void attach()}>Attach a file</Button></div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {tab === "timeline" ? (
          <div className="stack">
            <ul className="list-plain timeline">
              {[...j.timeline].reverse().map((e, i) => (
                <li key={`${e.at}-${i}`}><Text role="body-sm" muted>{fmtDateTime(e.at)}</Text><span><Text role="body" as="div">{e.event}</Text>{e.note ? <Text role="body-sm" muted as="div">{e.note}</Text> : null}</span></li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <Textarea label="Add a note" value={note} maxLength={2000} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} />
            <div><Button disabled={!note.trim()} loading={busy === "note"} onClick={() => void addNote()}>Add note</Button></div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {tab === "gaps" ? (
          d.gaps.length ? (
            <ul className="list-plain">
              {d.gaps.map((g) => (
                <li key={g} className="row row--between"><Text role="body">{g}</Text>
                  <Button size="sm" aria-label={`Study ${g}`} onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "learn", topic: topicSlug(g) })}>Study</Button></li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : <Text role="body" muted>No gaps recorded. Score the job with Claude to find them.</Text>
        ) : null}
      </Tabs>
    </>
  );
}

function ScoreTab({ d, base }: { d: JobDetail; base: string }) {
  const s = d.job.scores;
  return (
    <div className="stack">
      <div className="row">
        <Pill>Fit {s.fit.value ?? "–"}</Pill>
        <Pill>Sponsor {s.sponsor.value ?? "–"}{s.sponsor.tier ? ` · ${s.sponsor.tier}` : ""}</Pill>
        {s.career.match !== null ? <Pill>Match {s.career.match}</Pill> : null}
      </div>
      {s.sponsor.reasons.length ? <ul>{s.sponsor.reasons.map((r) => <li key={r}>{r}</li>)}</ul> : null}
      {d.scoreReport ? <Markdown text={d.scoreReport} baseDir={base} /> : <Text role="body" muted>No score yet. Ask Claude to score this job.</Text>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 21: Apply screen (prepare with Claude, review with claims, hand-off approval, confirm)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Apply.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/apply.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `apply.state/prepare/requestApproval/markApplied`, `claude.openDesktop`, `files.readText/reveal`; `Markdown`.
- Produces: `ApplyScreen({ route })`; `STEP_LABELS: Record<Exclude<ApplyState["step"], "done">, string>`.

Ruling: CareerOS never submits. Prepare writes `application/PREPARE.md`, copies the prompt to the clipboard and asks the shell to open Claude Desktop; when it can't, the prompt stays on screen to copy. Hand-off is an approval like any other, so it runs only after an explicit Approve.

`packages/app-ui/test/apply.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { ApplyScreen } from "../src/screens/Apply";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

const LABS = { screen: "apply", slug: "example-labs", id: "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm" } as const;
const current = () => document.querySelector('[aria-current="step"]')?.textContent;

describe("Apply", () => {
  it("prepares with Claude: writes the prompt, copies it and opens Claude", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<ApplyScreen route={LABS} />);
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /Backend Engineer \(LLM\)/ });
    expect(current()).toBe("Prepare");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Prepare with Claude" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(bridge.calls.map((c) => c.method)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["apply.prepare", "claude.openDesktop"])));
    expect(await navigator.clipboard.readText()).toContain("companies/example-labs/jobs/2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm");
    expect(screen.getByText(/Do not submit anything/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("moves to review when Claude's files appear, and shows every claim with its source", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    renderApp(<ApplyScreen route={LABS} />, { bridge: b });
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /Backend Engineer/ });
    b.simulateClaudeOutput(LABS.slug, LABS.id);
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toBe("Review"));
    expect(screen.getByText("Built retrieval pipelines")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("me/history/roles.md")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText(/I would like to apply for this role/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("warns when there is no claims file", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    renderApp(<ApplyScreen route={LABS} />, { bridge: b });
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /Backend Engineer/ });
    b.simulateClaudeOutput(LABS.slug, LABS.id, false);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/No claims file yet/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("hands off only after approval, then confirms", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    b.simulateClaudeOutput(LABS.slug, LABS.id);
    renderApp(<ApplyScreen route={LABS} />, { bridge: b });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Ask for hand-off approval" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toBe("Hand off"));
    expect(b.revealed).toEqual([]);
    const pending = (await b.approvals.list()).find((a) => a.action === "apply_handoff")!;
    await b.approvals.approve(pending.id);
    await waitFor(() => expect(current()).toBe("Confirm"));
    expect(b.revealed).toEqual([`companies/${LABS.slug}/jobs/${LABS.id}/application`]);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "I applied" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("This application is done.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect((await b.jobs.get(LABS.slug, LABS.id)).job.status).toBe("Applied");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Copy, FolderOpen, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Pill, Text, useToast } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { ApplyState, JobFile } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { Markdown } from "../components/Markdown";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { navigate, type Route } from "../router";

export const STEP_LABELS: Record<Exclude<ApplyState["step"], "done">, string> = { prepare: "Prepare", review: "Review", handoff: "Hand off", confirm: "Confirm" };

async function copy(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

export function ApplyScreen({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  if (route.screen !== "apply") return <Loading />;
  return <ApplyView slug={route.slug} id={route.id} />;
}

function ApplyView({ slug, id }: { slug: string; id: string }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const toast = useToast();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const { data: s, error } = useLoad((b) => b.apply.state(slug, id), [slug, id], ["jobs", "approvals"]);
  if (error && !s) return <ErrorState title="This application can't be shown" text={error} action={<Button onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "jobs" })}>Back to Jobs</Button>} />;
  if (!s) return <Loading />;

  const prepare = () => run("prepare", async () => {
    const st = await bridge.apply.prepare(slug, id);
    const copied = await copy(st.prompt);
    const opened = await bridge.claude.openDesktop();
    toast({ tone: "success", text: opened ? (copied ? "Claude is open. Paste the prompt to start." : "Claude is open. Copy the prompt below.") : "Open Claude and paste the prompt below." });
  });
  const files = s.files.filter((f) => !f.path.endsWith("PREPARE.md"));

  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title={`Apply: ${s.title}`} sub={s.company_name} right={<Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "job", slug, id })}>Open the job</Button>} />
      {s.step !== "done" ? (
        <ol className="steps" aria-label="Steps">
          {(Object.keys(STEP_LABELS) as (keyof typeof STEP_LABELS)[]).map((k) => <li key={k} aria-current={s.step === k ? "step" : undefined}>{STEP_LABELS[k]}</li>)}
        </ol>
      ) : null}

      {s.step === "prepare" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="body">Claude writes a tailored resume, a cover letter and answers into this job's application folder. Nothing is sent.</Text>
          <div className="row">
            <Button variant="primary" icon={Sparkles} loading={busy === "prepare"} onClick={() => void prepare()}>Prepare with Claude</Button>
            <Button icon={Copy} onClick={() => void copy(s.prompt).then((ok) => toast({ text: ok ? "Prompt copied" : "Copy the prompt by hand", tone: ok ? "success" : "neutral" }))}>Copy prompt</Button>
          </div>
          <pre className="markdown">{s.prompt}</pre>
          <Text role="body-sm" muted>This page moves on by itself when the files appear.</Text>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {s.step === "review" ? (
        <div className="split">
          <Card className="stack"><Text role="heading-2">Files</Text><FileReview files={files} /></Card>
          <Card className="stack">
            <Text role="heading-2">Claims</Text>
            {s.claims ? (
              <ul className="list-plain">
                {s.claims.map((c, i) => <li key={i}><Text role="body" as="div">{c.claim}</Text><Text role="body-sm" muted as="div">{c.source}</Text></li>)}
              </ul>
            ) : (
              <><Pill tone="warning">Unchecked</Pill><Text role="body">No claims file yet. Ask Claude to write application/claims.json so you can check every claim.</Text></>
            )}
            <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "handoff"} onClick={() => void run("handoff", () => bridge.apply.requestApproval(slug, id), "Hand-off is waiting in Approvals")}>Ask for hand-off approval</Button>
          </Card>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {s.step === "handoff" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="body">The hand-off is waiting for your approval. Approving will:</Text>
          <ul>{(s.approval?.steps ?? []).map((st) => <li key={st}>{st}</li>)}</ul>
          <div><Button variant="primary" onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "approvals" })}>Open Approvals</Button></div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {s.step === "confirm" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="body">The apply page and your files are open. Did you submit the application?</Text>
          <div className="row">
            <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "applied"} onClick={() => void run("applied", () => bridge.apply.markApplied(slug, id), "Marked as applied")}>I applied</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "job", slug, id })}>Not yet</Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {s.step === "done" ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="body">This application is done.</Text>
          <div><Button onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "job", slug, id, tab: "timeline" })}>See the timeline</Button></div>
        </Card>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

function FileReview({ files }: { files: JobFile[] }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // Open the cover letter first when there is one: it is the file people most want to read before a hand-off.
  const md = files.filter((f) => f.kind === "markdown");
  const current = files.find((f) => f.rel === sel) ?? md.find((f) => f.path.includes("cover-letter")) ?? md[0] ?? null;
  const text = useLoad((b) => (current?.kind === "markdown" ? b.files.readText(current.rel) : Promise.resolve("")), [current?.rel], ["jobs"]);
  return (
    <>
      <ul className="list-plain">
        {files.map((f) => (
          <li key={f.rel} className="row">
            <Button size="sm" variant={f.rel === current?.rel ? "secondary" : "ghost"} onClick={() => setSel(f.rel)}>{f.label}</Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={FolderOpen} aria-label={`Show ${f.label} in folder`} onClick={() => void bridge.files.reveal(f.rel)} />
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {current?.kind === "markdown" && text.data ? <Markdown text={text.data} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";

const fileCalls = (b: MockBridge) => b.calls.filter((c) => c.method === "inbox.file").map((c) => c.args);

describe("Inbox", () => {
  it("round-trips targets", () => {
    for (const t of [{ kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, { kind: "me", dir: "resumes" }] as const) expect(decodeTarget(encodeTarget(t), "")).toEqual(t);
    expect(decodeTarget("job:acme-corp:2026-01-12-ai-engineer", "application")).toEqual({ kind: "job", company_slug: "acme-corp", id: "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", subdir: "application" });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf" })).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(fileCalls(bridge)).toEqual([["Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf", { kind: "me", dir: "identity", sub: "visa" }, ""]]);
  });

  it("lets you pick a job and a job folder instead", async () => {

    await userEvent.selectOptions(within(cl).getByLabelText("Destination"), "job:example-labs:2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm");
    await userEvent.selectOptions(within(cl).getByLabelText("Job folder"), "interview");

    await waitFor(() => expect(fileCalls(bridge)).toEqual([[
      "acme-corp-cover-letter.pdf", { kind: "job", company_slug: "example-labs", id: "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm", subdir: "interview" }, "Prep pack",
    ]]));
  });

  it("adds dropped files and needs a folder when no rule matches", async () => {

    expect(within(item).getByText("No rule matched. Choose a folder.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(item).getByRole("button", { name: "File it" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("says when a dropped file has no path", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge({ inbox: [] });
    b.pathForFile = () => null;
    renderApp(<InboxScreen />, { bridge: b });
    fireEvent.drop(await screen.findByLabelText("Drop files here"), { dataTransfer: { files: [new File(["x"], "clip.txt")] } });
    expect(await screen.findByText(/couldn't be added/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(b.calls.some((c) => c.method === "inbox.add")).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Inbox.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState, type DragEvent } from "react";
import { FilePlus } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, Pill, Select, Text, useToast } from "@careeros/brand";
import { JOB_SUBDIRS, ME_DIRS, type InboxItem, type InboxTarget, type JobRow, type JobSubdir, type MeDir } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { bytes, targetText } from "../format";

export function encodeTarget(t: InboxTarget | null): string {
  if (!t) return "";
  return t.kind === "me" ? `me:${t.dir}${t.sub ? `:${t.sub}` : ""}` : `job:${t.company_slug}:${t.id}`;
}

export function decodeTarget(v: string, subdir: JobSubdir): InboxTarget | null {
  const [kind, a, ...rest] = v.split(":");
  if (kind === "me" && a && (ME_DIRS as readonly string[]).includes(a)) {
    const sub = rest.join(":");
    return sub ? { kind: "me", dir: a as MeDir, sub } : { kind: "me", dir: a as MeDir };
  }
  if (kind === "job" && a && rest[0]) return { kind: "job", company_slug: a, id: rest[0], subdir };
  return null;
}

const SUBDIR_TEXT: Record<JobSubdir, string> = { "": "Job folder (top)", application: "application", emails: "emails", interview: "interview" };
const CONF_TONE = { high: "success", medium: "accent", low: "neutral" } as const;

export function InboxScreen() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const toast = useToast();
  const { run } = useAction();
  const [over, setOver] = useState(false);
  const items = useLoad((b) => b.inbox.list(), [], ["inbox"]);
  const jobs = useLoad((b) => b.jobs.list({ showIneligible: true, limit: 500 }), [], ["jobs"]);

  const add = (paths: string[]) => run("add", () => bridge.inbox.add(paths), paths.length === 1 ? "Added to your inbox" : `Added ${paths.length} files`);
  const onDrop = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setOver(false);
    const files = [...(e.dataTransfer?.files ?? [])];
    const paths = files.map((f) => bridge.pathForFile(f)).filter((p): p is string => !!p);
    if (paths.length < files.length) toast({ tone: "danger", text: `${files.length - paths.length} file(s) couldn't be added. Drag them from Finder or File Explorer.` });
    if (paths.length) void add(paths);
  };
  const pick = () => run("pick", async () => { const p = await bridge.files.pick(); if (p) await bridge.inbox.add([p]); });

  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Inbox" sub="Files waiting to be put in the right folder." />
      <div className={`dropzone${over ? " is-over" : ""}`} aria-label="Drop files here" role="group"
        onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setOver(true); }} onDragLeave={() => setOver(false)} onDrop={onDrop}>
        <Text role="body">Drop files here to add them to your inbox.</Text>
        <Button icon={FilePlus} onClick={() => void pick()}>Choose a file</Button>
      </div>
      {items.error && !items.data ? <ErrorState title="The inbox can't load" text={items.error} /> : null}
      {!items.data ? <Loading /> : items.data.length === 0 ? <Text role="body" muted>Your inbox is empty.</Text> : (
        <div className="stack">
          {items.data.map((it) => <InboxRow key={it.name} item={it} jobs={jobs.data?.rows ?? []} />)}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function InboxRow({ item, jobs }: { item: InboxItem; jobs: JobRow[] }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const s = item.suggestion.target;
  const [dest, setDest] = useState(encodeTarget(s));
  const [subdir, setSubdir] = useState<JobSubdir>(s?.kind === "job" ? s.subdir ?? "" : "");
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");

  const options = [
    { value: "", label: "Choose a folder" },
    ...(s?.kind === "me" && s.sub ? [{ value: encodeTarget(s), label: targetText(s) }] : []),
    ...ME_DIRS.map((d) => ({ value: `me:${d}`, label: `me/${d}` })),
    ...jobs.map((j) => ({ value: `job:${j.company_slug}:${j.id}`, label: `Job: ${j.title} · ${j.company_name}` })),
  ];
  const target = decodeTarget(dest, subdir);
  const file = () => run(`file-${item.name}`, () => bridge.inbox.file(item.name, target!, note.trim()), `Filed ${item.name}`);

  return (
    <Card aria-label={item.name} className="stack">
      <div className="row row--between">
        <Text role="body" truncate className="grow">{item.name}</Text>
        <Text role="body-sm" muted>{bytes(item.size)}</Text>
      </div>
      <div className="row"><Pill tone={CONF_TONE[item.suggestion.confidence]}>{item.suggestion.confidence === "low" ? "No suggestion" : "Suggested"}</Pill><Text role="body-sm" muted>{item.suggestion.reason}</Text></div>
      <div className="filters">
        <Select label="Destination" value={dest} onChange={(e) => setDest(e.target.value)} options={options} />
        {dest.startsWith("job:") ? <Select label="Job folder" value={subdir} onChange={(e) => setSubdir(e.target.value as JobSubdir)} options={JOB_SUBDIRS.map((d) => ({ value: d, label: SUBDIR_TEXT[d] }))} /> : null}
        <Input label="Note" value={note} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} />
        <div><Button variant="primary" disabled={!target} loading={busy === `file-${item.name}`} onClick={() => void file()}>File it</Button></div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { BookOpen, Video } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { LearnItem } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { Markdown } from "../components/Markdown";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { navigate, type Route } from "../router";

const dirOf = (p: string) => p.split("/").slice(0, -1).join("/");

export function Reader({ item }: { item: LearnItem }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const text = useLoad((b) => (item.kind === "guide" ? b.files.readText(item.path) : Promise.resolve("")), [item.path], ["learn"]);
  if (item.kind === "video") return <video className="media" controls preload="metadata" src={bridge.fileUrl(item.path)} aria-label={item.title} />;
  if (text.error) return <ErrorState title="This guide can't be opened" text={text.error} />;
  return text.data === undefined ? <Loading /> : <Markdown text={text.data} baseDir={dirOf(item.path)} />;
}

export function LearnScreen({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  const topicSlug = route.screen === "learn" ? route.topic : undefined;
  const { data, error } = useLoad((b) => b.learn.list(), [], ["learn", "jobs"]);
  const [item, setItem] = useState<LearnItem | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => setItem(null), [topicSlug]);

  if (error && !data) return <ErrorState title="Learn can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!data) return <Loading />;
  const topic = topicSlug ? data.topics.find((t) => t.slug === topicSlug) : undefined;
  const shown = item ?? topic?.items[0] ?? null;

  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Learn" sub="Study guides and interview prep that Claude writes into your workspace." />
      <div className="cols">
        <div className="stack">
          {topicSlug && !topic ? (
            <Card className="stack">
              <Text role="body">No study guide for "{topicSlug}" yet.</Text>
              <Text role="body-sm" muted>Ask Claude to write one in learning/{topicSlug}/guide.md. It shows up here as soon as it's saved.</Text>
            </Card>
          ) : null}
          {topic ? (
            <Card className="stack">
              <Text role="heading-2">{topic.title}</Text>
              <div className="row">
                {topic.items.map((it) => (
                  <Button key={it.path} size="sm" icon={it.kind === "video" ? Video : BookOpen} variant={shown?.path === it.path ? "secondary" : "ghost"} onClick={() => setItem(it)}>{it.title}</Button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </Card>
          ) : null}
          {shown ? <Card><Reader item={shown} /></Card> : !topicSlug ? <Text role="body" muted>Pick a topic or an interview to start.</Text> : null}
        </div>
        <div className="stack">
          <Card className="stack">
            <Text role="heading-2">Topics</Text>
            {data.topics.length === 0 ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>No topics yet.</Text> : (
              <ul className="list-plain">
                {data.topics.map((t) => <li key={t.slug}><Button variant={t.slug === topicSlug ? "secondary" : "ghost"} onClick={() => navigate({ screen: "learn", topic: t.slug })}>{t.title}</Button></li>)}
              </ul>
            )}
          </Card>
          <Card className="stack">
            <Text role="heading-2">Interview prep</Text>
            {data.interviewPrep.length === 0 ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>Nothing yet.</Text> : (
              <ul className="list-plain">
                {data.interviewPrep.flatMap((p) => p.items.map((it) => (
                  <li key={it.path}><Button variant="ghost" title={`${p.title} · ${p.company_name}`} onClick={() => setItem(it)}>{it.title}</Button></li>
                )))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Pill, Select, Table, Text, type Column, type PillTone } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { SponsorRow } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { fmtDate } from "../format";

export const SPONSOR_STATUS: Record<string, { text: string; tone: PillTone }> = {
  verified: { text: "Verified sponsor", tone: "success" },
  "to-verify": { text: "To verify", tone: "warning" },
  "checked-none": { text: "Checked: none found", tone: "neutral" },
  sanctioned: { text: "Sanctioned", tone: "danger" },
  unknown: { text: "Not checked", tone: "neutral" },
};
const statusOf = (s: string) => SPONSOR_STATUS[s] ?? { text: s, tone: "neutral" as const };

export function SponsorsScreen() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { data, error } = useLoad((b) => b.sponsors.list(), [], ["sponsors", "jobs"]);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  if (error && !data) return <ErrorState title="Sponsors can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!data) return <Loading />;

  const q = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  const rows = data.filter((r) => (!q || r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) && (!status || r.status === status));
  const columns: Column<SponsorRow>[] = [
    { key: "name", header: "Employer", render: (r) => <Text role="body" truncate>{r.name}</Text> },
    { key: "status", header: "Status", width: "11rem", render: (r) => <Pill tone={statusOf(r.status).tone}>{statusOf(r.status).text}</Pill> },
    { key: "score", header: "Score", align: "end", width: "5rem", render: (r) => <Text role="numeric">{r.score ?? "–"}</Text> },
    { key: "jobs", header: "Jobs", align: "end", width: "4rem", render: (r) => <Text role="numeric">{r.jobs}</Text> },
    { key: "checked", header: "Last checked", width: "8rem", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm" muted>{fmtDate(r.last_checked) || "Never"}</Text> },
    {
      key: "evidence", header: "Latest evidence", render: (r) => {
        const e = r.evidence.at(-1);
        if (!e) return <Text role="body-sm" muted>None</Text>;
        return (
          <div className="row">
            <Text role="body-sm" truncate className="grow">{e.note}</Text>
            {e.url.startsWith("https://") ? <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={ExternalLink} aria-label={`Open source for ${r.name}`} onClick={() => void bridge.links.open(e.url)} /> : null}
          </div>
        );
      },
    },
  ];
  const statuses = [...new Set(data.map((r) => r.status))].sort();

  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Sponsors" sub="What you know about each employer's visa sponsorship. Claude adds evidence as it finds it." />
      <div className="filters">
        <Input label="Find an employer" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} />
        <Select label="Status" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: "", label: "Any status" }, ...statuses.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: statusOf(s).text }))]} />
      </div>
      <Table caption="Sponsors" columns={columns} rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.slug ?? r.name} emptyText="No employers match." />
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Pill, Select, Table, Text, type Column } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { SearchRow } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { fmtDateTime } from "../format";

const COLUMNS: Column<SearchRow>[] = [
  { key: "at", header: "When", width: "9rem", render: (r) => <div className="stack"><Text role="body-sm">{fmtDateTime(r.at)}</Text>{r.recent ? <Pill tone="accent">Last 7 days</Pill> : null}</div> },
  { key: "site", header: "Site", width: "8rem", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm" truncate>{r.site}</Text> },
  { key: "query", header: "Query", render: (r) => <Text role="body" truncate>{r.query}</Text> },
  { key: "location", header: "Location", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm" truncate>{r.location}</Text> },
  { key: "results", header: "Results", align: "end", width: "5rem", render: (r) => <Text role="numeric">{r.results}</Text> },
  { key: "kept", header: "Kept", align: "end", width: "4rem", render: (r) => <Text role="numeric">{r.kept}</Text> },
  { key: "notes", header: "Notes", render: (r) => <Text role="body-sm" muted truncate>{r.notes}</Text> },
];

export function SearchesScreen() {
  const { data, error } = useLoad((b) => b.searches.list(), [], ["searches"]);
  const [site, setSite] = useState("");
  if (error && !data) return <ErrorState title="Searches can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!data) return <Loading />;
  const rows = data.rows.filter((r) => !site || r.site === site);
  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Searches" sub="Every search Claude ran. Searches from the last 7 days are skipped, so nothing is repeated." />
      <div className="filters">
        <Select label="Site" value={site} onChange={(e) => setSite(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: "", label: "All sites" }, ...data.sites.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s }))]} />
      </div>
      <Table caption="Searches" columns={COLUMNS} rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => `${r.at}|${r.site}|${r.query}|${r.location}`} emptyText="No searches yet." />
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 26: Settings screen (schema-driven forms, secrets, theme)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/components/FieldForm.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Settings.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/settings.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `settings.get/save`, `secrets.status/set/remove`, `app.info`; `useThemePref`; `Input`, `Select`, `Checkbox`, `Tabs`.
- Produces:
  - `getPath(obj: unknown, path: string): unknown`
  - `toDisplay(f: FieldDef, v: unknown): unknown` and `toValue(f: FieldDef, raw: unknown): { value?: unknown; error?: string }`
  - `FieldForm({ kind }: { kind: SettingsKind })` (sends only changed fields)
  - `SecretsPanel()`, `SettingsScreen()`

Ruling: lists of objects (`profile.locations`) are shown read-only for now, with a pointer to `settings/profile.json`; editing them gets its own small form in a later plan. Secrets are write-only from the renderer: it can learn whether a secret is set, never its value.

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { toValue } from "../src/components/FieldForm";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { SettingsScreen } from "../src/screens/Settings";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

const saves = (b: MockBridge) => b.calls.filter((c) => c.method === "settings.save").map((c) => c.args);
const field = (over: object) => ({ path: "x", label: "X", type: "string", options: [], optionLabels: {}, help: "", pattern: "", fields: [], ...over });

describe("toValue", () => {
  it("converts form text to typed values", () => {
    expect(toValue(field({ type: "string[]" }), " a, b ,, c ")).toEqual({ value: ["a", "b", "c"] });
    expect(toValue(field({ type: "number|null" }), "")).toEqual({ value: null });
    expect(toValue(field({ type: "number" }), "12x")).toEqual({ error: "Enter a number." });
    expect(toValue(field({ pattern: "^(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})?$" }), "30/06/2028")).toEqual({ error: "Doesn't match the expected format." });
  });
});

describe("Settings", () => {
  it("saves only the fields you changed, and job eligibility follows", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<SettingsScreen />);
    await userEvent.selectOptions(await screen.findByLabelText("Work rights"), "citizen");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(saves(bridge)).toEqual([["profile", [{ path: "work_rights.status", value: "citizen" }]]]));
    expect((await bridge.jobs.list({})).hiddenIneligible).toBe(0);
  });
  it("keeps commas while typing a list and saves it as a list", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<SettingsScreen />);
    const roles = await screen.findByLabelText("Target roles");
    await userEvent.clear(roles);
    await userEvent.type(roles, "AI Engineer, Data Engineer");
    expect(roles).toHaveValue("AI Engineer, Data Engineer");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(saves(bridge)[0]).toEqual(["profile", [{ path: "targets.roles", value: ["AI Engineer", "Data Engineer"] }]]));
  });
  it("shows format errors and saves nothing", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<SettingsScreen />);
    const visa = await screen.findByLabelText("Visa expiry (YYYY-MM-DD)");
    await userEvent.clear(visa);
    await userEvent.type(visa, "30/06/2028");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Doesn't match the expected format.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(visa).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(saves(bridge)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("shows lists of places read-only", async () => {
    renderApp(<SettingsScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Edit this list in settings\/profile\.json/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("stores secrets without showing them back", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<SettingsScreen />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("tab", { name: "Connections" }));
    const input = await screen.findByLabelText("Gmail app password");
    expect(input).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");
    await user.type(input, "abcd" + "efgh" + "ijkl" + "mnop");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save Gmail app password" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Saved securely")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(input).toHaveValue("");
    expect(bridge.calls.find((c) => c.method === "secrets.set")?.args).toEqual(["gmail_app_password", "<redacted>"]);
  });
  it("says when secure storage is missing", async () => {
    renderApp(<SettingsScreen />, { bridge: new MockBridge({ secretStoreAvailable: false }) });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("tab", { name: "Connections" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Secure storage isn't available/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("Gmail app password")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("switches theme", async () => {
    renderApp(<SettingsScreen />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("tab", { name: "Appearance" }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText("Paper (light)"));
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("paper");
    expect(localStorage.getItem("careeros.theme")).toBe("paper");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, Checkbox, Input, Select, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { FieldDef, SettingsChange, SettingsKind } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading } from "./States";

export function getPath(obj: unknown, path: string): unknown {
  return path.split(".").reduce<unknown>((o, k) => (o && typeof o === "object" ? (o as Record<string, unknown>)[k] : undefined), obj);
}

const isNumber = (f: FieldDef) => /^(number|integer)(\|null)?$/.test(f.type);

/** What the form control shows for a stored value. Lists and numbers are edited as text. */
export function toDisplay(f: FieldDef, v: unknown): unknown {
  if (f.type === "string[]") return Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(", ") : "";
  if (isNumber(f)) return v === null || v === undefined ? "" : String(v);
  if (f.type === "enum[]") return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  if (f.type === "boolean") return v === true;
  return v ?? "";
}

/** Turns what the person typed into the stored value, or an error to show. */
export function toValue(f: FieldDef, raw: unknown): { value?: unknown; error?: string } {
  if (f.type === "string[]") return { value: String(raw).split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) };
  if (isNumber(f)) {
    const s = String(raw).trim();
    if (s === "") return f.type.endsWith("|null") ? { value: null } : { error: "Enter a number." };
    const n = Number(s);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? { value: n } : { error: "Enter a number." };
  }
  if (f.pattern && typeof raw === "string" && !new RegExp(f.pattern).test(raw)) return { error: "Doesn't match the expected format." };
  return { value: raw };
}

export function FieldForm({ kind }: { kind: SettingsKind }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const { data, error } = useLoad((b) => b.settings.get(kind), [kind], ["settings"]);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Record<string, unknown>>({});
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  if (error && !data) return <ErrorState title="Settings can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!data) return <Loading />;

  const shown = (f: FieldDef) => (Object.hasOwn(draft, f.path) ? draft[f.path] : toDisplay(f, getPath(data.values, f.path)));
  const change = (f: FieldDef, v: unknown) => { setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [f.path]: v })); setErrors((e) => { const { [f.path]: _drop, ...rest } = e; return rest; }); };

  const save = () => {
    const changes: SettingsChange[] = [];
    const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const f of data.fields) {
      if (!Object.hasOwn(draft, f.path)) continue;
      const r = toValue(f, draft[f.path]);
      if (r.error) errs[f.path] = r.error;
      else changes.push({ path: f.path, value: r.value });
    }
    setErrors(errs);
    if (Object.keys(errs).length || !changes.length) return;
    void run("save", async () => { await bridge.settings.save(kind, changes); setDraft({}); }, "Saved");
  };

  return (
    <form className="stack" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); save(); }}>
      {data.fields.map((f) => {
        const v = shown(f);
        const err = errors[f.path];
        if (f.type === "object") return <Text key={f.path} role="heading-2">{f.label}</Text>;
        if (f.type === "boolean") return <Checkbox key={f.path} label={f.label} help={f.help || undefined} checked={v === true} onChange={(e) => change(f, e.target.checked)} />;
        if (f.type === "enum") {
          return <Select key={f.path} label={f.label} help={f.help || undefined} error={err} value={String(v)} onChange={(e) => change(f, e.target.value)}
            options={f.options.map((o) => ({ value: o, label: f.optionLabels[o] ?? o }))} />;
        }
        if (f.type === "enum[]") {
          const list = v as string[];
          return (
            <fieldset key={f.path}>
              <legend>{f.label}</legend>
              <div className="chip-group">
                {f.options.map((o) => (
                  <Checkbox key={o} label={f.optionLabels[o] ?? o} checked={list.includes(o)}
                    onChange={(e) => change(f, e.target.checked ? [...list, o] : list.filter((x) => x !== o))} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
          );
        }
        if (f.type === "object[]") {
          const items = (getPath(data.values, f.path) as Record<string, unknown>[] | undefined) ?? [];
          return (
            <fieldset key={f.path}>
              <legend>{f.label}</legend>
              <ul className="list-plain">
                {items.map((it, i) => <li key={i}>{f.fields.map((x) => toDisplay(x, it[x.path])).map((x) => (Array.isArray(x) ? x.join(", ") : String(x))).filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}</li>)}
              </ul>
              <Text role="body-sm" muted>Edit this list in settings/{kind}.json for now.</Text>
            </fieldset>
          );
        }
        return (
          <Input key={f.path} label={f.label} error={err} type={isNumber(f) ? "number" : "text"} value={String(v)}
            help={f.help || (f.type === "string[]" ? "Separate with commas." : undefined)} onChange={(e) => change(f, e.target.value)} />
        );
      })}
      <div className="row">
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" loading={busy === "save"} disabled={!Object.keys(draft).length}>Save changes</Button>
        {Object.keys(draft).length ? <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => { setDraft({}); setErrors({}); }}>Discard</Button> : null}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Settings.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, Card, Input, Pill, Tabs, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import { SECRET_NAMES, type SecretName } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { FieldForm } from "../components/FieldForm";
import { Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { useThemePref, type ThemePref } from "../theme";

const SECRET_TEXT: Record<SecretName, string> = { gmail_app_password: "Gmail app password", anthropic_api_key: "Anthropic API key" };
const THEMES: [ThemePref, string][] = [["graphite", "Graphite (dark)"], ["paper", "Paper (light)"], ["system", "Follow the system"]];

export function SecretsPanel() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const status = useLoad((b) => Promise.all(SECRET_NAMES.map((n) => b.secrets.status(n))), [], []);
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const list = status.data;
  if (!list) return <Loading />;
  if (!list[0]?.available) return <Text role="body">Secure storage isn't available on this computer, so secrets can't be saved here. Keep using settings/.env for now.</Text>;

  const save = (n: SecretName) => run(`secret-${n}`, async () => {
    await bridge.secrets.set(n, values[n] ?? "");
    setValues((v) => ({ ...v, [n]: "" }));
    status.reload();
  }, `${SECRET_TEXT[n]} saved`);
  const remove = (n: SecretName) => run(`remove-${n}`, async () => { await bridge.secrets.remove(n); status.reload(); }, `${SECRET_TEXT[n]} removed`);

  return (
    <div className="stack">
      {list.map((s) => (
        <div key={s.name} className="row row--top">
          <div className="grow">
            <Input label={SECRET_TEXT[s.name]} type="password" autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} value={values[s.name] ?? ""}
              onChange={(e) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, [s.name]: e.target.value }))} />
          </div>
          <Pill tone={s.set ? "success" : "neutral"}>{s.set ? "Saved securely" : "Not set"}</Pill>
          <Button aria-label={`Save ${SECRET_TEXT[s.name]}`} disabled={!values[s.name]} loading={busy === `secret-${s.name}`} onClick={() => void save(s.name)}>Save</Button>
          {s.set ? <Button variant="ghost" aria-label={`Remove ${SECRET_TEXT[s.name]}`} onClick={() => void remove(s.name)}>Remove</Button> : null}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function SettingsScreen() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("profile");
  const [theme, setTheme] = useThemePref();
  const info = useLoad((b) => b.app.info(), [], []);
  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Settings" sub={info.data ? `CareerOS ${info.data.version}` : undefined} />
      <Tabs label="Settings sections" value={tab} onChange={setTab}
        tabs={[{ id: "profile", label: "Profile" }, { id: "search", label: "Search" }, { id: "connections", label: "Connections" }, { id: "appearance", label: "Appearance" }]}>
        {tab === "profile" ? <FieldForm kind="profile" /> : null}
        {tab === "search" ? <FieldForm kind="search" /> : null}
        {tab === "connections" ? (
          <div className="stack">
            <Card className="stack"><Text role="heading-2">Secrets</Text><SecretsPanel /></Card>
            <Card className="stack"><Text role="heading-2">Integrations</Text><FieldForm kind="integrations" /></Card>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {tab === "appearance" ? (
          <fieldset>
            <legend>Theme</legend>
            <div className="stack">
              {THEMES.map(([value, label]) => (
                <label key={value} className="checkbox">
                  <input type="radio" name="theme" value={value} checked={theme === value} onChange={() => setTheme(value)} /> {label}
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </fieldset>
        ) : null}
      </Tabs>
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 27: Privacy screen (locks, allow-once requests, grants, activity)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Privacy.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/privacy.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `privacy.get/setLocked/allow/deny/revoke`; `Dialog`, `Select`, `Pill`; `LOCKABLE_DIRS`.
- Produces: `PrivacyScreen()`; `ALLOW_MINUTES = [15, 30, 60, 120]`.

Ruling: unlocking a folder always asks first; locking never does. An allow-once grant is time-boxed (the services re-lock it on expiry, Task 32); the screen shows when it ends and offers "Re-lock now".

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { PrivacyScreen } from "../src/screens/Privacy";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

const calls = (b: MockBridge, m: string) => b.calls.filter((c) => c.method === m).map((c) => c.args);

describe("Privacy", () => {
  it("shows both folders locked", async () => {
    renderApp(<PrivacyScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("switch", { name: "Lock me/identity" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true");
    expect(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "Lock me/employment" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true");
  });
  it("asks before unlocking, and locks again without asking", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<PrivacyScreen />);
    const sw = await screen.findByRole("switch", { name: "Lock me/employment" });
    await userEvent.click(sw);
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole("dialog")).getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel" }));
    expect(calls(bridge, "privacy.setLocked")).toEqual([]);
    await userEvent.click(sw);
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole("dialog")).getByRole("button", { name: "Unlock" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(sw).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "false"));
    await userEvent.click(sw);
    await waitFor(() => expect(sw).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true"));
    expect(calls(bridge, "privacy.setLocked")).toEqual([["me/employment", false], ["me/employment", true]]);
  });
  it("allows a request once for the chosen time, then re-locks on demand", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<PrivacyScreen />);
    const req = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Access request" });
    expect(req).toHaveTextContent("Need your visa expiry date for the Acme Corp cover letter");

    await userEvent.click(within(req).getByRole("button", { name: "Allow once" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/me\/identity is open until/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Access request" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls(bridge, "privacy.allow")[0]?.[1]).toBe(30);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Re-lock me/identity now" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText(/me\/identity is open until/)).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Activity" })).toHaveTextContent("Re-locked");
  });
  it("denies a request", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<PrivacyScreen />);
    await userEvent.click(within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Access request" })).getByRole("button", { name: "Deny" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Access request" })).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(calls(bridge, "privacy.deny")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(calls(bridge, "privacy.allow")).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Privacy.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, Card, Dialog, Pill, Select, Text } from "@careeros/brand";
import { LOCKABLE_DIRS, type AccessRequestView, type LockableDir } from "@careeros/contract";
import { useAction, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { fmtDateTime, fmtTime } from "../format";

export const ALLOW_MINUTES = [15, 30, 60, 120];
const FOLDER_TEXT: Record<LockableDir, string> = {
  "me/identity": "Passport, visa and other identity documents.",
  "me/employment": "Payslips, contracts and references.",
};

export function PrivacyScreen() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const { data: p, error } = useLoad((b) => b.privacy.get(), [], ["privacy"]);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState<LockableDir | null>(null);
  if (error && !p) return <ErrorState title="Privacy settings can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!p) return <Loading />;

  const setLocked = (d: LockableDir, locked: boolean) => run(`lock-${d}`, () => bridge.privacy.setLocked(d, locked), locked ? `${d} is locked` : `${d} is unlocked`);
  const pending = p.requests.filter((r) => r.status === "pending");

  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Privacy" sub="Locked folders are hidden from Claude Code. You decide when it may look inside." />
      <Card className="stack">
        <Text role="heading-2">Locked folders</Text>
        {LOCKABLE_DIRS.map((d) => {
          const locked = p.locked.includes(d);
          const grant = p.grants.find((g) => g.folder === d);
          return (
            <div key={d} className="row row--between">
              <span className="grow"><Text role="body" as="div">{d}</Text><Text role="body-sm" muted as="div">{FOLDER_TEXT[d]}</Text></span>
              {grant ? <Pill tone="warning">Open once</Pill> : null}
              <button type="button" role="switch" className="switch" aria-checked={locked} aria-label={`Lock ${d}`} disabled={busy === `lock-${d}`}
                onClick={() => (locked ? setConfirm(d) : void setLocked(d, true))} />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </Card>

      {pending.map((r) => <RequestCard key={r.id} r={r} />)}

      {p.grants.length ? (
        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="heading-2">Open now</Text>
          {p.grants.map((g) => (
            <div key={g.folder} className="row row--between">
              <Text role="body">{g.folder} is open until {fmtTime(g.until)}</Text>
              <Button size="sm" aria-label={`Re-lock ${g.folder} now`} onClick={() => void run(`revoke-${g.folder}`, () => bridge.privacy.revoke(g.folder), `${g.folder} is locked again`)}>Re-lock now</Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      <Card className="stack">
        <Text role="heading-2">Activity</Text>
        {p.activity.length === 0 ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>Nothing yet.</Text> : (
          <ul className="list-plain timeline" aria-label="Activity">
            {p.activity.map((a, i) => <li key={`${a.at}-${i}`}><Text role="body-sm" muted>{fmtDateTime(a.at)}</Text><span>{a.event}{a.detail ? ` · ${a.detail}` : ""}</span></li>)}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Card>

      <Dialog open={confirm !== null} onClose={() => setConfirm(null)} title={`Unlock ${confirm ?? ""}?`}
        actions={<>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirm(null)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => { const d = confirm!; setConfirm(null); void setLocked(d, false); }}>Unlock</Button>
        </>}>
        <Text role="body">Claude Code will be able to read this folder until you lock it again.</Text>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}

function RequestCard({ r }: { r: AccessRequestView }) {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const { busy, run } = useAction();
  const [minutes, setMinutes] = useState(ALLOW_MINUTES.includes(r.minutes) ? r.minutes : 15);
  return (
    <Card highlight aria-label="Access request" className="stack">
      <Text role="label">{r.who === "claude" ? "Claude" : r.who} asks to read {r.folder}</Text>
      <Text role="body">{r.why}</Text>
      {r.field ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>Only needs: {r.field}</Text> : null}
      <div className="row">
        <Select label="Allow for" value={String(minutes)} onChange={(e) => setMinutes(Number(e.target.value))}
          options={ALLOW_MINUTES.map((m) => ({ value: String(m), label: `${m} minutes` }))} />
        <Button variant="primary" loading={busy === "allow"} onClick={() => void run("allow", () => bridge.privacy.allow(r.id, minutes), `${r.folder} is open for ${minutes} minutes`)}>Allow once</Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" loading={busy === "deny"} onClick={() => void run("deny", () => bridge.privacy.deny(r.id), "Request denied")}>Deny</Button>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

### Task 28: Approvals screen (approve once, decline without executing)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Approvals.tsx`, `packages/app-ui/test/approvals.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/app-ui/src/screens/registry.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `approvals.list/approve/decline`; `Dialog`, `Textarea`, `Pill`; `errorText`.
- Produces: `ApprovalsScreen()`.

This is the renderer half of Review Focus item 3 (double Approve). The screen keeps a per-id "started" set, so a second click before the first call returns is ignored; the services half (Task 32) refuses a second approve on disk.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`packages/app-ui/test/approvals.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MockBridge, loadFixture } from "../src/mock/MockBridge";
import { ApprovalsScreen } from "../src/screens/Approvals";
import { renderApp } from "./render";

const SLUG = "example-labs";
const ID = "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm";

describe("Approvals", () => {
  it("shows what approving will do", async () => {
    renderApp(<ApprovalsScreen />);
    const card = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Move Example Labs to Interview" });
    expect(within(card).getByText("From Claude")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(card).getByRole("list", { name: "What will happen" })).toHaveTextContent("Set the job status to Interview");
  });

  it("runs an approval exactly once, even on a double click", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const real = b.approvals.approve;
    let count = 0;
    b.approvals.approve = (id) => { count++; return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r(real(id)), 30)); };
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderApp(<ApprovalsScreen />, { bridge: b });
    await user.dblClick(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "History" })).toHaveTextContent("Done"));
    expect(count).toBe(1);
    expect((await b.jobs.get(SLUG, ID)).job.status).toBe("Interview");
    expect(screen.queryByText(/can't become approved/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("declines with a note and changes nothing", async () => {
    const { bridge } = renderApp(<ApprovalsScreen />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Decline" }));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Reason (optional)"), "Not yet");
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole("dialog")).getByRole("button", { name: "Decline" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "History" })).toHaveTextContent("Declined"));
    expect(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "History" })).toHaveTextContent("Not yet");
    expect((await bridge.jobs.get(SLUG, ID)).job.status).toBe("Found");
  });

  it("can't approve email sends yet", async () => {
    const f = loadFixture();
    f.approvals.push({ ...f.approvals[0]!, id: "20260115t090000-abc123", action: "email_send", title: "Send follow-up to Acme Corp", status: "pending" });
    renderApp(<ApprovalsScreen />, { bridge: new MockBridge({ fixture: f }) });
    const card = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Send follow-up to Acme Corp" });
    expect(within(card).getByRole("button", { name: "Approve" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(within(card).getByText("Sending email arrives in a later update.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("lets you retry after a failure", async () => {
    const b = new MockBridge();
    const real = b.approvals.approve;
    let fail = true;
    b.approvals.approve = (id) => (fail ? (fail = false, Promise.reject(new Error("disk is full"))) : real(id));
    renderApp(<ApprovalsScreen />, { bridge: b });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("disk is full")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "History" })).toHaveTextContent("Done"));
  });
});
```
