- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/app-ui/src/screens/Approvals.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Check, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Dialog, Pill, Text, Textarea, useToast, type PillTone } from "@careeros/brand";
import type { ApprovalStatus, ApprovalView } from "@careeros/contract";
import { errorText, useBridge, useLoad } from "../bridge-context";
import { ErrorState, Loading, ScreenHead } from "../components/States";
import { fmtDateTime } from "../format";

const FROM: Record<ApprovalView["created_by"], string> = { claude: "From Claude", app: "From you", cli: "From the CLI" };
const STATUS: Record<ApprovalStatus, { text: string; tone: PillTone }> = {
  pending: { text: "Waiting", tone: "accent" }, approved: { text: "Running", tone: "accent" }, done: { text: "Done", tone: "success" },
  declined: { text: "Declined", tone: "neutral" }, failed: { text: "Failed", tone: "danger" },
};

export function ApprovalsScreen() {
  const bridge = useBridge();
  const toast = useToast();
  const { data, error } = useLoad((b) => b.approvals.list(), [], ["approvals"]);
  const started = useRef(new Set<string>());
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [declining, setDeclining] = useState<ApprovalView | null>(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");

  // One decision per approval: later clicks are ignored until the first call fails.
  const decide = async (id: string, fn: () => Promise<ApprovalView>, ok: string) => {
    if (started.current.has(id)) return;
    started.current.add(id);
    setBusyId(id);
    try {
      const a = await fn();
      toast(a.status === "failed" ? { tone: "danger", text: `It didn't finish: ${a.result_note}` } : { tone: "success", text: ok });
    } catch (e) {
      started.current.delete(id);
      toast({ tone: "danger", text: errorText(e) });
    } finally {
      setBusyId(null);
    }
  };

  if (error && !data) return <ErrorState title="Approvals can't load" text={error} />;
  if (!data) return <Loading />;
  const pending = data.filter((a) => a.status === "pending");
  const history = data.filter((a) => a.status !== "pending").sort((a, b) => (b.decided_at || b.created_at).localeCompare(a.decided_at || a.created_at));

  return (
    <>
      <ScreenHead title="Approvals" sub="Nothing that acts on your behalf runs until you approve it here." />
      <div className="stack">
        {pending.length === 0 ? <Text role="body" muted>Nothing is waiting for you.</Text> : pending.map((a) => {
          const blocked = a.action === "email_send";
          return (
            <Card key={a.id} highlight aria-label={a.title} className="stack">
              <div className="row row--between"><Text role="heading-2">{a.title}</Text><Pill>{FROM[a.created_by]}</Pill></div>
              {a.summary ? <Text role="body" muted>{a.summary}</Text> : null}
              <Text role="label">What will happen</Text>
              <ol aria-label="What will happen">{a.steps.map((s) => <li key={s}>{s}</li>)}</ol>
              {blocked ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>Sending email arrives in a later update.</Text> : null}
              <div className="row">
                <Button variant="primary" icon={Check} disabled={blocked} loading={busyId === a.id}
                  onClick={() => void decide(a.id, () => bridge.approvals.approve(a.id), "Approved and done")}>Approve</Button>
                <Button variant="ghost" icon={X} disabled={busyId === a.id} onClick={() => { setNote(""); setDeclining(a); }}>Decline</Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          );
        })}

        <Card className="stack">
          <Text role="heading-2">History</Text>
          {history.length === 0 ? <Text role="body-sm" muted>No decisions yet.</Text> : (
            <ul className="list-plain" aria-label="History">
              {history.map((a) => (
                <li key={a.id} className="row">
                  <Pill tone={STATUS[a.status].tone}>{STATUS[a.status].text}</Pill>
                  <span className="grow"><Text role="body" truncate as="div">{a.title}</Text>{a.result_note ? <Text role="body-sm" muted as="div">{a.result_note}</Text> : null}</span>
                  <Text role="body-sm" muted>{fmtDateTime(a.decided_at || a.created_at)}</Text>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Dialog open={declining !== null} onClose={() => setDeclining(null)} title="Decline this?"
        actions={<>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeclining(null)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => { const a = declining!; setDeclining(null); void decide(a.id, () => bridge.approvals.decline(a.id, note.trim()), "Declined"); }}>Decline</Button>
        </>}>
        <Textarea label="Reason (optional)" value={note} maxLength={500} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} />
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}
```

In `registry.tsx`: add `import { ApprovalsScreen } from "./Approvals";` and change `approvals: Pending` to `approvals: ApprovalsScreen`. Every entry is now a real screen, so delete the `Pending` function and its `Text` import.

## Group D: shell-neutral Node services (`@careeros/services`, spec §5-6)

These modules do all file work for the app. They know nothing about Electron: the shell passes in a `Host` (folder pickers, opening links, secure storage, app state), and the shell's IPC layer calls `dispatch(method, args)`. Every handler validates nothing itself; validation happens once, in the dispatcher, with the contract's `validateArgs` (Task 33). Handlers are still safe to call directly in tests because the core functions they use check their own inputs.

### Task 29: Services package, host interfaces, context and workspace lifecycle

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/services/package.json`, `packages/services/tsconfig.json`, `packages/services/src/host.ts`, `packages/services/src/fakes.ts`, `packages/services/src/context.ts`, `packages/services/src/handlers.ts`, `packages/services/src/workspace.ts`, `packages/services/src/index.ts`, `packages/services/test/helpers.ts`, `packages/services/test/workspace.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: core `initWorkspace`, `isWorkspace`, `migrate`, `buildIndex`, `importLegacy`, `readMeta`, `readRecord`, `layout`, `revokeAllGrants`, `logActivity`; contract types.
- Produces:
  - `interface SecretStore { available(): boolean; has(name: SecretName): boolean; get(name: SecretName): string | null; set(name: SecretName, value: string): void; remove(name: SecretName): void }`
  - `interface AppState { lastWorkspace: string | null; recent: string[] }`, `interface AppStateStore { read(): AppState; write(s: AppState): void }`
  - `interface ShellHost { pickFolder(purpose: "create" | "open" | "legacy"): Promise<string | null>; pickFile(): Promise<string | null>; openExternal(url: string): Promise<boolean>; showItemInFolder(absPath: string): void; openClaudeDesktop(): Promise<boolean> }`
  - `interface Host { secrets: SecretStore; state: AppStateStore; shell: ShellHost; info: AppInfo; now?: () => Date }`
  - Fakes: `memorySecrets(available?)`, `memoryState(initial?)`, `fakeShell(opts?)` (records `opened`, `shown`; returns queued `picks` / `files`), `fakeHost(over?)`
  - `class ServiceContext { host; root: string | null; now(): Date; clock(): Clock; requireRoot(): string; setRoot(root: string | null): void; onOpen(fn: (root: string) => (() => void) | void): () => void; emit(...areas: ChangeArea[]): void; onChange(cb): () => void }`
  - `type Handler = (ctx: ServiceContext, ...args: any[]) => unknown`; `type HandlerTable = Record<MethodName, Handler>`
  - `workspaceHandlers: Pick<HandlerTable, "app.info" | "workspace.*">`, plus `openWorkspace(ctx, dir)`, `createWorkspace(ctx, dir, name)`, `closeWorkspace(ctx)`, `shutdownWorkspace(ctx)` (quit: re-lock but keep `lastWorkspace`), `restoreLast(ctx)`, `previewLegacy(ctx, src, dest)`, `importLegacyAndOpen(ctx, src, dest)`
  - Test helpers: `FIXED`, `tmpDir()`, `sampleCopy()`, `makeCtx(opts?)`, `openSample(opts?)`

Ruling (Review Focus 2): allow-once grants never outlive an app session. Opening a workspace revokes every grant still on disk (the app may have quit, crashed or slept while one was open) and logs "Re-locked"; closing does the same. Core already ignores expired grants, so a grant left behind by a crash is never honoured even before the app opens again.

`packages/services/src/host.ts`:

```ts
import type { AppInfo, SecretName } from "@careeros/contract";

/** OS secure storage (Keychain, DPAPI, libsecret). Values never travel to the renderer. */
export interface SecretStore {
  available(): boolean;
  has(name: SecretName): boolean;
  get(name: SecretName): string | null;
  set(name: SecretName, value: string): void;
  remove(name: SecretName): void;
}
export interface AppState { lastWorkspace: string | null; recent: string[] }
export interface AppStateStore { read(): AppState; write(s: AppState): void }
export interface ShellHost {
  pickFolder(purpose: "create" | "open" | "legacy"): Promise<string | null>;
  pickFile(): Promise<string | null>;
  /** Shows the full address and opens it in the system browser only if the person agrees. */
  openExternal(url: string): Promise<boolean>;
  showItemInFolder(absPath: string): void;
  openClaudeDesktop(): Promise<boolean>;
}
export interface Host { secrets: SecretStore; state: AppStateStore; shell: ShellHost; info: AppInfo; now?: () => Date }
```

`packages/services/src/fakes.ts`:

```ts
import type { SecretName } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { AppState, AppStateStore, Host, SecretStore, ShellHost } from "./host.js";

export function memorySecrets(available = true): SecretStore & { values: Map<SecretName, string> } {
  const values = new Map<SecretName, string>();
  const need = () => { if (!available) throw new Error("Secure storage isn't available on this computer, so the secret was not saved."); };
  return {
    values,
    available: () => available,
    has: (n) => values.has(n),
    get: (n) => values.get(n) ?? null,
    set: (n, v) => { need(); values.set(n, v); },
    remove: (n) => { values.delete(n); },
  };
}

export function memoryState(initial: Partial<AppState> = {}): AppStateStore & { value: AppState } {
  const store = {
    value: { lastWorkspace: null, recent: [], ...initial } as AppState,
    read: () => structuredClone(store.value),
    write: (s: AppState) => { store.value = structuredClone(s); },
  };
  return store;
}

export interface FakeShell extends ShellHost { picks: (string | null)[]; files: (string | null)[]; opened: string[]; shown: string[]; claude: boolean }

export function fakeShell(opts: { picks?: (string | null)[]; files?: (string | null)[]; claude?: boolean } = {}): FakeShell {
  const s: FakeShell = {
    picks: [...(opts.picks ?? [])], files: [...(opts.files ?? [])], opened: [], shown: [], claude: opts.claude ?? false,
    pickFolder: async () => s.picks.shift() ?? null,
    pickFile: async () => s.files.shift() ?? null,
    openExternal: async (url) => { s.opened.push(url); return true; },
    showItemInFolder: (p) => { s.shown.push(p); },
    openClaudeDesktop: async () => s.claude,
  };
  return s;
}

export type FakeHost = Host & { shell: FakeShell; secrets: ReturnType<typeof memorySecrets>; state: ReturnType<typeof memoryState> };

export function fakeHost(over: Partial<FakeHost> = {}): FakeHost {
  return {
    secrets: memorySecrets(), state: memoryState(), shell: fakeShell(),
    info: { version: "0.1.0", shell: "electron", platform: "test" }, ...over,
  };
}
```

`packages/services/src/context.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock } from "@careeros/core";
import type { ChangeArea, ChangeEvent } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { Host } from "./host.js";

type OpenHook = (root: string) => (() => void) | void;

/** Per-window state: the host, the open workspace, change listeners, and per-workspace hooks (timers, watcher). */
export class ServiceContext {
  root: string | null = null;
  private listeners = new Set<(e: ChangeEvent) => void>();
  private openHooks = new Set<OpenHook>();
  private closers: (() => void)[] = [];

  constructor(readonly host: Host) {}

  now(): Date { return this.host.now?.() ?? new Date(); }
  clock(): Clock { return { now: this.now() }; }
  requireRoot(): string {
    if (!this.root) throw new Error("No workspace is open.");
    return this.root;
  }

  /** Switches workspace: stops the old workspace's hooks, then starts every hook for the new one. */
  setRoot(root: string | null): void {
    for (const close of this.closers.splice(0)) { try { close(); } catch { /* a failing closer must not block the switch */ } }
    this.root = root;
    if (root) for (const hook of this.openHooks) { const c = hook(root); if (c) this.closers.push(c); }
  }

  onOpen(fn: OpenHook): () => void {
    this.openHooks.add(fn);
    if (this.root) { const c = fn(this.root); if (c) this.closers.push(c); }
    return () => { this.openHooks.delete(fn); };
  }

  emit(...areas: ChangeArea[]): void {
    if (!areas.length) return;
    const e = { areas: [...new Set(areas)] };
    for (const l of [...this.listeners]) l(e);
  }

  onChange(cb: (e: ChangeEvent) => void): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(cb);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(cb); };
  }
}
```

`packages/services/src/handlers.ts`:

```ts
import type { MethodName } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";

// Arguments are already validated by the dispatcher (contract METHODS), so handlers take them as typed values.
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
export type Handler = (ctx: ServiceContext, ...args: any[]) => unknown;
export type HandlerTable = Record<MethodName, Handler>;
```

`packages/services/test/helpers.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { ServiceContext } from "../src/context.js";
import { fakeHost, fakeShell, memorySecrets, type FakeHost } from "../src/fakes.js";
import { openWorkspace } from "../src/workspace.js";

export const FIXED = new Date("2026-01-15T09:00:00.000Z");
export const SAMPLE = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../../../samples/alex-example");

export function tmpDir(): string {
  return fs.realpathSync.native(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "careeros-svc-")));
}

export function sampleCopy(): string {
  const dest = path.join(tmpDir(), "CareerOS");
  fs.cpSync(SAMPLE, dest, { recursive: true });
  return dest;
}

export interface CtxOpts { now?: Date; picks?: (string | null)[]; files?: (string | null)[]; secretsAvailable?: boolean; claude?: boolean }

export function makeCtx(opts: CtxOpts = {}): { ctx: ServiceContext; host: FakeHost; setNow(d: Date): void } {
  let now = opts.now ?? FIXED;
  const host = fakeHost({
    now: () => now,
    shell: fakeShell({ picks: opts.picks, files: opts.files, claude: opts.claude }),
    secrets: memorySecrets(opts.secretsAvailable ?? true),
  });
  return { ctx: new ServiceContext(host), host, setNow: (d) => { now = d; } };
}

export function openSample(opts: CtxOpts = {}) {
  const x = makeCtx(opts);
  openWorkspace(x.ctx, sampleCopy());
  return { ...x, root: x.ctx.requireRoot() };
}

export const minutes = (n: number) => new Date(FIXED.getTime() + n * 60_000);
```

`packages/services/test/workspace.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { addGrant, readActivity, readMeta } from "@careeros/core";
import {
  closeWorkspace, createWorkspace, openWorkspace, previewLegacy, restoreLast, workspaceHandlers,
} from "../src/workspace.js";
import { FIXED, makeCtx, minutes, sampleCopy, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

describe("workspace lifecycle", () => {
  it("creates a workspace in a new folder and remembers it", () => {
    const { ctx, host } = makeCtx();
    const dir = path.join(tmpDir(), "CareerOS");
    const s = createWorkspace(ctx, dir, "Alex Example");
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ root: dir, name: "Alex Example", migrated: [], indexSkipped: 0 });
    expect(s.dataVersion).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    const deny = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, ".claude", "settings.json"), "utf8")).permissions.deny as string[];
    expect(deny).toContain("Read(./me/identity/**)");
    expect(host.state.value).toEqual({ lastWorkspace: dir, recent: [dir] });
    expect(workspaceHandlers["workspace.current"](ctx)).toMatchObject({ root: dir });
  });

  it("refuses a folder with files in it, and folders that aren't workspaces", () => {
    const { ctx } = makeCtx();
    const dir = tmpDir();
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, "notes.txt"), "x");
    expect(() => createWorkspace(ctx, dir, "Alex Example")).toThrow(/isn't empty/);
    expect(() => openWorkspace(ctx, dir)).toThrow(/isn't a CareerOS workspace/);
    expect(ctx.root).toBeNull();
  });

  it("re-locks grants left over from the last session when it opens (Review Focus 2)", () => {
    const root = sampleCopy();
    addGrant(root, "me/identity", 60, "20260115t085500-aaaaaa", { now: FIXED });
    const { ctx } = makeCtx({ now: minutes(5) });
    openWorkspace(ctx, root);
    expect(readMeta(root).privacy.grants).toEqual([]);
    const deny = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), "utf8")).permissions.deny as string[];
    expect(deny).toContain("Read(./me/identity/**)");
    expect(readActivity(root)[0]).toMatchObject({ event: "Re-locked", detail: "me/identity (app opened)" });
  });

  it("re-locks grants when it closes", () => {
    const root = sampleCopy();
    const { ctx } = makeCtx();
    openWorkspace(ctx, root);
    addGrant(root, "me/employment", 30, "20260115t090000-bbbbbb", ctx.clock());
    closeWorkspace(ctx);
    expect(readMeta(root).privacy.grants).toEqual([]);
    expect(ctx.root).toBeNull();
    expect(workspaceHandlers["workspace.current"](ctx)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("runs open hooks for each workspace and their cleanups on switch", () => {
    const { ctx } = makeCtx();
    const stop = vi.fn();
    const start = vi.fn(() => stop);
    ctx.onOpen(start);
    openWorkspace(ctx, sampleCopy());
    expect(start).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    closeWorkspace(ctx);
    expect(stop).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("forgets a last workspace that no longer exists", () => {
    const { ctx, host } = makeCtx();
    host.state.write({ lastWorkspace: path.join(tmpDir(), "gone"), recent: [] });
    expect(restoreLast(ctx)).toBeNull();
    expect(host.state.value.lastWorkspace).toBeNull();
  });

  it("previews a legacy import without writing anything", () => {
    const { ctx } = makeCtx();
    const src = tmpDir();
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(src, "Notes"));
    const dest = path.join(tmpDir(), "CareerOS");
    expect(previewLegacy(ctx, src, dest)).toMatchObject({ unmapped: ["Notes"] });
    expect(fs.existsSync(dest)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/services/src/workspace.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  buildIndex, importLegacy, initWorkspace, isWorkspace, layout, logActivity, migrate, readMeta, readRecord, revokeAllGrants,
} from "@careeros/core";
import type { LegacyPreview, WorkspaceSummary } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";

const IGNORABLE = new Set([".DS_Store", "Thumbs.db", "desktop.ini"]);

function summary(root: string, migrated: number[], indexSkipped: number): WorkspaceSummary {
  const meta = readMeta(root);
  return { root, name: readRecord("profile", layout(root).profile).contact.name, dataVersion: meta.data_version, migrated, indexSkipped };
}

function remember(ctx: ServiceContext, root: string | null): void {
  const s = ctx.host.state.read();
  ctx.host.state.write({ lastWorkspace: root, recent: root ? [root, ...s.recent.filter((r) => r !== root)].slice(0, 5) : s.recent });
}

/** Ends every allow-once grant and logs it. Used on open and on close. */
function relockAll(ctx: ServiceContext, root: string, why: string): void {
  for (const g of revokeAllGrants(root, ctx.clock())) logActivity(root, "Re-locked", `${g.folder} (${why})`, ctx.clock());
}

export function openWorkspace(ctx: ServiceContext, dir: string): WorkspaceSummary {
  if (!path.isAbsolute(dir) || !fs.existsSync(dir) || !isWorkspace(dir)) {
    throw new Error("That folder isn't a CareerOS workspace. Choose the folder that holds workspace.json.");
  }
  const root = fs.realpathSync.native(dir);
  if (ctx.root && ctx.root !== root) closeWorkspace(ctx);
  const { applied } = migrate(root, undefined, ctx.clock());
  relockAll(ctx, root, "app opened");
  const report = buildIndex(root);
  ctx.setRoot(root);
  remember(ctx, root);
  return summary(root, applied, report.skipped.length);
}

export function createWorkspace(ctx: ServiceContext, dir: string, name: string): WorkspaceSummary {
  if (fs.existsSync(dir)) {
    const st = fs.lstatSync(dir);
    if (st.isSymbolicLink() || !st.isDirectory()) throw new Error("Choose a folder, not a file or a link.");
    if (fs.readdirSync(dir).some((n) => !IGNORABLE.has(n))) throw new Error("That folder isn't empty. Choose an empty folder or make a new one.");
  }
  initWorkspace(dir, { name: name.trim(), now: ctx.now() });
  return openWorkspace(ctx, dir);
}

export function closeWorkspace(ctx: ServiceContext): void {
  if (ctx.root) relockAll(ctx, ctx.root, "app closed");
  ctx.setRoot(null);
  remember(ctx, null);
}

/** App quit: re-lock and stop the workspace's hooks, but remember it for the next launch. */
export function shutdownWorkspace(ctx: ServiceContext): void {
  if (ctx.root) relockAll(ctx, ctx.root, "app closed");
  ctx.setRoot(null);
}

/** Opens the workspace from the last session, or returns null (and forgets it) when that fails. */
export function restoreLast(ctx: ServiceContext): WorkspaceSummary | null {
  const last = ctx.host.state.read().lastWorkspace;
  if (!last) return null;
  try { return openWorkspace(ctx, last); } catch { remember(ctx, null); return null; }
}

function toPreview(r: ReturnType<typeof importLegacy>): LegacyPreview {
  return { actions: r.actions.map(({ kind, from, to, note }) => ({ kind, from, to, ...(note ? { note } : {}) })), unmapped: r.unmapped, skipped: r.skipped, issues: r.issues.map((i) => ({ file: i.file, problems: i.problems })) };
}

export function previewLegacy(ctx: ServiceContext, src: string, dest: string): LegacyPreview {
  return toPreview(importLegacy(src, dest, { dryRun: true, now: ctx.now() }));
}

export function importLegacyAndOpen(ctx: ServiceContext, src: string, dest: string): LegacyPreview {
  if (fs.existsSync(dest) && !isWorkspace(dest) && fs.readdirSync(dest).some((n) => !IGNORABLE.has(n))) {
    throw new Error("Import into an empty folder or an existing CareerOS workspace.");
  }
  const report = toPreview(importLegacy(src, dest, { now: ctx.now() }));
  openWorkspace(ctx, dest);
  return report;
}

export const workspaceHandlers = {
  "app.info": (ctx: ServiceContext) => ctx.host.info,
  "workspace.current": (ctx: ServiceContext) => (ctx.root ? summary(ctx.root, [], 0) : null),
  "workspace.pickFolder": (ctx: ServiceContext, purpose: "create" | "open" | "legacy") => ctx.host.shell.pickFolder(purpose),
  "workspace.create": createWorkspace,
  "workspace.open": openWorkspace,
  "workspace.previewLegacy": previewLegacy,
  "workspace.importLegacy": importLegacyAndOpen,
  "workspace.close": closeWorkspace,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

### Task 30: Read models, job edits and settings (live eligibility, broken job files)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/services/src/read.ts`, `packages/services/src/settings.ts`, `packages/services/test/read.test.ts`, `packages/services/test/settings.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/services/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: core `openIndex`, `buildIndex`, `refreshIndex`, `jobFilePath`, `readRecord`, `updateRecord`, `setStatus`, `addEvent`, `listInbox`, `suggestInboxTarget`, `listApprovals`, `listAccessRequests`, `readSearches`, `fieldsFor`, `insideRoot`; contract `jobRowFromRecord`, `isEligible`, `applyJobQuery`, `homeSummary`, `gapsFrom`, `fileKind`.
- Produces:
  - `jobRows(root): JobRow[]` (from the index `json` column; eligibility computed from the current profile on every call)
  - `jobDetail(root, slug, id): JobDetail`, `inboxItems(root): InboxItem[]`, `approvalView(a): ApprovalView`, `requestView(r): AccessRequestView`, `jobDirRel(slug, id): string`, `touch(root, ...relPaths)` (refreshes the index for paths a handler changed)
  - `readHandlers` for `home.summary`, `jobs.list/get/setStatus/addNote/views/saveView/deleteView`, `sponsors.list`, `searches.list`
  - `settingsHandlers` for `settings.get/save`; `settingsFile(root, kind)`

Ruling (Review Focus 4): a job whose files were deleted or broken by hand never crashes a screen. The index skips it (`refreshIndex` reports it), list views simply don't show it, and `jobs.get` throws a plain message that the Job screen turns into "This job can't be shown" (Task 20). Ruling (Review Focus 5): eligibility is not stored; every read computes it from `settings/profile.json`, so a work-rights change in Settings is reflected in the very next call, and the dispatcher's change event (Task 33) makes every open screen reload.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/services/src/read.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  addEvent, buildIndex, insideRoot, jobFilePath, layout, listAccessRequests, listApprovals, listInbox, openIndex, readRecord,
  readSearches, refreshIndex, setStatus, suggestInboxTarget, updateRecord, type AccessRequest, type Approval,
} from "@careeros/core";
import {
  applyJobQuery, fileKind, gapsFrom, homeSummary, isEligible, jobRowFromRecord,
  type AccessRequestView, type ApprovalView, type HomeSummary, type InboxItem, type JobDetail, type JobFile, type JobList, type JobQuery,
  type JobRecord, type JobRow, type JobStatus, type SavedView, type SearchList, type SponsorRow, type WorkRightsStatus,
} from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";

export const jobDirRel = (slug: string, id: string) => `companies/${slug}/jobs/${id}`;

/** Refreshes the index for paths a handler just changed, so the next read sees them before the watcher fires. */
export function touch(root: string, ...relPaths: string[]): void {
  refreshIndex(root, relPaths);
}

function profileStatus(root: string): WorkRightsStatus {
  return readRecord("profile", layout(root).profile).work_rights.status as WorkRightsStatus;
}

function query<T>(root: string, sql: string): T[] {
  const run = () => {
    const db = openIndex(root);
    try { return db.prepare(sql).all() as T[]; } finally { db.close(); }
  };
  try { return run(); } catch { buildIndex(root); return run(); }
}

export function jobRows(root: string): JobRow[] {
  const status = profileStatus(root);
  return query<{ json: string; company_name: string }>(root, "SELECT json, company_name FROM jobs").map((r) => {
    const j = JSON.parse(r.json) as JobRecord;
    return jobRowFromRecord(j, r.company_name, isEligible(status, j.work_rights.label));
  });
}

/** A regular file inside the job folder (no symlinks), or null. */
function fileStat(root: string, abs: string): fs.Stats | null {
  try {
    const st = fs.lstatSync(abs);
    return st.isFile() && insideRoot(root, abs) ? st : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

function readIfFile(root: string, abs: string): string {
  return fileStat(root, abs) ? fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8") : "";
}

export function jobDetail(root: string, slug: string, id: string): JobDetail {
  const file = jobFilePath(root, slug, id);
  const job = readRecord("job", file) as unknown as JobRecord;
  const dir = path.dirname(file);
  const base = jobDirRel(slug, id);
  let company: JobDetail["company"] = null;
  try {
    const c = readRecord("company", layout(root).companyFile(slug));
    company = { name: c.name, slug: c.slug, website: c.website, sponsor_status: c.sponsor.status };
  } catch { company = null; }
  const files: JobFile[] = job.files.map((f) => {
    const st = fileStat(root, path.join(dir, f.path));
    return { path: f.path, rel: `${base}/${f.path}`, label: f.label, at: f.at, exists: !!st, size: st ? st.size : null, kind: fileKind(f.path) };
  });
  return {
    job, company, description: readIfFile(root, path.join(dir, "jd.md")), scoreReport: readIfFile(root, path.join(dir, "score.md")),
    files, gaps: gapsFrom(job), eligible: isEligible(profileStatus(root), job.work_rights.label),
  };
}

export function inboxItems(root: string): InboxItem[] {
  const dir = layout(root).inbox;
  return listInbox(root).map((name) => ({ name, size: fs.lstatSync(path.join(dir, name)).size, suggestion: suggestInboxTarget(root, name) }));
}

export function approvalView(a: Approval): ApprovalView {
  return {
    id: a.id, action: a.action, title: a.title, summary: a.summary, steps: a.steps, payload: a.payload, created_at: a.created_at,
    created_by: a.created_by, status: a.status, decided_at: a.decided_at, result_note: a.result_note,
  };
}

export function requestView(r: AccessRequest): AccessRequestView {
  return {
    id: r.id, folder: r.folder, field: r.field, why: r.why, who: r.who, minutes: r.minutes, created_at: r.created_at,
    status: r.status, decided_at: r.decided_at, expires_at: r.expires_at,
  };
}

function views(root: string): SavedView[] {
  return (readRecord("search", layout(root).search).views ?? []) as SavedView[];
}
function writeViews(root: string, fn: (v: SavedView[]) => SavedView[]): SavedView[] {
  return (updateRecord("search", layout(root).search, (s) => { s.views = fn((s.views ?? []) as SavedView[]); }).views ?? []) as SavedView[];
}

function home(ctx: ServiceContext): HomeSummary {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const profile = readRecord("profile", layout(root).profile);
  return homeSummary({
    name: profile.contact.name, rows: jobRows(root),
    events: query<{ at: string; event: string }>(root, "SELECT at, event FROM events"),
    visaExpiry: profile.work_rights.visa_expiry, approvals: listApprovals(root).map(approvalView),
    requests: listAccessRequests(root).map(requestView), inbox: inboxItems(root), now: ctx.now(),
  });
}

function sponsors(ctx: ServiceContext): SponsorRow[] {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const L = layout(root);
  const rows = jobRows(root);
  const out: SponsorRow[] = [];
  const dir = L.companiesDir;
  for (const slug of fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).sort() : []) {
    let c;
    try { c = readRecord("company", L.companyFile(slug)); } catch { continue; }
    const mine = rows.filter((r) => r.company_slug === slug);
    const scores = mine.map((r) => r.sponsor).filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
    out.push({
      name: c.name, slug, status: c.sponsor.status, last_checked: c.sponsor.last_checked, jobs: mine.length, score: scores.length ? Math.max(...scores) : null,
      evidence: c.sponsor.evidence.map((e) => ({ date: e.date, note: e.note, url: e.url })),
    });
  }
  for (const e of readRecord("sponsors", L.sponsors).employers as Record<string, unknown>[]) {
    const name = String(e.name ?? "");
    if (name && !out.some((r) => r.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
      out.push({ name, slug: null, status: String(e.status ?? "unknown"), evidence: [], last_checked: String(e.last_checked ?? ""), score: null, jobs: 0 });
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

function searches(ctx: ServiceContext): SearchList {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const cutoff = new Date(ctx.now().getTime() - 7 * 86_400_000).toISOString();
  const rows = readSearches(root).entries.map((s) => ({ ...s, recent: s.at >= cutoff })).sort((a, b) => b.at.localeCompare(a.at));
  return { rows, sites: [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.site))].sort() };
}

export const readHandlers = {
  "home.summary": home,
  "jobs.list": (ctx: ServiceContext, q: JobQuery): JobList => applyJobQuery(jobRows(ctx.requireRoot()), q),
  "jobs.get": (ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string) => jobDetail(ctx.requireRoot(), slug, id),
  "jobs.setStatus": (ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string, status: JobStatus, note: string) => {
    const root = ctx.requireRoot();
    setStatus(root, slug, id, status, note, ctx.clock());
    touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
    return jobDetail(root, slug, id);
  },
  "jobs.addNote": (ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string, note: string) => {
    const root = ctx.requireRoot();
    addEvent(root, slug, id, "Note", note, ctx.clock());
    touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
    return jobDetail(root, slug, id);
  },
  "jobs.views": (ctx: ServiceContext) => views(ctx.requireRoot()),
  "jobs.saveView": (ctx: ServiceContext, name: string, q: JobQuery) =>
    writeViews(ctx.requireRoot(), (v) => [...v.filter((x) => x.name !== name), { name, query: q }].slice(-50)),
  "jobs.deleteView": (ctx: ServiceContext, name: string) => writeViews(ctx.requireRoot(), (v) => v.filter((x) => x.name !== name)),
  "sponsors.list": sponsors,
  "searches.list": searches,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

`views` on `SearchSettings` comes from Task 1 (`search.views`). `readRecord("job", ...)` returns core's `Job`, which matches `JobRecord` plus `schema` and `custom`; the cast goes through `unknown` because the contract's `JobRecord` doesn't declare those two keys.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/services/src/settings.ts`**

```ts
import { fieldsFor, layout, readRecord, updateRecord } from "@careeros/core";
import type { SettingsChange, SettingsData, SettingsKind } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { touch } from "./read.js";

export function settingsFile(root: string, kind: SettingsKind): string {
  const L = layout(root);
  return kind === "profile" ? L.profile : kind === "search" ? L.search : L.integrations;
}

function setPath(obj: Record<string, unknown>, dotted: string, value: unknown): void {
  const keys = dotted.split(".");
  let o = obj;
  for (const k of keys.slice(0, -1)) {
    const next = Object.hasOwn(o, k) ? o[k] : undefined;
    if (typeof next !== "object" || next === null || Array.isArray(next)) o[k] = {};
    o = o[k] as Record<string, unknown>;
  }
  o[keys.at(-1)!] = value;
}

function get(ctx: ServiceContext, kind: SettingsKind): SettingsData {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  return { kind, fields: fieldsFor(kind), values: readRecord(kind, settingsFile(root, kind)) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> };
}

/** Writes only fields the schema labels as editable leaves. The store validates the whole record before writing. */
function save(ctx: ServiceContext, kind: SettingsKind, changes: SettingsChange[]): SettingsData {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const editable = new Set(fieldsFor(kind).filter((f) => f.type !== "object" && f.type !== "object[]").map((f) => f.path));
  for (const c of changes) if (!editable.has(c.path)) throw new Error(`${c.path} can't be changed here`);
  const file = settingsFile(root, kind);
  updateRecord(kind, file, (cur) => { for (const c of changes) setPath(cur as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, c.path, c.value); });
  // A profile change can change eligibility; settings/ paths trigger a full index rebuild.
  touch(root, `settings/${kind}.json`);
  return get(ctx, kind);
}

export const settingsHandlers = {
  "settings.get": get,
  "settings.save": save,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

```ts
export * from "./settings.js";
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `packages/services/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: core `insideRoot`, `lockedFolderFor`, `uniquePath`, `fileInboxItem`, `attachFile`, `setStatus`, `proposeApproval`, `listApprovals`, `jobFilePath`, `layout`; contract `applyStep`, `buildApplyPrompt`, `fileKind`; `jobDetail`, `jobRows`, `inboxItems`, `approvalView`, `touch`, `jobDirRel` (Task 30).
- Produces:
  - `MAX_INCOMING_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024`, `MAX_TEXT_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024`
  - `resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, rel, use: "read" | "media" | "reveal"): string` (absolute real path, or throws)
  - `checkIncomingFile(root, abs): { abs: string; name: string; size: number }` (or throws)
  - `fileHandlers` (`files.readText/reveal/pick`), `inboxHandlers` (`inbox.list/add/file`, `jobs.attach`), `learnHandlers` (`learn.list`), `applyHandlers` (`apply.state/prepare/requestApproval/markApplied`), `applyState(ctx, slug, id): ApplyState`

Ruling (Review Focus 1): the renderer can only name workspace files by relative path, and every one goes through `resolveWorkspaceFile`: no segment may start with a dot (`settings/.env`, `.claude`, `.careeros`, `.index`, `.git`), the real path must stay inside the workspace (symlinks are resolved first), and locked folders are refused unless an allow-once grant is open, using core's `lockedFolderFor` so symlinks and case tricks are caught. Revealing a file in Finder is the person's own action, so it skips the lock check but keeps the others. Files coming in (drops, the picker, attachments) must be regular files, not symlinks, not hidden or inside hidden folders, at most 25 MB, and not already inside the workspace. A compromised renderer could still hand over the path of some other readable file the person owns; that is the limit of what the main process can check, and it is why every incoming file is copied (never moved) and shown in the inbox before it is filed.

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { addGrant, layout } from "@careeros/core";
import { checkIncomingFile, fileHandlers as F, resolveWorkspaceFile } from "../src/files.js";
import { inboxHandlers as I } from "../src/inbox.js";
import { learnHandlers } from "../src/learn.js";
import { openSample, tmpDir } from "./helpers.js";

function downloads() {
  const dir = tmpDir();
  const file = (name: string, text = "fictional") => { const p = path.join(dir, name); fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(p, text); return p; };
  return { dir, file };
}

describe("reading workspace files (Review Focus 1)", () => {
  it("reads normal files", async () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    expect(await F["files.readText"](ctx, "learning/rag-pipelines/guide.md")).toContain("Chunk the documents");
  });
  it("refuses dot segments, escapes and locked folders", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, "me", "identity", "visa"), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, "me", "identity", "visa", "grant.md"), "fictional grant");
    for (const rel of ["settings/.env", ".claude/settings.json", ".careeros/activity.jsonl", "../outside.md", "learning/../../x"]) {
      expect(() => resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, rel, "read"), rel).toThrow(/isn't available|outside/);
    }

  });
  it("follows symlinks before deciding", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    const outside = downloads().file("secret.md");
    fs.symlinkSync(outside, path.join(root, "learning", "rag-pipelines", "escape.md"));
    expect(() => resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, "learning/rag-pipelines/escape.md", "read")).toThrow(/outside/);
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, "me", "identity"), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, "me", "identity", "passport.md"), "fictional passport");
    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(root, "me", "identity", "passport.md"), path.join(root, "learning", "rag-pipelines", "notes.md"));
    expect(() => resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, "learning/rag-pipelines/notes.md", "read")).toThrow(/me\/identity is locked/);

    addGrant(root, "me/identity", 15, "20260115t090000-cccccc", ctx.clock());

  });
  it("refuses media that isn't media", () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();

  });
});

describe("files coming in (Review Focus 1)", () => {
  it("accepts a normal file and copies it into the inbox", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();

    expect(left).toEqual([]);

    expect(() => I["inbox.file"](ctx, "../workspace.json", { kind: "me", dir: "resumes" }, "")).toThrow(/not an inbox file name/);
  });
  it("attaches files with the same checks", () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    const d = downloads();
    const detail = I["jobs.attach"](ctx, "acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", d.file("offer.pdf"), "Offer", "emails");
    expect(detail.files.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ path: "emails/offer.pdf", label: "Offer", exists: true });
    expect(() => I["jobs.attach"](ctx, "acme-corp", "2026-01-12-ai-engineer", d.file(".env"), "x", "")).toThrow(/Hidden/);
  });
  it("asks the shell for a file", async () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample({ files: ["/fictional/alex-example/Downloads/cv.pdf"] });
    expect(await F["files.pick"](ctx)).toBe("/fictional/alex-example/Downloads/cv.pdf");
  });
});

describe("learn", () => {
  it("lists topics, videos and interview prep, and skips linked folders", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, "learning", "rag-pipelines", "demo.mp4"), "");

});
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { layout, listApprovals } from "@careeros/core";
import { applyHandlers as A } from "../src/apply.js";
import { openSample } from "./helpers.js";

const LABS = ["example-labs", "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm"] as const;

describe("apply flow", () => {
  it("prepares: writes PREPARE.md with the prompt and moves the job to Applying", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    const s = A["apply.prepare"](ctx, ...LABS);
    expect(s.step).toBe("prepare");
    const file = path.join(layout(root).jobDir(...LABS), "application", "PREPARE.md");
    expect(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")).toContain("Do not submit anything");
    expect(s.prompt).toContain(`companies/${LABS[0]}/jobs/${LABS[1]}`);
    expect(A["apply.state"](ctx, ...LABS).files.map((f) => f.path)).toEqual(["application/PREPARE.md"]);
  });

  it("won't ask for a hand-off before there is anything to hand off", () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    A["apply.prepare"](ctx, ...LABS);
    expect(() => A["apply.requestApproval"](ctx, ...LABS)).toThrow(/Nothing to hand off yet/);
  });

  it("reviews Claude's files and claims, then queues a hand-off approval", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    const app = path.join(layout(root).jobDir(...LABS), "application");
    fs.mkdirSync(app, { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(app, "resume.md"), "# Alex Example\n");

    expect(h.step).toBe("handoff");

    expect(a).toMatchObject({ created_by: "app", status: "pending", payload: { company_slug: LABS[0], id: LABS[1], files: ["application/claims.json", "application/resume.md"] } });
  });

  it("treats a broken claims file as no claims", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    const app = path.join(layout(root).jobDir(...LABS), "application");
    fs.mkdirSync(app, { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(app, "resume.md"), "x");
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(app, "claims.json"), "{ nope");
    expect(A["apply.state"](ctx, ...LABS).claims).toBeNull();
  });

  it("marks the job applied", () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    expect(A["apply.markApplied"](ctx, ...LABS).step).toBe("done");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/services/src/files.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { insideRoot, lockedFolderFor } from "@careeros/core";
import { fileKind } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";

export const MAX_INCOMING_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024;
export const MAX_TEXT_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
const MEDIA = new Set(["image", "video", "pdf"]);

/**
 * Turns a workspace-relative path from the renderer into a real absolute path, or throws.
 * "read": text files for display; "media": images, video and PDFs for the careeros-file: scheme;
 * "reveal": any file or folder, shown in the OS file manager (the person's own action, so locks don't apply).
 */
export function resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx: ServiceContext, rel: string, use: "read" | "media" | "reveal"): string {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const parts = rel.split("/");
  if (!rel || rel.includes("\\") || path.isAbsolute(rel) || parts.some((p) => p === "" || p === "." || p === ".." || p.startsWith("."))) {
    throw new Error(`"${rel}" isn't available to the app.`);
  }
  const abs = path.join(root, ...parts);
  if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) throw new Error(`"${rel}" can't be found. It may have been moved or deleted.`);
  if (!insideRoot(root, abs)) throw new Error(`"${rel}" points outside the workspace.`);
  const real = fs.realpathSync.native(abs);
  if (use !== "reveal") {
    const locked = lockedFolderFor(root, real, ctx.clock());
    if (locked) throw new Error(`${locked} is locked. Allow it once in Privacy first.`);
    const st = fs.statSync(real);
    if (!st.isFile()) throw new Error(`"${rel}" isn't a file.`);
    const kind = fileKind(real);
    if (use === "read" && (!(kind === "markdown" || kind === "text") || st.size > MAX_TEXT_BYTES)) throw new Error(`"${rel}" can't be shown as text.`);
    if (use === "media" && !MEDIA.has(kind)) throw new Error(`"${rel}" can't be shown here.`);
  }
  return real;
}

/** Checks a file the person dropped or picked, before anything is copied. */
export function checkIncomingFile(root: string, abs: string): { abs: string; name: string; size: number } {
  const name = path.basename(abs);
  if (!path.isAbsolute(abs)) throw new Error(`"${name}" can't be added: the app needs the full path.`);
  if (path.resolve(abs).split(path.sep).some((seg) => seg.startsWith("."))) throw new Error(`Hidden files and folders can't be added ("${name}").`);
  let st: fs.Stats;
  try { st = fs.lstatSync(abs); } catch { throw new Error(`"${name}" can't be found.`); }
  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error(`"${name}" is a link. Add the file it points to instead.`);
  if (!st.isFile()) throw new Error(`"${name}" isn't a regular file.`);
  if (st.size > MAX_INCOMING_BYTES) throw new Error(`"${name}" is larger than 25 MB.`);
  const real = fs.realpathSync.native(abs);
  const realRoot = fs.realpathSync.native(root);
  if (real === realRoot || real.startsWith(realRoot + path.sep)) throw new Error(`"${name}" is already in your workspace.`);
  return { abs: real, name, size: st.size };
}

export const fileHandlers = {
  "files.readText": (ctx: ServiceContext, rel: string) => fs.readFileSync(resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, rel, "read"), "utf8"),
  "files.reveal": (ctx: ServiceContext, rel: string) => { ctx.host.shell.showItemInFolder(resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, rel, "reveal")); },
  "files.pick": (ctx: ServiceContext) => ctx.host.shell.pickFile(),
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

The hidden-segment check runs on the whole absolute path, so a file under a hidden folder (for example a dotted config folder in the home directory) is refused even when its own name is plain. The sample temp directories created by `tmpDir()` never contain dot segments on macOS or Linux.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/services/src/inbox.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { attachFile, fileInboxItem, layout, uniquePath } from "@careeros/core";
import type { InboxTarget, JobSubdir } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import { checkIncomingFile } from "./files.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { inboxItems, jobDetail, jobDirRel, touch } from "./read.js";

function add(ctx: ServiceContext, paths: string[]) {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  // Check every file first, so one bad file adds nothing.
  const files = paths.map((p) => checkIncomingFile(root, p));
  const dir = layout(root).inbox;
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  for (const f of files) fs.copyFileSync(f.abs, uniquePath(path.join(dir, f.name)), fs.constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
  return inboxItems(root);
}

function file(ctx: ServiceContext, name: string, target: InboxTarget, note: string) {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  fileInboxItem(root, name, target, note, ctx.clock());
  if (target.kind === "job") touch(root, jobDirRel(target.company_slug, target.id));
  return inboxItems(root);
}

function attach(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string, src: string, label: string, subdir: JobSubdir) {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const f = checkIncomingFile(root, src);
  attachFile(root, slug, id, f.abs, { label, subdir }, ctx.clock());
  touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
  return jobDetail(root, slug, id);
}

export const inboxHandlers = {
  "inbox.list": (ctx: ServiceContext) => inboxItems(ctx.requireRoot()),
  "inbox.add": add,
  "inbox.file": file,
  "jobs.attach": attach,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/services/src/learn.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { layout } from "@careeros/core";
import { fileKind, type LearnData, type LearnItem } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { jobDirRel, jobRows } from "./read.js";

const HEAD_BYTES = 4096;

function realDirs(dir: string): string[] {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((n) => !n.startsWith(".")).filter((n) => {
    const st = fs.lstatSync(path.join(dir, n));
    return st.isDirectory() && !st.isSymbolicLink();
  }).sort();
}

function titleOf(file: string, fallback: string): string {
  const fd = fs.openSync(file, "r");
  try {
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(HEAD_BYTES);
    const n = fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, HEAD_BYTES, 0);
    return buf.subarray(0, n).toString("utf8").match(/^#\s+(.+)$/m)?.[1]?.trim() ?? fallback;
  } finally { fs.closeSync(fd); }
}

/** Guides (.md) and videos in one folder, as workspace-relative items. Links and hidden files are skipped. */
function items(root: string, rel: string): LearnItem[] {
  const dir = path.join(root, rel);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((n) => !n.startsWith(".")).sort().flatMap((n): LearnItem[] => {
    const p = path.join(dir, n);
    const st = fs.lstatSync(p);
    if (!st.isFile()) return [];
    const kind = fileKind(n);
    if (kind === "markdown") return [{ path: `${rel}/${n}`, title: titleOf(p, n), kind: "guide" }];
    if (kind === "video") return [{ path: `${rel}/${n}`, title: n, kind: "video" }];
    return [];
  });
}

function list(ctx: ServiceContext): LearnData {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const topics = realDirs(layout(root).learning).map((slug) => {
    const its = items(root, `learning/${slug}`);
    return { slug, title: its.find((i) => i.kind === "guide")?.title ?? slug, items: its };
  });
  const interviewPrep = jobRows(root).flatMap((r) => {
    const its = items(root, `${jobDirRel(r.company_slug, r.id)}/interview`);
    return its.length ? [{ company_slug: r.company_slug, id: r.id, title: r.title, company_name: r.company_name, items: its }] : [];
  }).sort((a, b) => a.company_name.localeCompare(b.company_name) || a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
  return { topics, interviewPrep };
}

export const learnHandlers = { "learn.list": list } satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `packages/services/src/apply.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { listApprovals, proposeApproval, setStatus, writeFileAtomic } from "@careeros/core";
import { applyStep, buildApplyPrompt, fileKind, type ApplyState, type ClaimLink, type JobFile } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { approvalView, jobDetail, jobDirRel, touch } from "./read.js";

function appFiles(root: string, slug: string, id: string): JobFile[] {
  const rel = `${jobDirRel(slug, id)}/application`;
  const dir = path.join(root, rel);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir) || fs.lstatSync(dir).isSymbolicLink()) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((n) => !n.startsWith(".")).sort().flatMap((n): JobFile[] => {
    const st = fs.lstatSync(path.join(dir, n));
    if (!st.isFile()) return [];
    return [{ path: `application/${n}`, rel: `${rel}/${n}`, label: n, at: st.mtime.toISOString(), exists: true, size: st.size, kind: fileKind(n) }];
  });
}

/** claims.json must be a list of { claim, source } strings; anything else counts as "no claims". */
function readClaims(root: string, slug: string, id: string): ClaimLink[] | null {
  const f = path.join(root, jobDirRel(slug, id), "application", "claims.json");
  try {
    if (fs.lstatSync(f).isSymbolicLink()) return null;
    const v = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8")) as unknown;
    if (!Array.isArray(v)) return null;
    return v.filter((c): c is ClaimLink => typeof c === "object" && c !== null && typeof c.claim === "string" && typeof c.source === "string")
      .map((c) => ({ claim: c.claim, source: c.source }));
  } catch { return null; }
}

export function applyState(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string): ApplyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const d = jobDetail(root, slug, id);
  const files = appFiles(root, slug, id);
  const approval = listApprovals(root).filter((a) => a.action === "apply_handoff" && a.payload.company_slug === slug && a.payload.id === id).map(approvalView).at(-1) ?? null;
  const company = d.company?.name ?? slug;
  return {
    company_slug: slug, id, title: d.job.title, company_name: company,
    url: [d.job.apply_via, d.job.url].find((u) => u.startsWith("https://")) ?? "",
    step: applyStep(d.job.status, files, approval), files, claims: readClaims(root, slug, id), approval,
    prompt: buildApplyPrompt(d.job.title, company, slug, id),
  };
}

function prepare(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string): ApplyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const s = applyState(ctx, slug, id);
  const file = path.join(root, jobDirRel(slug, id), "application", "PREPARE.md");
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) writeFileAtomic(file, `# Prepare this application\n\nPaste this into Claude:\n\n${s.prompt}\n`);
  const status = jobDetail(root, slug, id).job.status;
  if (status === "Found" || status === "Saved") setStatus(root, slug, id, "Applying", "Preparing the application", ctx.clock());
  touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
  return applyState(ctx, slug, id);
}

function requestApproval(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string): ApplyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const s = applyState(ctx, slug, id);
  const files = s.files.filter((f) => !f.path.endsWith("PREPARE.md"));
  if (!files.length) throw new Error("Nothing to hand off yet. Prepare the application first.");
  proposeApproval(root, {
    action: "apply_handoff", title: `Hand off application to ${s.company_name}`, summary: `${files.length} file(s) ready for ${s.title}.`,
    steps: [...(s.url ? [`Open ${s.url} in your browser`] : []), `Show the application folder (${files.length} files)`, 'Add a timeline event "Handed off"'],
    payload: { company_slug: slug, id, url: s.url, files: files.map((f) => f.path) }, created_by: "app",
  }, ctx.clock());
  return applyState(ctx, slug, id);
}

function markApplied(ctx: ServiceContext, slug: string, id: string): ApplyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  setStatus(root, slug, id, "Applied", "Marked applied in CareerOS", ctx.clock());
  touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
  return applyState(ctx, slug, id);
}

export const applyHandlers = {
  "apply.state": applyState,
  "apply.prepare": prepare,
  "apply.requestApproval": requestApproval,
  "apply.markApplied": markApplied,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

### Task 32: Privacy grants with timed re-lock, approval execution, secrets and links

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/services/src/privacy.ts`, `packages/services/src/approvals.ts`, `packages/services/src/secrets.ts`, `packages/services/test/privacy.test.ts`, `packages/services/test/approvals.test.ts`, `packages/services/test/secrets.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/services/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: core `readMeta`, `setLocked`, `addGrant`, `revokeGrant`, `expireGrants`, `readAccessRequest`, `listAccessRequests`, `decideAccessRequest`, `readApproval`, `setApprovalStatus`, `logActivity`, `readActivity`, `setStatus`, `addEvent`; `resolveWorkspaceFile` (Task 31); `approvalView`, `requestView`, `touch`, `jobDirRel` (Task 30).
- Produces:
  - `privacyState(ctx): PrivacyState`, `checkGrants(ctx): number` (expires due grants now; returns how many), `installGrantTimer(ctx): void`, `privacyHandlers`
  - `approvalHandlers` (`approvals.list/approve/decline`); approve returns the final `ApprovalView` (`done` or `failed`)
  - `secretHandlers` (`secrets.status/set/remove`), `linkHandlers` (`links.open`, `claude.openDesktop`), `isSafeLink(url): boolean`

Ruling (Review Focus 2, services half): every allow-once grant has a timer. When it fires, `expireGrants` re-writes `.claude/settings.json` with the folder denied again, the request becomes `expired`, the activity log says "Re-locked · <folder> (time is up)", and the app gets a `privacy` change event. Timers are capped at 60 seconds and re-armed, so a clock change or a laptop that slept past the deadline is caught within a minute of waking; the Electron shell also calls `checkGrants` on power resume (Task 34). If the app crashes while a grant is open, Claude's settings stay open until the app next starts (Task 29 re-locks on open); grants last at most 240 minutes. A Claude Code `SessionStart` hook that runs grant expiry would close that gap; it is listed as a follow-up for plan 3b rather than built here.

Ruling (Review Focus 3, services half): approving is a state change on disk before anything runs (`pending -> approved`, Task 8), done synchronously, so a second approve, from a double click, a second window or a retried IPC call, fails with "approval X is approved; it can't become approved" and nothing runs twice. A per-process "running" set guards the async part too. `email_send` is refused before any state change.

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { listAccessRequests, readMeta, requestAccess } from "@careeros/core";
import { checkGrants, installGrantTimer, privacyHandlers as P } from "../src/privacy.js";
import { FIXED, minutes, openSample } from "./helpers.js";

const deny = (root: string) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ".claude", "settings.json"), "utf8")).permissions.deny as string[];
const pendingId = (root: string) => listAccessRequests(root).find((r) => r.status === "pending")!.id;

describe("privacy", () => {
  beforeEach(() => { vi.useFakeTimers(); vi.setSystemTime(FIXED); });
  afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); });

  it("locks and unlocks folders and logs it", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    expect(P["privacy.setLocked"](ctx, "me/employment", false).locked).toEqual(["me/identity"]);
    expect(deny(root)).not.toContain("Read(./me/employment/**)");
    const s = P["privacy.setLocked"](ctx, "me/employment", true);
    expect(s.locked).toEqual(["me/identity", "me/employment"]);
    expect(s.activity.map((a) => a.event).slice(0, 2)).toEqual(["Locked", "Unlocked"]);
  });

  it("allows a request once, then re-locks when the time is up (Review Focus 2)", () => {
    const { ctx, root, setNow } = openSample();
    installGrantTimer(ctx);
    const seen: string[][] = [];
    ctx.onChange((e) => seen.push(e.areas));
    const id = pendingId(root);
    const s = P["privacy.allow"](ctx, id, 15);
    expect(s.grants).toEqual([{ folder: "me/identity", until: minutes(15).toISOString(), request_id: id }]);
    expect(deny(root)).not.toContain("Read(./me/identity/**)");
    setNow(minutes(16));
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(16 * 60_000);
    expect(readMeta(root).privacy.grants).toEqual([]);
    expect(deny(root)).toContain("Read(./me/identity/**)");
    expect(listAccessRequests(root).find((r) => r.id === id)?.status).toBe("expired");
    expect(P["privacy.get"](ctx).activity[0]).toMatchObject({ event: "Re-locked", detail: "me/identity (time is up)" });
    expect(seen).toContainEqual(["privacy", "home"]);
  });

  it("catches a grant that ran out while the computer slept", () => {
    const { ctx, root, setNow } = openSample();
    P["privacy.allow"](ctx, pendingId(root), 15);
    setNow(minutes(90));
    expect(checkGrants(ctx)).toBe(1);
    expect(deny(root)).toContain("Read(./me/identity/**)");
  });

  it("never shows an expired grant as open, even before the timer fires", () => {
    const { ctx, root, setNow } = openSample();
    P["privacy.allow"](ctx, pendingId(root), 15);
    setNow(minutes(20));
    expect(P["privacy.get"](ctx).grants).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("re-locks on demand and denies requests", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    const id = pendingId(root);
    P["privacy.allow"](ctx, id, 30);
    expect(P["privacy.revoke"](ctx, "me/identity").grants).toEqual([]);
    expect(listAccessRequests(root).find((r) => r.id === id)?.status).toBe("expired");
    const second = requestAccess(root, { folder: "me/employment", why: "Need a reference letter date" }, ctx.clock());
    P["privacy.deny"](ctx, second.id);
    expect(() => P["privacy.allow"](ctx, second.id, 15)).toThrow(/is denied; it can't become allowed/);
  });

  it("allows a request for a folder that is already unlocked without adding a grant", () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    P["privacy.setLocked"](ctx, "me/identity", false);
    expect(P["privacy.allow"](ctx, pendingId(root), 15).grants).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import {
  addGrant, decideAccessRequest, expireGrants, listAccessRequests, logActivity, readAccessRequest, readActivity, readMeta, revokeGrant, setLocked,
} from "@careeros/core";
import type { LockableDir, PrivacyState } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { requestView } from "./read.js";

const MAX_WAIT_MS = 60_000;
const timers = new WeakMap<ServiceContext, () => void>();

export function privacyState(ctx: ServiceContext): PrivacyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const meta = readMeta(root);
  const now = ctx.now().toISOString();
  return {
    locked: meta.privacy.locked,
    grants: meta.privacy.grants.filter((g) => g.until > now).map(({ folder, until, request_id }) => ({ folder, until, request_id })),
    requests: listAccessRequests(root).map(requestView).reverse(),
    activity: readActivity(root, 50),
  };
}

/** Marks the allowed request behind a grant as expired. A request that is already final is left alone. */
function expireRequest(ctx: ServiceContext, root: string, id: string): void {
  try { if (readAccessRequest(root, id).status === "allowed") decideAccessRequest(root, id, "expired", {}, ctx.clock()); } catch { /* request file gone */ }
}

/** Ends every grant whose time is up. Returns how many ended. */
export function checkGrants(ctx: ServiceContext): number {
  const root = ctx.root;
  if (!root) return 0;
  const gone = expireGrants(root, ctx.clock());
  for (const g of gone) {
    logActivity(root, "Re-locked", `${g.folder} (time is up)`, ctx.clock());
    expireRequest(ctx, root, g.request_id);
  }
  if (gone.length) ctx.emit("privacy", "home");
  timers.get(ctx)?.();
  return gone.length;
}

/** Keeps one timer per context aimed at the next grant end, re-armed at least every minute. */
export function installGrantTimer(ctx: ServiceContext): void {
  let handle: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  const arm = () => {
    if (handle) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = undefined;
    const root = ctx.root;
    if (!root) return;
    const next = readMeta(root).privacy.grants.map((g) => Date.parse(g.until)).sort((a, b) => a - b)[0];
    if (next === undefined) return;
    const wait = Math.min(Math.max(0, next - ctx.now().getTime()) + 250, MAX_WAIT_MS);
    handle = setTimeout(() => { handle = undefined; checkGrants(ctx); arm(); }, wait);
    handle.unref?.();
  };
  timers.set(ctx, arm);
  ctx.onOpen(() => { arm(); return () => { if (handle) clearTimeout(handle); handle = undefined; }; });
}

const rearm = (ctx: ServiceContext) => timers.get(ctx)?.();

function allow(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string, minutes: number): PrivacyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const r = readAccessRequest(root, id);
  if (r.status !== "pending") throw new Error(`request ${id} is ${r.status}; it can't become allowed`);
  decideAccessRequest(root, id, "allowed", { minutes }, ctx.clock());
  // A folder that is already unlocked needs no grant; the request is still recorded as allowed.
  if (readMeta(root).privacy.locked.includes(r.folder)) addGrant(root, r.folder, minutes, id, ctx.clock());
  logActivity(root, "Allowed once", `${r.folder} for ${minutes} minutes`, ctx.clock());
  rearm(ctx);
  return privacyState(ctx);
}

function deny(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string): PrivacyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const r = decideAccessRequest(root, id, "denied", {}, ctx.clock());
  logActivity(root, "Denied", r.folder, ctx.clock());
  return privacyState(ctx);
}

function revoke(ctx: ServiceContext, folder: LockableDir): PrivacyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const ids = readMeta(root).privacy.grants.filter((g) => g.folder === folder).map((g) => g.request_id);
  if (revokeGrant(root, folder, ctx.clock())) {
    for (const id of ids) expireRequest(ctx, root, id);
    logActivity(root, "Re-locked", folder, ctx.clock());
  }
  rearm(ctx);
  return privacyState(ctx);
}

function lock(ctx: ServiceContext, folder: LockableDir, locked: boolean): PrivacyState {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  setLocked(root, folder, locked, ctx.clock());
  logActivity(root, locked ? "Locked" : "Unlocked", folder, ctx.clock());
  rearm(ctx);
  return privacyState(ctx);
}

export const privacyHandlers = {
  "privacy.get": privacyState,
  "privacy.setLocked": lock,
  "privacy.allow": allow,
  "privacy.deny": deny,
  "privacy.revoke": revoke,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/services/src/approvals.ts`**

```ts
import { addEvent, listApprovals, logActivity, readApproval, setApprovalStatus, setStatus, type Approval } from "@careeros/core";
import type { ApprovalView, JobStatus } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import { resolveWorkspaceFile } from "./files.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { approvalView, jobDirRel, touch } from "./read.js";
import { isSafeLink } from "./secrets.js";

const running = new Set<string>();

async function execute(ctx: ServiceContext, a: Approval): Promise<string> {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const p = a.payload as { company_slug?: string; id?: string; status?: JobStatus; note?: string; url?: string };
  const slug = String(p.company_slug ?? "");
  const id = String(p.id ?? "");
  if (a.action === "status_change") {
    setStatus(root, slug, id, String(p.status), p.note ?? "", ctx.clock());
    touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
    return `Status set to ${p.status}`;
  }
  if (a.action === "apply_handoff") {
    const url = typeof p.url === "string" && isSafeLink(p.url) ? p.url : "";
    const dir = resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, `${jobDirRel(slug, id)}/application`, "reveal");
    if (url) await ctx.host.shell.openExternal(url);
    ctx.host.shell.showItemInFolder(dir);
    addEvent(root, slug, id, "Handed off", url, ctx.clock());
    touch(root, jobDirRel(slug, id));
    return url ? "Opened the apply page and the files" : "Opened the files";
  }
  throw new Error(`can't run "${a.action}" yet`);
}

async function approve(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string): Promise<ApprovalView> {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const key = `${root}\u0000${id}`;
  if (running.has(key)) throw new Error(`approval ${id} is already running`);
  running.add(key);
  try {
    const a = readApproval(root, id);
    if (a.action === "email_send") throw new Error("Sending email arrives in a later update.");
    // Synchronous claim on disk: a second approve now fails with "is approved; it can't become approved".
    setApprovalStatus(root, id, "approved", "", ctx.clock());
    try {
      const note = await execute(ctx, a);
      setApprovalStatus(root, id, "done", note, ctx.clock());
      logActivity(root, "Approved", a.title, ctx.clock());
    } catch (e) {
      setApprovalStatus(root, id, "failed", (e as Error).message.split("\n")[0]!.slice(0, 300), ctx.clock());
      logActivity(root, "Approval failed", a.title, ctx.clock());
    }
    return approvalView(readApproval(root, id));
  } finally {
    running.delete(key);
  }
}

function decline(ctx: ServiceContext, id: string, note: string): ApprovalView {
  const root = ctx.requireRoot();
  const a = setApprovalStatus(root, id, "declined", note, ctx.clock());
  logActivity(root, "Declined", a.title, ctx.clock());
  return approvalView(a);
}

export const approvalHandlers = {
  "approvals.list": (ctx: ServiceContext) => listApprovals(ctx.requireRoot()).map(approvalView),
  "approvals.approve": approve,
  "approvals.decline": decline,
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/services/src/secrets.ts`**

```ts
import type { SecretName, SecretStatus } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";

const status = (ctx: ServiceContext, name: SecretName): SecretStatus => ({ name, available: ctx.host.secrets.available(), set: ctx.host.secrets.has(name) });

export const secretHandlers = {
  "secrets.status": status,
  "secrets.set": (ctx: ServiceContext, name: SecretName, value: string) => {
    if (!ctx.host.secrets.available()) throw new Error("Secure storage isn't available on this computer, so the secret was not saved.");
    ctx.host.secrets.set(name, value);
    return status(ctx, name);
  },
  "secrets.remove": (ctx: ServiceContext, name: SecretName) => { ctx.host.secrets.remove(name); return status(ctx, name); },
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;

/** Plain https links only: no other scheme, no user name or password, a real host. */
export function isSafeLink(url: string): boolean {
  let u: URL;
  try { u = new URL(url); } catch { return false; }
  return u.protocol === "https:" && !u.username && !u.password && u.hostname.length > 0;
}

export const linkHandlers = {
  "links.open": async (ctx: ServiceContext, url: string) => {
    if (!isSafeLink(url)) return false;
    return ctx.host.shell.openExternal(new URL(url).href);
  },
  "claude.openDesktop": (ctx: ServiceContext) => ctx.host.shell.openClaudeDesktop(),
} satisfies Partial<HandlerTable>;
```

`new URL("https://jobs.example.com/acme/1").href` is the same string, so the fake shell records exactly what the test expects.

```ts
export * from "./approvals.js";
export * from "./secrets.js";
```

### Task 33: Dispatcher, file watcher and runtime

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/services/src/dispatch.ts`, `packages/services/src/watcher.ts`, `packages/services/src/runtime.ts`, `packages/services/test/dispatch.test.ts`, `packages/services/test/watcher.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/services/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every handler table (Tasks 29-32); contract `validateArgs`, `METHODS`, `CHANGE_AREAS`; core `refreshIndex`.
- Produces:
  - `HANDLERS: HandlerTable`, `MUTATION_AREAS: Partial<Record<MethodName, ChangeArea[]>>`, `dispatch(ctx, method: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown>`
  - `ignoredPath(rel): boolean`, `areasFor(rels: string[]): ChangeArea[]`, `startWatcher(ctx, root, opts?: { debounceMs?: number }): () => void`
  - `interface Runtime { ctx; dispatch(method: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown>; onChange(cb): () => void; checkGrants(): number; resolveMedia(rel: string): string; shutdown(): void }`, `createRuntime(host: Host, opts?: { watch?: boolean; debounceMs?: number }): Runtime`

Ruling: validation happens in exactly one place, `dispatch`, before any handler runs; a handler never sees raw renderer input. Mutations announce the areas they touch right away; the watcher also announces outside edits (Claude Code writing files, the CLI, a text editor) after a 300 ms debounce, refreshing the index first so the screens read fresh data. The watcher ignores `.index/`, `.backup/` and `.git/`, so the app's own index writes never loop.

`packages/services/test/dispatch.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ArgError, METHODS } from "@careeros/contract";
import { addGrant, readMeta } from "@careeros/core";
import { createRuntime } from "../src/runtime.js";
import { dispatch, HANDLERS, MUTATION_AREAS } from "../src/dispatch.js";
import { fakeHost, memoryState } from "../src/fakes.js";
import { openSample, sampleCopy } from "./helpers.js";

describe("dispatch", () => {
  it("has exactly one handler per contract method", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(HANDLERS).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(METHODS).sort());
    for (const m of Object.keys(MUTATION_AREAS)) expect(METHODS).toHaveProperty([m]);
  });
  it("validates before any handler runs", async () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    await expect(dispatch(ctx, "fs.readFile", ["/etc/passwd"])).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ArgError);
    await expect(dispatch(ctx, "jobs.get", ["../etc", "x"])).rejects.toThrow(/invalid format/);
    await expect(dispatch(ctx, "files.readText", ["settings/../.env"])).rejects.toThrow(ArgError);
    await expect(dispatch(ctx, "settings.save", ["profile", [{ path: "__proto__.x", value: 1 }]])).rejects.toThrow(/not allowed/);
  });
  it("announces mutations, not reads", async () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    const seen: string[][] = [];
    ctx.onChange((e) => seen.push(e.areas));
    await dispatch(ctx, "jobs.list", [{}]);
    expect(seen).toEqual([]);
    await dispatch(ctx, "jobs.setStatus", ["example-labs", "2026-01-12-backend-engineer-llm", "Saved", ""]);
    expect(seen).toEqual([["jobs", "home"]]);
  });
  it("passes handler errors through with their message", async () => {
    const { ctx } = openSample();
    await expect(dispatch(ctx, "jobs.get", ["acme-corp", "2026-01-01-deleted"])).rejects.toThrow("no job acme-corp/2026-01-01-deleted");
  });
});

describe("runtime", () => {
  it("restores the last workspace and re-locks grants on shutdown, keeping it for next time", async () => {
    const root = sampleCopy();
    const state = memoryState({ lastWorkspace: root });
    const rt = createRuntime(fakeHost({ state }), { watch: false });
    expect(await rt.dispatch("workspace.current", [])).toMatchObject({ name: "Alex Example" });
    addGrant(rt.ctx.requireRoot(), "me/identity", 30, "20260115t090000-dddddd");
    rt.shutdown();
    expect(readMeta(root).privacy.grants).toEqual([]);
    expect(rt.ctx.root).toBeNull();
    expect(state.value.lastWorkspace).toBe(fs.realpathSync.native(root));
  });
  it("serves media only from allowed places", () => {
    const rt = createRuntime(fakeHost({ state: memoryState({ lastWorkspace: sampleCopy() }) }), { watch: false });
    expect(() => rt.resolveMedia("learning/rag-pipelines/guide.md")).toThrow(/can't be shown here/);
    expect(() => rt.resolveMedia("settings/.env")).toThrow(/isn't available/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { newJob } from "@careeros/core";
import { readHandlers } from "../src/read.js";
import { areasFor, ignoredPath, startWatcher } from "../src/watcher.js";
import { openSample } from "./helpers.js";

describe("watcher rules", () => {
  it("ignores the app's own index writes and editor litter", () => {
    for (const p of [".index/careeros.sqlite", ".index/careeros.sqlite-journal", ".backup/x", ".git/HEAD", "learning/.DS_Store", "inbox/x.pdf~", "companies/a/jobs/b/job.json.123.tmp"]) expect(ignoredPath(p), p).toBe(true);
    for (const p of ["companies/a/jobs/b/job.json", "inbox/cv.pdf", ".careeros/approvals/x.json"]) expect(ignoredPath(p), p).toBe(false);
  });
  it("maps changed paths to screen areas", () => {
    expect(areasFor(["companies/acme-corp/jobs/x/job.json"])).toEqual(["jobs", "home", "sponsors", "learn"]);
    expect(areasFor(["inbox/cv.pdf"])).toEqual(["inbox", "home"]);
    expect(areasFor([".careeros/approvals/a.json"])).toEqual(["approvals", "home"]);
    expect(areasFor([".careeros/requests/r.json", ".careeros/activity.jsonl"])).toEqual(["privacy", "home"]);
    expect(areasFor(["settings/profile.json"])).toEqual(["settings", "jobs", "home"]);
    expect(areasFor(["knowledge/search-log.jsonl"])).toEqual(["searches"]);
    expect(areasFor(["learning/rag/guide.md"])).toEqual(["learn"]);
    expect(areasFor([""])).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(areasFor(["notes.txt"])).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("watcher", () => {
  it("refreshes the index and announces changes made outside the app", async () => {
    const { ctx, root } = openSample();
    const seen: string[][] = [];
    ctx.onChange((e) => seen.push(e.areas));
    const stop = startWatcher(ctx, root, { debounceMs: 50 });
    try {
      newJob(root, { company: "Example Labs", title: "Applied AI Engineer", url: "https://jobs.example.com/labs/9" });
      await vi.waitFor(() => expect(seen.flat()).toContain("jobs"), { timeout: 5000, interval: 50 });
      expect(readHandlers["jobs.list"](ctx, { text: "Applied AI" }).rows).toHaveLength(1);
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, "inbox", "offer.pdf"), "fictional");
      await vi.waitFor(() => expect(seen.flat()).toContain("inbox"), { timeout: 5000, interval: 50 });
    } finally {
      stop();
    }
  });
});
```
