```ts
import { CHANGE_AREAS, validateArgs, type ChangeArea, type MethodName } from "@careeros/contract";
import { applyHandlers } from "./apply.js";
import { approvalHandlers } from "./approvals.js";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import { fileHandlers } from "./files.js";
import type { HandlerTable } from "./handlers.js";
import { inboxHandlers } from "./inbox.js";
import { learnHandlers } from "./learn.js";
import { privacyHandlers } from "./privacy.js";
import { readHandlers } from "./read.js";
import { linkHandlers, secretHandlers } from "./secrets.js";
import { settingsHandlers } from "./settings.js";
import { workspaceHandlers } from "./workspace.js";

export const HANDLERS: HandlerTable = {
  ...workspaceHandlers, ...readHandlers, ...settingsHandlers, ...fileHandlers, ...inboxHandlers, ...learnHandlers,
  ...applyHandlers, ...privacyHandlers, ...approvalHandlers, ...secretHandlers, ...linkHandlers,
};

const ALL = [...CHANGE_AREAS];

export const MUTATION_AREAS: Partial<Record<MethodName, ChangeArea[]>> = {
  "workspace.create": ALL, "workspace.open": ALL, "workspace.importLegacy": ALL, "workspace.close": ["workspace"],
  "jobs.setStatus": ["jobs", "home"], "jobs.addNote": ["jobs"], "jobs.attach": ["jobs"], "jobs.saveView": ["jobs"], "jobs.deleteView": ["jobs"],
  "apply.prepare": ["jobs", "home"], "apply.requestApproval": ["approvals", "home", "jobs"], "apply.markApplied": ["jobs", "home"],
  "inbox.add": ["inbox", "home"], "inbox.file": ["inbox", "home", "jobs"],
  "settings.save": ["settings", "jobs", "home"],
  "privacy.setLocked": ["privacy"], "privacy.allow": ["privacy", "home"], "privacy.deny": ["privacy", "home"], "privacy.revoke": ["privacy"],
  "approvals.approve": ["approvals", "jobs", "home"], "approvals.decline": ["approvals", "home"],
};

/** The only entry point from a shell. Validates, runs, then announces what changed. */
export async function dispatch(ctx: ServiceContext, method: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
  const clean = validateArgs(method, args);
  const result = await HANDLERS[method as MethodName](ctx, ...clean);
  const areas = MUTATION_AREAS[method as MethodName];
  if (areas) ctx.emit(...areas);
  return result;
}
```

`HandlerTable` is a full `Record<MethodName, Handler>`, so the spread fails to typecheck if any method is missing a handler; the first dispatch test checks the same at runtime.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/services/src/watcher.ts`**

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { refreshIndex } from "@careeros/core";
import { CHANGE_AREAS, type ChangeArea } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";

const IGNORED_TOP = new Set([".index", ".backup", ".git", ".claude"]);

export function ignoredPath(rel: string): boolean {
  const parts = rel.split("/");
  const base = parts.at(-1) ?? "";
  return IGNORED_TOP.has(parts[0]!) || base === ".DS_Store" || base === "Thumbs.db" || base.endsWith("~") || /\.\d+\.tmp$|\.tmp$/.test(base) || base.endsWith("-journal");
}

export function areasFor(rels: string[]): ChangeArea[] {
  const out = new Set<ChangeArea>();
  for (const rel of rels) {
    const [top, second] = rel.split("/");
    if (rel === "" || rel === "." || rel === "workspace.json") return [...CHANGE_AREAS];
    if (top === "settings") ["settings", "jobs", "home"].forEach((a) => out.add(a as ChangeArea));
    else if (top === "companies") ["jobs", "home", "sponsors", "learn"].forEach((a) => out.add(a as ChangeArea));
    else if (top === "inbox") ["inbox", "home"].forEach((a) => out.add(a as ChangeArea));
    else if (top === "learning") out.add("learn");
    else if (top === "knowledge") out.add(second === "sponsors.json" ? "sponsors" : "searches");
    else if (top === ".careeros" && second === "approvals") ["approvals", "home"].forEach((a) => out.add(a as ChangeArea));
    else if (top === ".careeros") ["privacy", "home"].forEach((a) => out.add(a as ChangeArea));
  }
  return [...out];
}

/** Watches the whole workspace (recursive fs.watch: macOS, Windows, Linux on Node 20+). */
export function startWatcher(ctx: ServiceContext, root: string, opts: { debounceMs?: number } = {}): () => void {
  const wait = opts.debounceMs ?? 300;
  const pending = new Set<string>();
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

  const flush = () => {
    timer = undefined;
    const rels = [...pending];
    pending.clear();
    if (ctx.root !== root) return;
    try { refreshIndex(root, rels.includes("") ? ["."] : rels); } catch { /* the next read rebuilds the index */ }
    const areas = areasFor(rels);
    if (areas.length) ctx.emit(...areas);
  };

  const watcher = fs.watch(root, { recursive: true }, (_event, file) => {
    const rel = file ? file.toString().split(path.sep).join("/") : "";
    if (rel && ignoredPath(rel)) return;
    pending.add(rel);
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(flush, wait);
  });
  // If the OS drops the watcher (folder moved, too many files), fall back to a full refresh once.
  watcher.on("error", () => { pending.add(""); if (!timer) timer = setTimeout(flush, wait); });

  return () => { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); timer = undefined; watcher.close(); };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/services/src/runtime.ts`**

```ts
import type { ChangeEvent } from "@careeros/contract";
import { ServiceContext } from "./context.js";
import { dispatch } from "./dispatch.js";
import { resolveWorkspaceFile } from "./files.js";
import type { Host } from "./host.js";
import { checkGrants, installGrantTimer } from "./privacy.js";
import { startWatcher } from "./watcher.js";
import { restoreLast, shutdownWorkspace } from "./workspace.js";

export interface Runtime {
  ctx: ServiceContext;
  dispatch(method: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
  onChange(cb: (e: ChangeEvent) => void): () => void;
  checkGrants(): number;
  resolveMedia(rel: string): string;
  shutdown(): void;
}

/** One per app window. Restores the last workspace, keeps grant timers and the watcher running for it. */
export function createRuntime(host: Host, opts: { watch?: boolean; debounceMs?: number } = {}): Runtime {
  const ctx = new ServiceContext(host);
  installGrantTimer(ctx);
  if (opts.watch !== false) ctx.onOpen((root) => startWatcher(ctx, root, { debounceMs: opts.debounceMs }));
  restoreLast(ctx);
  return {
    ctx,
    dispatch: (method, args) => dispatch(ctx, method, args),
    onChange: (cb) => ctx.onChange(cb),
    checkGrants: () => checkGrants(ctx),
    resolveMedia: (rel) => resolveWorkspaceFile(ctx, rel, "media"),
    shutdown: () => shutdownWorkspace(ctx),
  };
}
```

## Group E: Electron shell (`@careeros/app-electron`, spec §2, §6)

The shell is thin: it hardens the window, serves the built renderer over `app://`, serves workspace media over `careeros-file:`, forwards one IPC channel to `runtime.dispatch`, and implements `Host` with Electron APIs. The renderer still only sees `window.careerosTransport`, which `createRemoteBridge` (Task 14) turns into the same `CareerosBridge` the MockBridge implements.

### Task 34: Electron main process, preload, protocols and hardening

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/app-electron/package.json`, `packages/app-electron/tsconfig.json`, `packages/app-electron/scripts/build.mjs`, `packages/app-electron/src/channels.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/security.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/state-store.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/secret-store.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/claude.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/e2e.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/shell.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/main.ts`, `packages/app-electron/src/preload.cts`, `packages/app-electron/test/security.test.ts`, `packages/app-electron/test/stores.test.ts`, `packages/app-electron/test/helpers.test.ts`
- Modify: `.gitignore`, `docs/dependencies.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createRuntime`, `isSafeLink`, `Host`, `ShellHost`, `SecretStore`, `AppStateStore`, `AppState` (services); `Transport`, `ChangeEvent` (contract).
- Produces:
  - `CALL = "careeros:call"`, `CHANGE = "careeros:change"`
  - `APP_ORIGIN = "app://careeros"`, `CSP: string`, `appFileFor(rendererDir, url): string | null`, `fileUrlToRel(url): string | null`, `isTrustedSender(frameUrl, devUrl): boolean`, `contentType(file): string`, `devUrlFrom(env, packaged): string | null`
  - `fileStateStore(file): AppStateStore`; `interface SafeStorageLike`; `safeStorageSecrets(file, ss, platform): SecretStore`
  - `openClaudeDesktop(platform, run): Promise<boolean>`; `e2ePicks(env, packaged): string[] | null`; `e2eUserData(env, packaged): string | null`
  - `electronShell(getWindow, picks): ShellHost`
  - `window.careerosTransport: Transport` (preload)
  - `npm run build -w @careeros/app-electron` (renderer + main + preload + schemas into `packages/app-electron/dist`), `npm start -w @careeros/app-electron`, `npm run dev -w @careeros/app-electron` (with `npm run dev -w @careeros/app-ui` running)

New dependencies (exact pins; licences): `electron` 44.7.0 (MIT), `esbuild` 0.28.2 (MIT); both devDependencies of `@careeros/app-electron`.

Rulings:
- Only `https:` links leave the app, and only after a dialog that shows the full address (`ShellHost.openExternal`).
- `careeros-file:` checks every request with `resolveWorkspaceFile(..., "media")` (Task 31): images, video and PDF only, inside the workspace, never from a locked folder without a grant. Range headers are passed through so video can seek.
- The bundle is ESM; `core` finds its JSON schemas at `../schemas/` next to the bundle, so the build copies `packages/core/schemas` to `packages/app-electron/schemas` (gitignored).
- Opening Claude Desktop: macOS runs `open -a Claude`; other platforms return `false` and the Apply screen tells the person to open Claude and paste the prompt.
- `CAREEROS_E2E_PICK` (a JSON list of folder paths returned by the pickers) and `CAREEROS_USER_DATA` are honoured only when the app is not packaged.
- Installers, signing and auto-update stay out of 3a; the app runs from the repo with `npm start`.

`packages/app-electron/test/security.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { appFileFor, contentType, CSP, devUrlFrom, fileUrlToRel, isTrustedSender } from "../src/security.js";

const R = path.resolve("/fictional/app/renderer");
const src = (f: string) => fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../src", f), "utf8");

describe("CSP", () => {
  it("allows only our own code and the workspace media scheme", () => {
    for (const d of ["default-src 'none'", "script-src 'self'", "style-src 'self'", "font-src 'self'", "img-src 'self' careeros-file:", "media-src careeros-file:",
      "connect-src 'self'", "base-uri 'none'", "form-action 'none'", "frame-ancestors 'none'", "object-src 'none'"]) expect(CSP).toContain(d);
    expect(CSP).not.toMatch(/unsafe-|https:|http:|data:|\*/);
  });
});

describe("app:// files", () => {
  it("maps app URLs into the renderer folder", () => {
    expect(appFileFor(R, "app://careeros/index.html")).toBe(path.join(R, "index.html"));
    expect(appFileFor(R, "app://careeros/")).toBe(path.join(R, "index.html"));
    expect(appFileFor(R, "app://careeros/assets/geist-latin-400.woff2")).toBe(path.join(R, "assets", "geist-latin-400.woff2"));
  });
  it("refuses other hosts, schemes and escapes", () => {
    for (const u of ["app://other/index.html", "file:///etc/passwd", "app://careeros/../../etc/passwd", "app://careeros/%2e%2e/%2e%2e/etc/passwd", "app://careeros/a%5c..%5c..%5cx", "app://careeros/%00", "not a url"]) {
      const f = appFileFor(R, u);
      expect(f === null || f.startsWith(R + path.sep), u).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(appFileFor(R, "app://other/index.html")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("labels content types", () => {
    expect(contentType("a/index.html")).toBe("text/html; charset=utf-8");
    expect(contentType("a.woff2")).toBe("font/woff2");
    expect(contentType("a.unknown")).toBe("application/octet-stream");
  });
});

describe("careeros-file:// URLs", () => {
  it("turns URLs into workspace-relative paths", () => {
    expect(fileUrlToRel("careeros-file://ws/learning/rag/demo%20clip.mp4")).toBe("learning/rag/demo clip.mp4");
    expect(fileUrlToRel("careeros-file://other/x.png")).toBeNull();
    expect(fileUrlToRel("careeros-file://ws/")).toBeNull();
    expect(fileUrlToRel("careeros-file://ws/%E0%A4%A")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("never yields a dot-dot segment", () => {
    const rel = fileUrlToRel("careeros-file://ws/learning/%2e%2e/%2e%2e/settings/.env");
    expect(rel === null || !rel.split("/").includes("..")).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("IPC senders and dev mode", () => {
  it("trusts only the app origin, and the dev server only when given", () => {
    expect(isTrustedSender("app://careeros/index.html", null)).toBe(true);
    expect(isTrustedSender("https://evil.example.com/", null)).toBe(false);
    expect(isTrustedSender("app://careerosx/index.html", null)).toBe(false);
    expect(isTrustedSender(undefined, null)).toBe(false);
    expect(isTrustedSender("http://127.0.0.1:5173/", "http://127.0.0.1:5173")).toBe(true);
    expect(isTrustedSender("http://127.0.0.1:5173/", null)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("uses a dev URL only for an unpackaged app on the loopback address", () => {
    expect(devUrlFrom({ CAREEROS_DEV_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:5173" }, false)).toBe("http://127.0.0.1:5173");
    expect(devUrlFrom({ CAREEROS_DEV_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:5173" }, true)).toBeNull();
    expect(devUrlFrom({ CAREEROS_DEV_URL: "https://evil.example.com" }, false)).toBeNull();
    expect(devUrlFrom({}, false)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("main process hardening (source check)", () => {
  it("keeps the required window and app settings", () => {
    const main = src("main.ts");
    for (const s of ["contextIsolation: true", "sandbox: true", "nodeIntegration: false", "webSecurity: true", "app.enableSandbox()", "requestSingleInstanceLock()",
      "setPermissionRequestHandler", "setPermissionCheckHandler", "setWindowOpenHandler", "\"will-navigate\"", "\"will-attach-webview\""]) expect(main, s).toContain(s);
    expect(main).not.toMatch(/nodeIntegration: true|contextIsolation: false|sandbox: false|webSecurity: false|enableRemoteModule/);
  });
  it("keeps Electron out of the pure modules", () => {
    for (const f of ["security.ts", "state-store.ts", "secret-store.ts", "claude.ts", "e2e.ts", "channels.ts"]) expect(src(f), f).not.toMatch(/from "electron"/);
  });
});
```

`packages/app-electron/test/stores.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { safeStorageSecrets, type SafeStorageLike } from "../src/secret-store.js";
import { fileStateStore } from "../src/state-store.js";

const tmp = () => fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "careeros-shell-"));
function fakeSafeStorage(over: Partial<SafeStorageLike> = {}): SafeStorageLike {
  return {
    isEncryptionAvailable: () => true,
    encryptString: (s) => Buffer.from([...Buffer.from(s, "utf8")].map((b) => b ^ 0x5a)),
    decryptString: (b) => Buffer.from([...b].map((x) => x ^ 0x5a)).toString("utf8"),
    getSelectedStorageBackend: () => "gnome_libsecret",
    ...over,
  };
}

describe("fileStateStore", () => {
  it("round-trips and survives a missing or broken file", () => {
    const f = path.join(tmp(), "state.json");
    const s = fileStateStore(f);
    expect(s.read()).toEqual({ lastWorkspace: null, recent: [] });
    s.write({ lastWorkspace: "/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS", recent: ["/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS"] });
    expect(fileStateStore(f).read().lastWorkspace).toBe("/fictional/alex-example/CareerOS");
    if (process.platform !== "win32") expect(fs.statSync(f).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
    fs.writeFileSync(f, "{ broken");
    expect(fileStateStore(f).read()).toEqual({ lastWorkspace: null, recent: [] });
  });
});

describe("safeStorageSecrets", () => {
  it("stores only encrypted values", () => {
    const f = path.join(tmp(), "secrets.json");
    const s = safeStorageSecrets(f, fakeSafeStorage(), "darwin");
    const value = "abcd" + "efgh" + "ijkl" + "mnop";
    s.set("gmail_app_password", value);
    expect(s.has("gmail_app_password")).toBe(true);
    expect(s.get("gmail_app_password")).toBe(value);
    expect(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8")).not.toContain(value);
    if (process.platform !== "win32") expect(fs.statSync(f).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
    s.remove("gmail_app_password");
    expect(s.has("gmail_app_password")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("refuses Linux's plain-text fallback and machines without encryption", () => {
    const linux = safeStorageSecrets(path.join(tmp(), "s.json"), fakeSafeStorage({ getSelectedStorageBackend: () => "basic_text" }), "linux");
    expect(linux.available()).toBe(false);
    expect(() => linux.set("anthropic_api_key", "x")).toThrow(/Secure storage isn't available/);
    expect(safeStorageSecrets(path.join(tmp(), "t.json"), fakeSafeStorage({ isEncryptionAvailable: () => false }), "darwin").available()).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`packages/app-electron/src/security.ts`:

```ts
import path from "node:path";

export const APP_ORIGIN = "app://careeros";

export const CSP = [
  "default-src 'none'", "script-src 'self'", "style-src 'self'", "font-src 'self'", "img-src 'self' careeros-file:", "media-src careeros-file:",
  "connect-src 'self'", "base-uri 'none'", "form-action 'none'", "frame-ancestors 'none'", "object-src 'none'",
].join("; ");

/** app://careeros/<path> -> a file inside the renderer folder, or null. */
export function appFileFor(rendererDir: string, url: string): string | null {
  let u: URL;
  try { u = new URL(url); } catch { return null; }
  if (u.protocol !== "app:" || u.host !== "careeros") return null;
  let rel: string;
  try { rel = decodeURIComponent(u.pathname); } catch { return null; }
  if (rel === "" || rel === "/") rel = "/index.html";
  if (rel.includes("\u0000") || rel.includes("\\")) return null;
  const root = path.resolve(rendererDir);
  const abs = path.resolve(root, `.${rel}`);
  return abs.startsWith(root + path.sep) ? abs : null;
}

/** careeros-file://ws/<encoded segments> -> "a/b/c", or null. Every segment is checked again by the services. */
export function fileUrlToRel(url: string): string | null {
  let u: URL;
  try { u = new URL(url); } catch { return null; }
  if (u.protocol !== "careeros-file:" || u.host !== "ws") return null;
  const parts = u.pathname.split("/").slice(1);
  try {
    const decoded = parts.map((p) => decodeURIComponent(p));
    if (!decoded.length || decoded.some((p) => p === "" || p === "." || p === ".." || p.includes("\u0000"))) return null;
    return decoded.join("/");
  } catch { return null; }
}

export function isTrustedSender(frameUrl: string | undefined, devUrl: string | null): boolean {
  if (!frameUrl) return false;
  if (frameUrl.startsWith(`${APP_ORIGIN}/`)) return true;
  return !!devUrl && frameUrl.startsWith(devUrl.replace(/\/?$/, "/"));
}

const TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8", ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8", ".json": "application/json",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2", ".woff": "font/woff", ".svg": "image/svg+xml", ".png": "image/png", ".ico": "image/x-icon",
};
export const contentType = (file: string) => TYPES[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] ?? "application/octet-stream";

export function devUrlFrom(env: Record<string, string | undefined>, packaged: boolean): string | null {
  const u = env.CAREEROS_DEV_URL;
  return !packaged && u && /^http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:\d{2,5}\/?$/.test(u) ? u.replace(/\/$/, "") : null;
}
```

`packages/app-electron/src/state-store.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { AppState, AppStateStore } from "@careeros/services";

const EMPTY: AppState = { lastWorkspace: null, recent: [] };

/** App state lives in the user-data folder, never inside a workspace. */
export function fileStateStore(file: string): AppStateStore {
  return {
    read() {
      try {
        const v = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")) as Partial<AppState>;
        return {
          lastWorkspace: typeof v.lastWorkspace === "string" && path.isAbsolute(v.lastWorkspace) ? v.lastWorkspace : null,
          recent: Array.isArray(v.recent) ? v.recent.filter((r): r is string => typeof r === "string" && path.isAbsolute(r)).slice(0, 5) : [],
        };
      } catch { return { ...EMPTY, recent: [] }; }
    },
    write(s) {
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
      const tmp = `${file}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(s, null, 2), { mode: 0o600 });
      fs.renameSync(tmp, file);
    },
  };
}
```

`packages/app-electron/src/secret-store.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { SecretName } from "@careeros/contract";
import type { SecretStore } from "@careeros/services";

/** The parts of Electron's safeStorage we use; a fake implements it in tests. */
export interface SafeStorageLike {
  isEncryptionAvailable(): boolean;
  encryptString(s: string): Buffer;
  decryptString(b: Buffer): string;
  getSelectedStorageBackend?(): string;
}

export function safeStorageSecrets(file: string, ss: SafeStorageLike, platform: NodeJS.Platform): SecretStore {
  // On Linux without a secret service, safeStorage falls back to "basic_text" (a hard-coded key): treat that as unavailable.
  const available = () => ss.isEncryptionAvailable() && !(platform === "linux" && ss.getSelectedStorageBackend?.() === "basic_text");
  const read = (): Record<string, string> => {
    try { const v = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")); return v && typeof v === "object" ? v as Record<string, string> : {}; } catch { return {}; }
  };
  const write = (m: Record<string, string>) => {
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
    const tmp = `${file}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
    fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(m), { mode: 0o600 });
    fs.renameSync(tmp, file);
  };
  return {
    available,
    has: (n: SecretName) => Object.hasOwn(read(), n),
    get: (n: SecretName) => {
      const v = read()[n];
      if (!v || !available()) return null;
      try { return ss.decryptString(Buffer.from(v, "base64")); } catch { return null; }
    },
    set: (n: SecretName, value: string) => {
      if (!available()) throw new Error("Secure storage isn't available on this computer, so the secret was not saved.");
      write({ ...read(), [n]: ss.encryptString(value).toString("base64") });
    },
    remove: (n: SecretName) => { const m = read(); delete m[n]; write(m); },
  };
}
```

```ts
/** Opens the Claude desktop app if we know how on this platform. */
export async function openClaudeDesktop(platform: NodeJS.Platform, run: Run): Promise<boolean> {
```

```ts
import path from "node:path";

type Env = Record<string, string | undefined>;

/** Folder paths the pickers return in end-to-end tests. Ignored in a packaged app. */
export function e2ePicks(env: Env, packaged: boolean): string[] | null {
  if (packaged || !env.CAREEROS_E2E_PICK) return null;
  try {
    const v = JSON.parse(env.CAREEROS_E2E_PICK) as unknown;
    return Array.isArray(v) && v.every((p) => typeof p === "string" && path.isAbsolute(p)) ? (v as string[]) : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

export function e2eUserData(env: Env, packaged: boolean): string | null {
  const d = env.CAREEROS_USER_DATA;
  return !packaged && d && path.isAbsolute(d) ? d : null;
}
```

`packages/app-electron/src/shell.ts`:

```ts
import { execFile } from "node:child_process";
import { promisify } from "node:util";
import { dialog, shell, type BrowserWindow, type OpenDialogOptions } from "electron";
import { isSafeLink, type ShellHost } from "@careeros/services";
import { openClaudeDesktop } from "./claude.js";

const run = promisify(execFile);

export function electronShell(getWindow: () => BrowserWindow | null, picks: string[] | null): ShellHost {
  const ask = async (opts: OpenDialogOptions): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (picks) return picks.shift() ?? null;
    const win = getWindow();
    const r = win ? await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, opts) : await dialog.showOpenDialog(opts);
    return r.canceled ? null : r.filePaths[0] ?? null;
  };
  return {
    pickFolder: (purpose) => ask({
      title: purpose === "create" ? "Choose a folder for your workspace" : purpose === "open" ? "Open a CareerOS workspace" : "Choose your old career folder",
      properties: purpose === "create" ? ["openDirectory", "createDirectory", "promptToCreate"] : ["openDirectory"],
    }),
    pickFile: () => ask({ title: "Choose a file", properties: ["openFile"] }),
    openExternal: async (url) => {
      if (!isSafeLink(url)) return false;
      const win = getWindow();
      const opts = { type: "question" as const, buttons: ["Open", "Cancel"], defaultId: 0, cancelId: 1, message: "Open this link in your browser?", detail: url };
      const { response } = win ? await dialog.showMessageBox(win, opts) : await dialog.showMessageBox(opts);
      if (response !== 0) return false;
      await shell.openExternal(url);
      return true;
    },
    showItemInFolder: (p) => shell.showItemInFolder(p),
    openClaudeDesktop: () => openClaudeDesktop(process.platform, (cmd, args) => run(cmd, args)),
  };
}
```

`packages/app-electron/src/main.ts`:

```ts
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { app, BrowserWindow, ipcMain, net, powerMonitor, protocol, safeStorage, session } from "electron";
import { createRuntime, isSafeLink, type Runtime } from "@careeros/services";
import { CALL, CHANGE } from "./channels.js";
import { e2ePicks, e2eUserData } from "./e2e.js";
import { safeStorageSecrets } from "./secret-store.js";
import { APP_ORIGIN, appFileFor, contentType, CSP, devUrlFrom, fileUrlToRel, isTrustedSender } from "./security.js";
import { electronShell } from "./shell.js";
import { fileStateStore } from "./state-store.js";

const HERE = import.meta.dirname;
const RENDERER = path.join(HERE, "renderer");
const PRELOAD = path.join(HERE, "preload.cjs");
const devUrl = devUrlFrom(process.env, app.isPackaged);
const userData = e2eUserData(process.env, app.isPackaged);
if (userData) app.setPath("userData", userData);

protocol.registerSchemesAsPrivileged([
  { scheme: "app", privileges: { standard: true, secure: true, supportFetchAPI: true } },
  { scheme: "careeros-file", privileges: { standard: true, secure: true, stream: true } },
]);
app.enableSandbox();

let win: BrowserWindow | null = null;
let runtime: Runtime | null = null;

async function serveApp(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  const file = appFileFor(RENDERER, req.url);
  if (!file) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const res = await net.fetch(pathToFileURL(file).toString());
  if (!res.ok) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  return new Response(res.body, { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": contentType(file), "Content-Security-Policy": CSP, "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff" } });
}

async function serveFile(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  const rel = fileUrlToRel(req.url);
  if (!rel || !runtime) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  let abs: string;
  try { abs = runtime.resolveMedia(rel); } catch { return new Response("Not available", { status: 403 }); }
  const range = req.headers.get("Range");
  return net.fetch(pathToFileURL(abs).toString(), range ? { headers: { Range: range } } : undefined);
}

function createWindow(): void {
  win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1280, height: 820, minWidth: 960, minHeight: 640, show: false, title: "CareerOS",
    backgroundColor: "#0d0e11", // Graphite background (color.ink.900); avoids a white flash before the CSS loads.
    webPreferences: { preload: PRELOAD, contextIsolation: true, sandbox: true, nodeIntegration: false, webSecurity: true, spellcheck: false, devTools: !app.isPackaged },
  });
  win.once("ready-to-show", () => win?.show());
  win.on("closed", () => { win = null; });
  void win.loadURL(devUrl ?? `${APP_ORIGIN}/index.html`);
}

// Every web contents, now or later: no navigation, no new windows (https links go to the browser after a prompt), no webviews.
app.on("web-contents-created", (_e, contents) => {
  contents.on("will-navigate", (e) => e.preventDefault());
  contents.on("will-redirect", (e) => e.preventDefault());
  contents.on("will-attach-webview", (e) => e.preventDefault());
  contents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (isSafeLink(url)) void runtime?.ctx.host.shell.openExternal(url);
    return { action: "deny" };
  });
});

if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  app.on("second-instance", () => {
    if (!win) return;
    if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore();
    win.focus();
  });

  void app.whenReady().then(() => {
    session.defaultSession.setPermissionRequestHandler((_wc, _permission, cb) => cb(false));
    session.defaultSession.setPermissionCheckHandler(() => false);
    protocol.handle("app", serveApp);
    protocol.handle("careeros-file", serveFile);

    const data = app.getPath("userData");
    runtime = createRuntime({
      secrets: safeStorageSecrets(path.join(data, "secrets.json"), safeStorage, process.platform),
      state: fileStateStore(path.join(data, "state.json")),
      shell: electronShell(() => win, e2ePicks(process.env, app.isPackaged)),
      info: { version: app.getVersion(), shell: "electron", platform: process.platform },
    });
    runtime.onChange((e) => win?.webContents.send(CHANGE, e));

    ipcMain.handle(CALL, (event, method: unknown, args: unknown) => {
      if (!win || event.sender !== win.webContents || !isTrustedSender(event.senderFrame?.url, devUrl)) throw new Error("This request was blocked.");
      return runtime!.dispatch(String(method), args);
    });

    // A laptop that slept past a grant's end re-locks as soon as it wakes.
    powerMonitor.on("resume", () => { runtime?.checkGrants(); });
    powerMonitor.on("unlock-screen", () => { runtime?.checkGrants(); });

    createWindow();
  });

  app.on("before-quit", () => { runtime?.shutdown(); });
  app.on("window-all-closed", () => { app.quit(); });
}
```

`packages/app-electron/src/preload.cts`:

```ts
// Runs in the sandboxed preload. Exposes exactly one object; no Node or Electron APIs reach the page.
import { contextBridge, ipcRenderer, webUtils, type IpcRendererEvent } from "electron";
import type { ChangeEvent, Transport } from "@careeros/contract";
import { CALL, CHANGE } from "./channels.js";

const transport: Transport = {
  call: (method, args) => ipcRenderer.invoke(CALL, method, args),
  onChange: (cb) => {
    const h = (_e: IpcRendererEvent, ev: ChangeEvent) => cb(ev);
    ipcRenderer.on(CHANGE, h);
    return () => { ipcRenderer.removeListener(CHANGE, h); };
  },
  pathForFile: (file) => {
    try { return webUtils.getPathForFile(file as File) || null; } catch { return null; }
  },
  fileUrl: (rel) => `careeros-file://ws/${rel.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/")}`,
};

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld("careerosTransport", transport);
```

`packages/app-electron/scripts/build.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Builds the desktop app into packages/app-electron/dist: renderer (Vite), main (ESM) and preload (CJS), plus core's schemas.
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { build } from "esbuild";

const pkg = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const repo = path.resolve(pkg, "../..");
const dist = path.join(pkg, "dist");
const withRenderer = !process.argv.includes("--no-renderer");

fs.rmSync(dist, { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.mkdirSync(dist, { recursive: true });

if (withRenderer) {
  execFileSync(process.platform === "win32" ? "npm.cmd" : "npm", ["run", "build", "-w", "@careeros/app-ui"], { cwd: repo, stdio: "inherit" });
  fs.cpSync(path.join(repo, "packages/app-ui/dist"), path.join(dist, "renderer"), { recursive: true });
}

const common = { bundle: true, platform: "node", target: "node24", external: ["electron"], sourcemap: "linked", logLevel: "info" };
await build({
  ...common, entryPoints: [path.join(pkg, "src/main.ts")], outfile: path.join(dist, "main.js"), format: "esm",
  // Bundled CommonJS dependencies may call require() for Node built-ins; give the ESM bundle a real require.
  banner: { js: "import { createRequire as __careerosRequire } from 'node:module'; const require = __careerosRequire(import.meta.url);" },
});
await build({ ...common, entryPoints: [path.join(pkg, "src/preload.cts")], outfile: path.join(dist, "preload.cjs"), format: "cjs" });

// core loads its JSON schemas from ../schemas relative to the running module.
fs.rmSync(path.join(pkg, "schemas"), { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.cpSync(path.join(repo, "packages/core/schemas"), path.join(pkg, "schemas"), { recursive: true });
```

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { _electron as electron, expect, test, type ElectronApplication, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

const PKG = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const SAMPLE = path.resolve(PKG, "../../samples/alex-example");
// 1x1 transparent PNG, for checking that workspace images load.
const PNG = Buffer.from("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mNkYAAAAAYAAjCB0C8AAAAASUVORK5CYII=", "base64");

let app: ElectronApplication;
let page: Page;
let ws: string;

test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" });

test.beforeAll(async () => {
  const tmp = fs.realpathSync.native(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "careeros-e2e-")));
  ws = path.join(tmp, "CareerOS");
  fs.cpSync(SAMPLE, ws, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ws, "learning", "rag-pipelines", "chart.png"), PNG);
  app = await electron.launch({
    args: [PKG],
    env: { ...process.env, CAREEROS_USER_DATA: path.join(tmp, "user-data"), CAREEROS_E2E_PICK: JSON.stringify([ws]) },
  });
  page = await app.firstWindow();
});

test.afterAll(async () => { await app?.close(); });

const loadImage = (src: string) => page.evaluate((s) => new Promise<string>((resolve) => {
  const img = new Image();
  img.onload = () => resolve("load");
  img.onerror = () => resolve("error");
  img.src = s;
}), src);

test("onboarding opens an existing workspace", async () => {
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Open a workspace/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText("Alex.");
});

test("filters jobs and opens one", async () => {
  await page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" }).getByRole("button", { name: /Jobs/ }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Search jobs").fill("labs");
  const rows = page.getByRole("table").getByRole("row");
  await expect(rows).toHaveCount(2);
  await page.getByText("Backend Engineer (LLM)").click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Backend Engineer (LLM)", level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
});

test("files an inbox item that arrived from outside the app", async () => {
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ws, "inbox", "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf"), "fictional");
  await page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" }).getByRole("button", { name: /Inbox/ }).click();
  const item = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf" });
  await expect(item).toBeVisible();
  await item.getByRole("button", { name: "File it" }).click();
  await expect(item).toHaveCount(0);
  expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(ws, "me", "identity", "visa", "Visa_Grant_Notice.pdf"))).toBe(true);
});

test("unlocking a folder rewrites Claude's settings, and locking restores them", async () => {
  const deny = () => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ws, ".claude", "settings.json"), "utf8")).permissions.deny as string[];
  await page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" }).getByRole("button", { name: /Privacy/ }).click();
  const sw = page.getByRole("switch", { name: "Lock me/employment" });
  await sw.click();
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", { name: "Unlock" }).click();
  await expect(sw).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "false");
  expect(deny()).not.toContain("Read(./me/employment/**)");
  await sw.click();
  await expect(sw).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true");
  expect(deny()).toContain("Read(./me/employment/**)");
});

test("the page has no Node, no eval and no way out", async () => {
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => [typeof (globalThis as Record<string, unknown>).require, typeof (globalThis as Record<string, unknown>).process, typeof (globalThis as Record<string, unknown>).module])).toEqual(["undefined", "undefined", "undefined"]);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => { try { return String((0, eval)("1")); } catch { return "blocked"; } })).toBe("blocked");
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.open("https://example.com/") === null)).toBe(true);
  await page.evaluate(() => { window.location.href = "https://example.com/"; });
  await page.waitForTimeout(500);
  expect(page.url().startsWith("app://careeros/")).toBe(true);
  expect(app.windows()).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("the bridge refuses bad arguments", async () => {
  const msg = await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { careerosTransport: { call(m: string, a: unknown[]): Promise<unknown> } })
    .careerosTransport.call("files.readText", ["../workspace.json"]).then(() => "allowed", (e: Error) => e.message));
  expect(msg).toMatch(/must not contain empty, \. or \.\. segments/);
});

test("careeros-file serves workspace images but never secrets, locked folders or remote images", async () => {
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ws, "me", "identity", "passport.png"), PNG);
  expect(await loadImage("careeros-file://ws/learning/rag-pipelines/chart.png")).toBe("load");
  expect(await loadImage("careeros-file://ws/settings/.env")).toBe("error");
  expect(await loadImage("careeros-file://ws/me/identity/passport.png")).toBe("error");
  expect(await loadImage("careeros-file://ws/learning/%2e%2e/me/identity/passport.png")).toBe("error");
  expect(await loadImage("https://example.com/x.png")).toBe("error");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Run it**

Run: `npm run e2e -w @careeros/app-electron` (on Linux: `xvfb-run -a npm run e2e -w @careeros/app-electron`)
Expected: 7 passed.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the end-to-end step to CI**

In `.github/workflows/checks.yml`, add after the brand visual step:

```yaml
      - name: Desktop app end-to-end (Electron)
        run: |
          if [ "$RUNNER_OS" = "Linux" ]; then
            # Ubuntu 24.04 blocks the unprivileged user namespaces Chromium's sandbox needs; allow them rather than turning the sandbox off.
            sudo sysctl -w kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns=0
            xvfb-run -a npm run e2e -w @careeros/app-electron
          else
            npm run e2e -w @careeros/app-electron
          fi
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Run everything once more and commit**

```bash
npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run scan
git add packages/app-electron .github/workflows/checks.yml
git commit -m "test(app-electron): end-to-end flows and security checks in real Electron; CI on Linux and macOS"
```

## Self-review

- **Spec coverage.** Core changes (§8): Tasks 1-8. Brand system (owner brief): tokens and build (9), fonts, typography roles and lint (10), primitives (11), composites (12), assets, guide and visual snapshots (13). Typed bridge and seam (§3): contract (14), MockBridge (15), renderer boundary test (16), preload transport (34). Screens (§4): Onboarding 17, Home 18, Jobs 19, Job 20, Apply 21, Inbox 22, Learn 23, Sponsors 24, Searches 25, Settings 26, Privacy 27, Approvals 28. Services (§5-6): 29-33. Electron (§2, §6): 34-35. Mail (3b), in-app chat and voice (3c) and packaging (4) stay out, per Global Constraints.
- **Placeholder scan.** Every step names exact files, full code, the command to run and the expected result. No step says "similar to", "TBD" or "handle errors appropriately".
- **Type consistency.** The renderer and both bridge implementations use only `@careeros/contract` types; `HandlerTable = Record<MethodName, Handler>` makes the services fail to typecheck if a method lacks a handler, and the dispatch test checks the same at runtime; the bridge test checks that `createRemoteBridge` calls exactly the `METHODS` list. `ShellHost.openExternal` returns `Promise<boolean>` everywhere (fake, Electron shell, `links.open`).
- **Review Focus pins.** 1: Task 31 (services tests) and Task 35 (careeros-file in Electron). 2: Task 2, Task 29 (re-lock on open and close), Task 32 (timer, resume check), Task 34 (`powerMonitor`). 3: Task 8, Task 28, Task 32. 4: Task 4, Task 20, Task 30. 5: Task 30 (and the Settings screen test in Task 26).
